#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -e
GREEN='\033[0;32m'; NC='\033[0m'
say() { echo -e "${GREEN}▶ $1${NC}"; }

# ==========================================================
# 1. Réécriture complète de src/app/seal/page.tsx
# ==========================================================
say "1/2 → src/app/seal/page.tsx (onglet Vérifier + cases + statut + await)"

cat > src/app/seal/page.tsx << 'EOF'
'use client';
import { useState, useRef, Suspense } from 'react';
import { sha256 } from '@noble/hashes/sha256';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { useSearchParams } from 'next/navigation';

function SealContent() {
  const searchParams = useSearchParams();
  const isPactMode = searchParams.get('pact') === 'true';
  const chatId = searchParams.get('chat_id');
  const chatType = searchParams.get('chat_type');
  const challengeIdParam = searchParams.get('challenge_id');

  const [isFlashMode, setIsFlashMode] = useState(false);
  const [mode, setMode] = useState<'file' | 'text' | 'media' | 'audio'>('file');

  const [title, setTitle] = useState('');
  const [desc, setDesc] = useState('');
  const [textContent, setTextContent] = useState('');
  const [file, setFile] = useState<File | null>(null);
  const [pactFiles, setPactFiles] = useState<File[]>([]);

  const [visibility, setVisibility] = useState('public');
  const [isHosted, setIsHosted] = useState(true);
  const [isMystery, setIsMystery] = useState(false);
  const [isEphemeral, setIsEphemeral] = useState(false);
  const [unlockLikes, setUnlockLikes] = useState(10);
  const [requiredSignatures, setRequiredSignatures] = useState(2);

  const [status, setStatus] = useState('');
  const [sealedResult, setSealedResult] = useState<any>(null);

  const [isRecording, setIsRecording] = useState(false);
  const [audioUrl, setAudioUrl] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const mediaRecorderRef = useRef<MediaRecorder | null>(null);
  const audioChunksRef = useRef<Blob[]>([]);

  async function startRecording() {
    try {
      const stream = await navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({ audio: true });
      const mediaRecorder = new MediaRecorder(stream);
      mediaRecorderRef.current = mediaRecorder;
      audioChunksRef.current = [];
      mediaRecorder.ondataavailable = (event) => { if (event.data.size > 0) audioChunksRef.current.push(event.data); };
      mediaRecorder.onstop = () => {
        const audioBlob = new Blob(audioChunksRef.current, { type: 'audio/webm' });
        setFile(new File([audioBlob], `audio-${Date.now()}.webm`, { type: 'audio/webm' }));
        setAudioUrl(URL.createObjectURL(audioBlob));
      };
      mediaRecorder.start();
      setIsRecording(true);
    } catch { setStatus("❌ Impossible d'accéder au micro."); }
  }

  function stopRecording() {
    if (mediaRecorderRef.current && isRecording) {
      mediaRecorderRef.current.stop();
      mediaRecorderRef.current.stream.getTracks().forEach(track => track.stop());
      setIsRecording(false);
    }
  }

  async function handleSeal() {
    setStatus('');

    if (!title.trim()) return setStatus("❌ Un titre est requis");
    if (isPactMode && pactFiles.length === 0) return setStatus("❌ Ajoute au moins un fichier au pacte");
    if (!isPactMode && mode === 'text' && !textContent.trim()) return setStatus("❌ Écris ton texte");
    if (!isPactMode && mode === 'file' && !file) return setStatus("❌ Choisis un fichier à sceller");
    if (!isPactMode && mode === 'media' && !file) return setStatus("❌ Prends une photo ou une vidéo");
    if (!isPactMode && mode === 'audio' && !file) return setStatus("❌ Enregistre un audio");

    setStatus(isPactMode ? "Préparation du pacte..." : "Calcul de l'empreinte...");
    try {
      let hashContent: Uint8Array;

      if (isPactMode) {
        const metaString = `${title}-${desc}-${pactFiles.map(f => f.name).join(',')}`;
        hashContent = sha256(new TextEncoder().encode(metaString));
      } else if (mode === 'text') {
        hashContent = sha256(new TextEncoder().encode(textContent));
      } else {
        if (!file) return setStatus("❌ Fichier manquant");
        hashContent = sha256(new Uint8Array(await file.arrayBuffer()));
      }

      let hashStr = '';
      for (let i = 0; i < hashContent.length; i++) hashStr += String.fromCharCode(hashContent[i]);
      await sendToServer(btoa(hashStr));
    } catch (e: any) {
      console.error(e);
      setStatus("❌ Erreur de gravure : " + (e?.message || 'inconnue'));
    }
  }

  async function sendToServer(hashB64: string) {
    const pubkey = localStorage.getItem('sceau_pubkey');
    if (!pubkey) return setStatus("❌ Connecte-toi d'abord !");

    const formData = new FormData();
    formData.append('hash', hashB64);
    formData.append('title', title);
    formData.append('description', mode === 'text' ? textContent : desc);
    formData.append('pubkey', pubkey);

    let endpoint = '/api/seal';

    if (isPactMode) {
      endpoint = '/api/pacts/create';
      formData.append('group_id', chatType === 'group' ? (chatId as string) : '');
      formData.append('required_signatures', requiredSignatures.toString());
      pactFiles.forEach(f => formData.append('files', f));
    } else {
      formData.append('isFlash', isFlashMode.toString());
      formData.append('visibility', visibility);
      formData.append('isMystery', isMystery.toString());
      formData.append('isEphemeral', isEphemeral.toString());
      formData.append('unlockLikes', unlockLikes.toString());
      formData.append('isHosted', isHosted.toString());
      if (challengeIdParam) formData.append('challenge_id', challengeIdParam);
      if (mode !== 'text' && file) formData.append('file', file);
    }

    setStatus(isPactMode ? "Envoi du pacte..." : "Ancrage dans la blockchain (max 8s)…");

    try {
      const res = await fetch(endpoint, { method: 'POST', body: formData });
      const data = await res.json().catch(() => ({} as any));

      if (!res.ok) {
        return setStatus("❌ " + (data?.error || `Erreur serveur (${res.status})`));
      }

      if (isPactMode) {
        const msg = `[PACTE]${data.id}|${data.title}`;
        if (chatType === 'dm') {
          await fetch('/api/messages', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ sender_handle: localStorage.getItem('sceau_handle'), receiver_handle: chatId, content: msg }) });
        } else {
          await fetch('/api/groups/messages', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ group_id: chatId, sender_handle: localStorage.getItem('sceau_handle'), content: msg }) });
        }
        window.location.href = '/messages';
      } else {
        setStatus("✅ Scellé !");
        setSealedResult({ title, hash: hashB64, date: new Date().toLocaleDateString('fr-FR'), isFlash: isFlashMode, ots: !!data.ots_secured });
      }
    } catch (e: any) {
      console.error(e);
      setStatus("❌ Erreur réseau : " + (e?.message || 'impossible de contacter le serveur'));
    }
  }

  if (sealedResult) {
    return (
      <div className="p-6 font-sans text-center flex flex-col items-center max-w-md mx-auto">
        <div className="text-6xl mb-4">{sealedResult.isFlash ? '⚡' : '✅'}</div>
        <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold mb-2" style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>C'est gravé.</h1>
        <div className="bg-[var(--bg-surface)] p-6 rounded-3xl border border-[var(--border-color)] w-full text-left shadow-lg mt-6">
          <h3 className="font-bold mb-2" style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>{sealedResult.title}</h3>
          <p className="text-[0.6rem] font-mono break-all text-[var(--text-muted)] bg-[var(--bg-main)] p-2 rounded border border-[var(--border-color)]">{sealedResult.hash}</p>
          <p className="text-xs text-right mt-3 text-[var(--text-muted)]">Scellé le {sealedResult.date}</p>
          {sealedResult.ots && <p className="text-xs mt-2 text-[#5D7A3A] font-bold">✅ Preuve horodatée</p>}
        </div>
        <button onClick={() => window.location.reload()} className="mt-8 bg-[#2C1810] text-white p-3 rounded-xl font-bold w-full">Sceller une autre œuvre</button>
        <Link href="/feed" className="mt-3 border-2 border-[var(--border-color)] text-[var(--text-main)] p-3 rounded-xl font-bold w-full text-center">Voir le Feed</Link>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="p-6 font-sans max-w-md mx-auto">

      {/* === ONGLETS SCeller / Vérifier === */}
      {!isPactMode && (
        <div className="flex justify-center mb-6">
          <div className="flex bg-[var(--bg-main)] p-1 rounded-xl border border-[var(--border-color)]">
            <Link href="/seal" className="px-6 py-2 rounded-lg font-bold text-sm bg-[var(--bg-surface)] shadow text-[#E76F51]">✍️ Sceller</Link>
            <Link href="/verify" className="px-6 py-2 rounded-lg font-bold text-sm opacity-50 hover:opacity-100 transition" style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>🔍 Vérifier</Link>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      <div className="text-center mb-6">
        <h1 className="text-4xl font-bold mb-2" style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>
          {isPactMode ? "Nouveau Pacte." : (isFlashMode ? "Flash." : "L'Atelier.")}
        </h1>
      </div>

      {/* === Toggle Normal / Flash === */}
      {!isPactMode && (
        <div className="flex bg-[var(--bg-main)] p-1 rounded-xl mb-4 border border-[var(--border-color)]">
          <button type="button" onClick={() => { setIsFlashMode(false); setMode('file'); }} className={`flex-1 py-2 rounded-lg font-bold text-sm transition ${!isFlashMode ? 'bg-[var(--bg-surface)] shadow text-[#E76F51]' : 'opacity-50'}`}>📁 Normal</button>
          <button type="button" onClick={() => { setIsFlashMode(true); setMode('text'); }} className={`flex-1 py-2 rounded-lg font-bold text-sm transition ${isFlashMode ? 'bg-[#2C1810] text-white shadow' : 'opacity-50'}`}>⚡ Flash</button>
        </div>
      )}

      <div className="space-y-4 bg-[var(--bg-surface)] p-6 rounded-2xl border border-[var(--border-color)] shadow-sm">

        {/* === Sélecteur de mode === */}
        {!isPactMode && (
          <div className="flex bg-[var(--bg-main)] p-1 rounded-xl text-xs font-mono">
            {!isFlashMode && <button type="button" onClick={() => setMode('file')} className={`flex-1 py-2 rounded-lg font-bold transition ${mode === 'file' ? 'bg-[var(--bg-surface)] shadow' : 'opacity-50'}`} style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>📁 Fichier</button>}
            <button type="button" onClick={() => setMode('text')} className={`flex-1 py-2 rounded-lg font-bold transition ${mode === 'text' ? 'bg-[var(--bg-surface)] shadow' : 'opacity-50'}`} style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>✍️ Texte</button>
            {!isFlashMode && <button type="button" onClick={() => setMode('media')} className={`flex-1 py-2 rounded-lg font-bold transition ${mode === 'media' ? 'bg-[var(--bg-surface)] shadow' : 'opacity-50'}`} style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>📸 Photo</button>}
            {!isFlashMode && <button type="button" onClick={() => setMode('audio')} className={`flex-1 py-2 rounded-lg font-bold transition ${mode === 'audio' ? 'bg-[var(--bg-surface)] shadow' : 'opacity-50'}`} style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>🎙️ Audio</button>}
          </div>
        )}

        {/* === Titre === */}
        <div>
          <label className="text-xs font-mono font-bold uppercase" style={{ color: 'var(--text-muted)' }}>Titre</label>
          <input
            type="text"
            className="w-full border-2 border-[var(--border-color)] focus:border-[#E76F51] rounded-xl p-3 outline-none bg-transparent mt-1"
            style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}
            value={title}
            onChange={e => setTitle(e.target.value)}
            placeholder={isPactMode ? "Nom du pacte" : (isFlashMode ? "Ton idée en un mot" : "Titre de l'œuvre")}
          />
        </div>

        {/* === Pacte : fichiers === */}
        {isPactMode && (
          <div className="bg-[#FDF8F0] p-4 rounded-xl border border-[#EFE0CA]">
            <label className="text-xs font-mono font-bold block mb-2" style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>📁 Médias (multi-fichiers)</label>
            <input type="file" multiple className="w-full text-sm" onChange={e => setPactFiles(Array.from(e.target.files || []))} />
            {pactFiles.length > 0 && <p className="text-xs mt-1 text-[var(--text-muted)]">{pactFiles.length} fichier(s) sélectionné(s)</p>}
          </div>
        )}

        {/* === Description (sauf text) === */}
        {!isPactMode && mode !== 'text' && (
          <div>
            <label className="text-xs font-mono font-bold uppercase" style={{ color: 'var(--text-muted)' }}>Description</label>
            <textarea className="w-full border-2 border-[var(--border-color)] focus:border-[#E76F51] rounded-xl p-3 outline-none bg-transparent mt-1" rows={2} value={desc} onChange={e => setDesc(e.target.value)} />
          </div>
        )}

        {/* === Texte === */}
        {mode === 'text' && !isPactMode && (
          <div>
            <label className="text-xs font-mono font-bold uppercase" style={{ color: 'var(--text-muted)' }}>Texte à sceller</label>
            <textarea className="w-full border-2 border-[var(--border-color)] focus:border-[#E76F51] rounded-xl p-4 outline-none bg-transparent min-h-[100px] font-mono text-sm mt-1" value={textContent} onChange={e => setTextContent(e.target.value)} placeholder="Écris ton texte ici..." />
          </div>
        )}

        {/* === Fichier === */}
        {!isPactMode && mode === 'file' && (
          <div>
            <label className="text-xs font-mono font-bold uppercase" style={{ color: 'var(--text-muted)' }}>Fichier</label>
            <input type="file" className="w-full text-sm mt-1" onChange={e => setFile(e.target.files?.[0] || null)} />
            {file && <p className="text-xs mt-1 text-[var(--text-muted)]">📎 {file.name} ({(file.size / 1024).toFixed(0)} Ko)</p>}
          </div>
        )}

        {/* === Photo / vidéo === */}
        {!isPactMode && mode === 'media' && (
          <div>
            <label className="text-xs font-mono font-bold uppercase" style={{ color: 'var(--text-muted)' }}>Photo / Vidéo</label>
            <input type="file" accept="image/*,video/*" capture="environment" className="w-full text-sm mt-1" onChange={e => setFile(e.target.files?.[0] || null)} />
            {file && <p className="text-xs mt-1 text-[var(--text-muted)]">📎 {file.name}</p>}
          </div>
        )}

        {/* === Audio === */}
        {!isPactMode && mode === 'audio' && (
          <div>
            <label className="text-xs font-mono font-bold uppercase" style={{ color: 'var(--text-muted)' }}>Audio</label>
            <div className="mt-1">
              {!isRecording && !audioUrl && <button type="button" onClick={startRecording} className="w-full bg-red-500 text-white py-2 rounded-lg font-bold text-xs">🔴 Enregistrer</button>}
              {isRecording && <button type="button" onClick={stopRecording} className="w-full bg-[#2C1810] text-white py-2 rounded-lg font-bold text-xs animate-pulse">⏹ Arrêter</button>}
              {audioUrl && !isRecording && <audio controls src={audioUrl} className="w-full mt-2 h-10" />}
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* === Options (non-flash, non-pacte) === */}
        {!isFlashMode && !isPactMode && (
          <div className="bg-[var(--bg-main)] p-4 rounded-xl border border-[var(--border-color)] space-y-3 mt-4">
            <div>
              <label className="text-[0.65rem] font-mono font-bold uppercase block mb-2" style={{ color: 'var(--text-muted)' }}>Visibilité</label>
              <div className="flex bg-[var(--bg-surface)] rounded-xl border border-[var(--border-color)] p-1">
                <button type="button" onClick={() => setVisibility('public')} className={`flex-1 py-2 text-xs font-bold rounded transition ${visibility === 'public' ? 'bg-[#F5F9EE] text-[#5D7A3A]' : 'opacity-50'}`}>🌍 Public</button>
                <button type="button" onClick={() => setVisibility('friends')} className={`flex-1 py-2 text-xs font-bold rounded transition ${visibility === 'friends' ? 'bg-[#F0F6FC] text-[#2D6A9E]' : 'opacity-50'}`}>👥 Amis</button>
                <button type="button" onClick={() => setVisibility('private')} className={`flex-1 py-2 text-xs font-bold rounded transition ${visibility === 'private' ? 'bg-[#FDF4F0] text-[#C44A2E]' : 'opacity-50'}`}>🔒 Privé</button>
              </div>
            </div>

            <label className="flex items-center gap-3 cursor-pointer">
              <input type="checkbox" checked={isHosted} onChange={e => setIsHosted(e.target.checked)} className="w-4 h-4 accent-[#E76F51]" />
              <span className="text-sm font-bold" style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>☁️ Héberger le fichier</span>
            </label>

            <label className="flex items-center gap-3 cursor-pointer">
              <input type="checkbox" checked={isMystery} onChange={e => setIsMystery(e.target.checked)} className="w-4 h-4 accent-[#E76F51]" />
              <span className="text-sm font-bold" style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>🎭 Sceau Mystère</span>
            </label>

            {isMystery && (
              <div className="pl-7">
                <label className="text-[0.6rem] font-mono text-[var(--text-muted)] block">Likes requis pour révéler</label>
                <input type="number" min="1" value={unlockLikes} onChange={e => setUnlockLikes(parseInt(e.target.value) || 1)} className="w-full border border-[var(--border-color)] p-2 rounded text-sm bg-[var(--bg-surface)] mt-1" style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }} />
              </div>
            )}

            <label className="flex items-center gap-3 cursor-pointer">
              <input type="checkbox" checked={isEphemeral} onChange={e => setIsEphemeral(e.target.checked)} className="w-4 h-4 accent-[#E76F51]" />
              <span className="text-sm font-bold" style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>⏳ Éphémère (24h)</span>
            </label>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* === Pacte : signatures === */}
        {isPactMode && (
          <div>
            <label className="text-xs font-mono font-bold uppercase" style={{ color: 'var(--text-muted)' }}>Signatures requises</label>
            <input type="number" min="2" value={requiredSignatures} onChange={e => setRequiredSignatures(parseInt(e.target.value) || 2)} className="w-full border-2 border-[var(--border-color)] p-2 rounded-lg text-center font-bold text-lg bg-transparent mt-1" style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }} />
          </div>
        )}

        {/* === Bouton SCELLER === */}
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={handleSeal}
          className={`w-full p-4 rounded-xl font-bold text-white shadow-lg mt-2 ${isPactMode ? 'bg-[#4A90C2]' : (isFlashMode ? 'bg-[#2C1810]' : 'bg-gradient-to-r from-[#E76F51] to-[#F4A261]')}`}
        >
          {isPactMode ? 'CRÉER LE PACTE' : (isFlashMode ? 'GRAVER EN 1 CLIC' : 'SCELLER MON ŒUVRE')}
        </button>

        {/* === ✅ Affichage du statut — CE QUI MANQUAIT === */}
        {status && (
          <p className={`text-center font-bold text-sm p-3 rounded-lg border ${status.includes('✅') ? 'bg-[#F5F9EE] text-[#5D7A3A] border-[#A8C77E]' : status.includes('❌') ? 'bg-[#FDF4F0] text-[#C44A2E] border-[#FCE0D6]' : 'bg-[#F0F6FC] text-[#2D6A9E] border-[#C9D8E8]'}`}>
            {status}
          </p>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export default function Seal() {
  return <Suspense fallback={<div className="p-10 text-center font-mono">Chargement...</div>}><SealContent /></Suspense>;
}
EOF

FICHIER : ./fix-verify-merkle.sh

#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -e
GREEN='\0;32m'; NC='\033[0m'
say() { echo -e "${GREEN}▶ $1${NC}"; }

# ==========================================================
# 1. Correction du typage TS dans merkle.ts
# ==========================================================
say "1/3 → Correction de merkle.ts (Typage TS)"

cat > src/lib/merkle.ts << 'EOF'
import { sha256 } from '@noble/hashes/sha256';

/**
 * Prend une liste de hashs (en Base64) et calcule le Hash Racine (Merkle Root).
 * Utilise Uint8Array pour éviter les conflits de types avec Buffer.
 */
export function buildMerkleRoot(hashesB64: string[]): string {
  if (hashesB64.length === 0) return '';
  if (hashesB64.length === 1) return hashesB64[0];

  // Convertir les hashs Base64 en Uint8Array
  let currentLevel: Uint8Array[] = hashesB64.map(h => {
    const binaryString = atob(h);
    const bytes = new Uint8Array(binaryString.length);
    for (let i = 0; i < binaryString.length; i++) {
      bytes[i] = binaryString.charCodeAt(i);
    }
    return bytes;
  });

  while (currentLevel.length > 1) {
    const nextLevel: Uint8Array[] = [];
    
    for (let i = 0; i < currentLevel.length; i += 2) {
      if (i + 1 < currentLevel.length) {
        // Concaténer les deux hashs
        const combined = new Uint8Array(currentLevel[i].length + currentLevel[i + 1].length);
        combined.set(currentLevel[i], 0);
        combined.set(currentLevel[i + 1], currentLevel[i].length);
        
        // Calculer le hash du résultat
        const hash = sha256(combined);
        nextLevel.push(hash);
      } else {
        // S'il reste un élément impair, on le passe au niveau supérieur
        nextLevel.push(currentLevel[i]);
      }
    }
    currentLevel = nextLevel;
  }

  // Convertir le Uint8Array final en Base64
  const root = currentLevel[0];
  let binary = '';
  const len = root.byteLength;
  for (let i = 0; i < len; i++) {
    binary += String.fromCharCode(root[i]);
  }
  
  return btoa(binary);
}
EOF

# ==========================================================
# 2. API Verify : Ajout de la recherche dans les Projets
# ==========================================================
say "2/3 → Mise à jour de /api/verify (Recherche inversée)"

cat > "src/app/api/verify/route.ts" << 'EOF'
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  try {
    const { hash, query } = await req.json();
    
    // 1. Recherche par Hash (Vérification classique + Projets)
    if (hash) {
      // a) On cherche dans les sceaux individuels
      const sealResult = await pool.query(`
        SELECT s.title, s.timestamp, s.is_hosted, s.file_path, s.category, u.handle as author,
               'seal' as type
        FROM seals s JOIN users u ON s.author_id = u.id
        WHERE encode(s.hash, 'base64') = $1
      `, [hash]);

      // b) On cherche dans les fichiers de projets (Arbre de Merkle)
      const projectResult = await pool.query(`
        SELECT pf.file_name as title, p.created_at as timestamp, 
               CASE WHEN pf.file_path IS NOT NULL THEN true ELSE false END as is_hosted, 
               pf.file_path, 'Projet' as category, u.handle as author,
               'project' as type, p.title as project_title, p.id as project_id
        FROM project_files pf 
        JOIN projects p ON pf.project_id = p.id
        JOIN users u ON p.author_id = u.id
        WHERE pf.file_hash = $1
      `, [hash]);

      const combinedSeals = [...sealResult.rows, ...projectResult.rows];

      if (combinedSeals.length > 0) {
        return NextResponse.json({ found: true, seals: combinedSeals });
      }
      return NextResponse.json({ found: false });
    }

    // 2. Recherche par Titre (Découverte publique)
    if (query) {
      const result = await pool.query(`
        SELECT s.title, s.timestamp, s.is_hosted, s.file_path, s.category, encode(s.hash, 'base64') as hash, u.handle as author
        FROM seals s JOIN users u ON s.author_id = u.id
        WHERE s.title ILIKE $1 AND s.is_public = TRUE
        ORDER BY s.timestamp DESC LIMIT 20
      `, [`%${query}%`]);
      
      return NextResponse.json({ found: result.rows.length > 0, seals: result.rows });
    }

    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Requête invalide' }, { status: 400 });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(error);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 });
  }
}
EOF

#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -e
GREEN='\0;32m'; NC='\033[0m'
say() { echo -e "${GREEN}▶ $1${NC}"; }

say "1/2 → Écrasement du fichier feed/page.tsx avec la version finale"

cat > src/app/feed/page.tsx << 'EOF'
'use client';
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import Link from 'next/link';

export default function Feed() {
  const [seals, setSeals] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [stories, setStories] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [selectedSeal, setSelectedSeal] = useState<any>(null);
  const [activeCategory, setActiveCategory] = useState('all');
  const [activeStoryIndex, setActiveStoryIndex] = useState<number | null>(null);

  // Barre de recherche globale
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');
  const [searchResults, setSearchResults] = useState<any[]>([]);

  // Créations rattachées (Feed interne)
  const [creations, setCreations] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [newComment, setNewComment] = useState('');
  const [isSubmitting, setIsSubmitting] = useState(false);
  
  // Feed Global
  const [showCreationsFeed, setShowCreationsFeed] = useState(false);
  const [globalCreations, setGlobalCreations] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [loadingGlobal, setLoadingGlobal] = useState(false);

  // Modale de création
  const [showCreateModal, setShowCreateModal] = useState(false);
  const [createContent, setCreateContent] = useState('');
  const [createFile, setCreateFile] = useState<File | null>(null);
  const [isCreating, setIsCreating] = useState(false);

  const myHandle = typeof window !== 'undefined' ? localStorage.getItem('sceau_handle') : null;
  const myPubkey = typeof window !== 'undefined' ? localStorage.getItem('sceau_pubkey') : null;

  const loadFeed = () => {
    setLoading(true);
    fetch(`/api/feed?me=${myHandle || ''}&category=${activeCategory}`)
      .then(res => res.json())
      .then(data => { setSeals(data.seals || []); setLoading(false); });
  };

  const loadStories = () => {
    fetch('/api/feed/stories').then(res => res.json()).then(data => setStories(data.stories || []));
  };

  const loadCreations = (sealId: string) => {
    fetch(`/api/creations?seal_id=${sealId}`).then(r => r.json()).then(d => setCreations(d.creations || []));
  };

  useEffect(() => { loadFeed(); loadStories(); }, [myHandle, activeCategory]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (selectedSeal) loadCreations(selectedSeal.id);
  }, [selectedSeal]);

  const loadGlobalFeed = () => {
    setLoadingGlobal(true);
    fetch('/api/creations/global')
      .then(r => r.json())
      .then(d => { setGlobalCreations(d.creations || []); setLoadingGlobal(false); })
      .catch(() => setLoadingGlobal(false));
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    if (showCreationsFeed) loadGlobalFeed();
  }, [showCreationsFeed]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (activeStoryIndex !== null) {
      const timer = setTimeout(() => {
        if (activeStoryIndex < stories.length - 1) setActiveStoryIndex(activeStoryIndex + 1);
        else setActiveStoryIndex(null);
      }, 5000);
      return () => clearTimeout(timer);
    }
  }, [activeStoryIndex, stories.length]);

  async function submitCreation() {
    if ((!createContent.trim() && !createFile) || !myPubkey || !selectedSeal) return;
    setIsCreating(true);

    const formData = new FormData();
    formData.append('seal_id', selectedSeal.id);
    formData.append('pubkey', myPubkey);
    formData.append('content', createContent);
    formData.append('type', createFile ? (createFile.type.startsWith('video') ? 'video' : 'image') : 'text');
    if (createFile) formData.append('file', createFile);

    await fetch('/api/creations', { method: 'POST', body: formData });
    
    setCreateContent('');
    setCreateFile(null);
    setShowCreateModal(false);
    setIsCreating(false);
    loadCreations(selectedSeal.id);
  }

  async function submitComment() {
    if (!newComment.trim() || !myPubkey || !selectedSeal) return;
    setIsSubmitting(true);

    const formData = new FormData();
    formData.append('seal_id', selectedSeal.id);
    formData.append('pubkey', myPubkey);
    formData.append('content', newComment);
    formData.append('type', 'text');

    await fetch('/api/creations', { method: 'POST', body: formData });
    setNewComment('');
    loadCreations(selectedSeal.id);
    setIsSubmitting(false);
  }

  const rollRandom = async () => {
    const res = await fetch('/api/feed/random');
    const data = await res.json();
    if (data.seal) {
      setSelectedSeal(data.seal);
      if (myPubkey) {
        fetch(`/api/seal/${data.seal.id}/log-view`, { 
          method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, 
          body: JSON.stringify({ pubkey: myPubkey, context: 'random' }) 
        }).catch(() => {});
      }
    } else if (data.reason === 'empty') {
      alert("Le réseau est encore vide. Scelle la première œuvre !");
    } else {
      alert("Erreur réseau.");
    }
  };

  // === SI ON EST DANS LE FEED GLOBAL DES CRÉATIONS ===
  if (showCreationsFeed) {
    return (
      <div className="p-6 max-w-lg mx-auto font-sans min-h-screen bg-[var(--bg-main)] pb-20">
        <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-6">
          <button onClick={() => setShowCreationsFeed(false)} className="text-sm font-bold flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2 bg-[var(--bg-surface)] rounded-xl border border-[var(--border-color)]" style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>
            ← Retour
          </button>
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold" style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>Créations</h1>
        </div>

        {loadingGlobal ? (
           <p className="text-center font-mono py-10">Chargement des créations...</p>
        ) : globalCreations.length === 0 ? (
           <div className="text-center py-10 bg-[var(--bg-surface)] rounded-2xl border border-[var(--border-color)]">
             <p className="italic text-[var(--text-muted)]">Aucune création sur le réseau pour le moment.</p>
           </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="space-y-6">
            {globalCreations.map((c: any) => (
              <div key={c.id} className="bg-[var(--bg-surface)] rounded-2xl border border-[var(--border-color)] shadow-sm overflow-hidden flex flex-col">
                <div className="p-4 flex justify-between items-center border-b border-[var(--border-color)]">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                    <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-gradient-to-br from-[#E76F51] to-[#F4A261] text-white flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-xs">
                      {c.author[0].toUpperCase()}
                    </div>
                    <div>
                      <div className="font-bold text-sm" style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>@{c.author}</div>
                      <div className="text-[0.6rem] text-[var(--text-muted)]">a publié une création</div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>

                {c.media_url && (
                  <div className="w-full bg-black flex justify-center max-h-[400px] overflow-hidden">
                    {c.type === 'video' ? (
                      <video src={c.media_url} controls className="max-h-[400px] w-full object-contain" />
                    ) : (
                      <img src={c.media_url} alt="création" className="w-full object-cover max-h-[400px]" />
                    )}
                  </div>
                )}

                <div className="p-4">
                  {c.content && <p className="text-sm mb-4" style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>{c.content}</p>}
                  
                  <button 
                    onClick={() => { setShowCreationsFeed(false); setSelectedSeal({ id: c.seal_id, title: c.seal_title, author: c.author, description: '' }); }}
                    className="w-full text-left bg-[var(--bg-main)] border border-[var(--border-color)] p-3 rounded-xl hover:border-[#E76F51] transition flex items-center justify-between group"
                  >
                    <div>
                      <div className="text-[0.6rem] font-mono font-bold text-[var(--text-muted)] uppercase">Sceau fondateur</div>
                      <div className="font-bold text-sm" style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>{c.seal_title}</div>
                    </div>
                    <span className="text-[#E76F51] group-hover:translate-x-1 transition">→</span>
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    );
  }

  // === SINON, ON EST DANS LE FEED NORMAL (CADRANS) ===
  return (
    <main className="min-h-screen p-6 font-sans max-w-3xl mx-auto relative">
      <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
        <h1 className="text-4xl font-bold" style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>SCEAU<span className="text-[#E76F51] italic">.</span></h1>
        <Link href="/seal" className="bg-[#E76F51] text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-lg font-bold text-sm shadow-md">+ Sceller</Link>
      </div>

      {/* BARRE DE RECHERCHE GLOBALE */}
      <div className="mb-6 relative z-40">
        <input 
          type="text" 
          placeholder="Rechercher une œuvre ou un créateur..." 
          className="w-full border-2 border-[var(--border-color)] p-3 rounded-xl outline-none bg-[var(--bg-surface)]"
          style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}
          value={searchQuery}
          onChange={async (e) => {
            setSearchQuery(e.target.value);
            if (e.target.value.length > 2) {
              const res = await fetch(`/api/search?q=${encodeURIComponent(e.target.value)}`);
              const d = await res.json();
              setSearchResults(d.results || []);
            } else {
              setSearchResults([]);
            }
          }}
        />
        {searchResults.length > 0 && (
          <div className="absolute top-full left-0 right-0 mt-2 bg-[var(--bg-surface)] border border-[var(--border-color)] rounded-xl shadow-xl overflow-hidden max-h-60 overflow-y-auto">
             {searchResults.map(r => (
                <div key={r.id} onClick={() => { setSelectedSeal(r); setSearchResults([]); setSearchQuery(''); }} className="p-3 border-b border-[var(--border-color)] hover:bg-[var(--bg-main)] cursor-pointer">
                   <div className="font-bold text-sm" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>{r.title}</div>
                   <div className="text-xs text-[var(--text-muted)]">Par @{r.author}</div>
                </div>
             ))}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Stories */}
      {stories.length > 0 && (
        <div className="flex gap-4 overflow-x-auto pb-4 mb-2 snap-x hide-scrollbar">
          {stories.map((story, index) => (
            <div key={story.id} onClick={() => setActiveStoryIndex(index)} className="snap-start shrink-0 flex flex-col items-center cursor-pointer group">
              <div className="w-16 h-16 rounded-full p-[2px] bg-gradient-to-tr from-[#E76F51] to-[#F4A261]">
                <div className="w-full h-full bg-[var(--bg-surface)] rounded-full border-2 border-[var(--bg-surface)] flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-xl overflow-hidden relative">
                  {story.file_path && story.is_hosted ? <img src={story.file_path} alt="story" className="w-full h-full object-cover" /> : <span className="opacity-70">{story.author[0].toUpperCase()}</span>}
                  <span className="absolute bottom-0 right-0 bg-[#E76F51] text-white text-[0.5rem] px-1 rounded-tl-md">⏳</span>
                </div>
              </div>
              <span className="text-[0.6rem] font-bold mt-1 truncate w-16 text-center" style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>@{story.author}</span>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Toggle Feed Cadran / Feed Créations */}
      <div className="flex bg-[var(--bg-surface)] border border-[var(--border-color)] p-1 rounded-xl mb-4">
        <button className="flex-1 py-2 text-sm font-bold rounded-lg bg-[var(--bg-main)] shadow" style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>Cadrans (Œuvres)</button>
        <button onClick={() => setShowCreationsFeed(true)} className="flex-1 py-2 text-sm font-bold rounded-lg opacity-50">Créations (Feed)</button>
      </div>

      <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-4 gap-2">
        <div className="flex gap-2 overflow-x-auto pb-2 snap-x flex-1 hide-scrollbar">
          {['all', 'Art', 'Musique', 'Code', 'Écrit'].map(cat => (
            <button key={cat} onClick={() => setActiveCategory(cat)} className={`snap-start shrink-0 px-4 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold transition border ${activeCategory === cat ? 'bg-[#E76F51] text-white border-[#E76F51] shadow-md' : 'bg-[var(--bg-surface)] text-[var(--text-muted)] border-[var(--border-color)]'}`}>
              {cat === 'all' ? '🌍 Tout' : cat}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
        <button onClick={rollRandom} className="shrink-0 bg-[#2C1810] text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold shadow-md flex items-center gap-1">🎲 Surprise</button>
      </div>

      {loading ? (
         <p className="text-center font-mono py-10">Connexion...</p>
      ) : (
        <div className="grid gap-6 mb-10">
          {seals.filter(s => !s.is_ephemeral).map((seal) => (
            <div key={seal.id} onClick={() => setSelectedSeal(seal)} className="bg-[var(--bg-surface)] p-6 rounded-2xl border border-[var(--border-color)] shadow-sm cursor-pointer hover:shadow-md transition">
              <div className="flex justify-between items-start mb-2">
                <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold" style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>{seal.title}</h2>
              </div>
              <p className="text-sm text-[var(--text-muted)] mb-2">Par @{seal.author} • {seal.category}</p>
              <p className="text-sm text-[var(--text-muted)] italic line-clamp-2">{seal.description}</p>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}

      {/* === LE NOUVEAU CADRAN === */}
      {selectedSeal && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/60 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 backdrop-blur-sm" onClick={() => setSelectedSeal(null)}>
          <div className="bg-[var(--bg-surface)] w-full max-w-lg rounded-3xl relative max-h-[90vh] flex flex-col overflow-hidden shadow-2xl" onClick={e => e.stopPropagation()}>
            
            {/* Header du Cadran */}
            <div className="p-6 border-b border-[var(--border-color)] flex justify-between items-start bg-[var(--bg-surface)] z-10">
              <div>
                <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold mb-1" style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>{selectedSeal.title}</h2>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                  <p className="text-sm text-[var(--text-muted)]">Par @{selectedSeal.author}</p>
                </div>
              </div>
              <button onClick={() => setSelectedSeal(null)} className="text-2xl opacity-50 hover:opacity-100">✕</button>
            </div>

            {/* Contenu */}
            <div className="p-6 overflow-y-auto flex-1 bg-[var(--bg-main)]">
              
              {/* Bloc Description */}
              <div className="bg-[var(--bg-surface)] p-5 rounded-2xl border border-[var(--border-color)] shadow-sm mb-6">
                <h3 className="text-xs font-mono font-bold uppercase mb-2 text-[var(--text-muted)]">Description du créateur</h3>
                <p className="text-sm italic" style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>{selectedSeal.description || "Aucune description fournie."}</p>
              </div>

              {/* Le fameux bouton "Voir les créations" */}
              <div className="mb-6">
                <button 
                  onClick={() => setShowCreateModal(true)}
                  className="w-full flex items-center justify-between p-4 rounded-xl border-2 border-dashed border-[#E76F51] bg-[#FDF4F0] hover:bg-[#FCEAD6] transition group"
                >
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                    <span className="text-2xl">🎬</span>
                    <div className="text-left">
                      <div className="font-bold text-[#C44A2E]">
                        {creations.length > 0 ? `${creations.length} création(s) rattachée(s)` : "Aucune création pour l'instant"}
                      </div>
                      <div className="text-xs text-[#C44A2E] opacity-80">
                        {creations.length > 0 ? "Cliquez pour explorer et commenter" : "Lancez la vôtre !"}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <span className="font-bold text-xl text-[#C44A2E] group-hover:translate-x-1 transition">→</span>
                </button>
              </div>

              {/* Aperçu des 2 dernières créations (ou commentaires) */}
              <div className="space-y-3 mb-6">
                {creations.slice(0, 2).map((c, i) => (
                  <div key={i} className="bg-[var(--bg-surface)] p-4 rounded-xl border border-[var(--border-color)]">
                    <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-2">
                      <span className="font-bold text-xs" style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>@{c.author}</span>
                      <span className="text-[0.6rem] bg-[#E76F51] text-white px-2 py-0.5 rounded">Texte</span>
                    </div>
                    <p className="text-sm" style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>{c.content}</p>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Barre de commentaire */}
            {myHandle && (
              <div className="p-4 border-t border-[var(--border-color)] bg-[var(--bg-surface)]">
                <div className="flex gap-2">
                  <input 
                    type="text" 
                    value={newComment} 
                    onChange={e => setNewComment(e.target.value)} 
                    placeholder="Écrire un commentaire SCEAU..." 
                    className="flex-1 bg-[var(--bg-main)] border border-[var(--border-color)] rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm outline-none focus:border-[#E76F51]" 
                    style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }} 
                    onKeyDown={e => e.key === 'Enter' && submitComment()}
                  />
                  <button 
                    onClick={submitComment} 
                    disabled={isSubmitting}
                    className="bg-[#E76F51] text-white px-6 rounded-xl font-bold disabled:opacity-50"
                  >
                    {isSubmitting ? '...' : 'Publier'}
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* === MODALE : CRÉER UNE CRÉATION RATTACHÉE === */}
      {showCreateModal && selectedSeal && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/60 z-[60] flex items-center justify-center p-4 backdrop-blur-sm" onClick={() => setShowCreateModal(false)}>
          <div className="bg-[var(--bg-surface)] w-full max-w-md rounded-3xl p-6 shadow-2xl" onClick={e => e.stopPropagation()}>
            <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
              <h2 className="text-xl font-bold" style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>Nouvelle Création</h2>
              <button onClick={() => setShowCreateModal(false)} className="text-xl opacity-50 hover:opacity-100">✕</button>
            </div>
            <p className="text-xs text-[var(--text-muted)] mb-4">Rattacher une création au sceau : <strong>{selectedSeal.title}</strong></p>

            <textarea 
              className="w-full border-2 border-[var(--border-color)] rounded-xl p-3 outline-none bg-transparent mb-4" 
              rows={3} 
              placeholder="Écris ton commentaire ou la description de ton média..."
              value={createContent}
              onChange={e => setCreateContent(e.target.value)}
              style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}
            />

            <div className="mb-6">
              <label className="block text-xs font-mono font-bold text-[var(--text-muted)] uppercase mb-2">Média (Photo ou Vidéo)</label>
              <input 
                type="file" 
                accept="image/*,video/*" 
                onChange={e => setCreateFile(e.target.files?.[0] || null)} 
                className="w-full text-sm" 
              />
              {createFile && <p className="text-xs mt-2 text-[#5D7A3A] font-bold">📎 {createFile.name}</p>}
            </div>

            <button 
              onClick={submitCreation}
              disabled={isCreating || (!createContent.trim() && !createFile)}
              className="w-full bg-[#E76F51] text-white py-3 rounded-xl font-bold shadow-md disabled:opacity-50 transition"
            >
              {isCreating ? 'Publication...' : 'Publier la création'}
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Visionneuse Stories */}
      {activeStoryIndex !== null && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black z-[100] flex flex-col">
          <div className="flex gap-1 p-2 bg-gradient-to-b from-black/50 to-transparent absolute top-0 w-full z-10">
            {stories.map((_, i) => (
              <div key={i} className="h-1 flex-1 bg-white/30 rounded-full overflow-hidden">
                {i === activeStoryIndex && <div className="h-full bg-white animate-[progress_5s_linear_forwards]"></div>}
                {i < activeStoryIndex && <div className="h-full bg-white w-full"></div>}
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
          <button onClick={() => setActiveStoryIndex(null)} className="absolute top-6 right-4 text-white text-2xl z-20">✕</button>
          <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col items-center justify-center p-6 text-center">
             {stories[activeStoryIndex].file_path && stories[activeStoryIndex].is_hosted ? (
                <img src={stories[activeStoryIndex].file_path} alt="story" className="max-h-[70vh] rounded-xl object-contain" />
             ) : (
                <div className="bg-[var(--bg-surface)] p-8 rounded-3xl border-4 border-[#E76F51] max-w-sm w-full">
                  <h2 className="text-3xl font-bold mb-4" style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>{stories[activeStoryIndex].title}</h2>
                  <p className="text-[var(--text-muted)] italic">Sceau éphémère de @{stories[activeStoryIndex].author}</p>
                </div>
             )}
             <Link href={`/o/${stories[activeStoryIndex].id}`} className="mt-8 bg-white/10 text-white px-6 py-3 rounded-full font-bold backdrop-blur-md border border-white/20">Voir la preuve complète</Link>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </main>
  );
}

#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -e
GREEN='\033[0;32m'; YELLOW='\033[1;33m'; NC='\033[0m'
say() { echo -e "${GREEN}▶ $1${NC}"; }

# ==========================================================
# A. Sécuriser /api/seal/[id]/ots/refresh (auth propriétaire)
# ==========================================================
say "A/2 → src/app/api/seal/[id]/ots/refresh/route.ts"

cat > "src/app/api/seal/[id]/ots/refresh/route.ts" << 'EOF'
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';
import { upgradeOts } from '@/lib/ots';
import { apiError } from '@/lib/api-error';

export const runtime = 'nodejs';
export const maxDuration = 30;

export async function POST(req: Request, { params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }) {
  const { id } = await params;
  const { searchParams } = new URL(req.url);
  const pubkey = searchParams.get('pubkey');

  if (!pubkey) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Clé manquante' }, { status: 401 });

  try {
    const user = await pool.query(
      "SELECT id FROM users WHERE encode(pubkey, 'base64') = $1",
      [pubkey]
    );
    if (user.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Inconnu' }, { status: 401 });

    const res = await pool.query(
      "SELECT author_id, hash, ots_proof FROM seals WHERE id = $1",
      [id]
    );
    if (res.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Introuvable' }, { status: 404 });

    const row = res.rows[0];
    if (row.author_id !== user.rows[0].id) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Non autorisé' }, { status: 403 });
    }
    if (!row.ots_proof) {
      return NextResponse.json({ status: 'no_proof', message: 'Aucune preuve .ots stockée.' });
    }

    const upgraded = await upgradeOts(new Uint8Array(row.hash), row.ots_proof);
    if (!upgraded) {
      return NextResponse.json({ status: 'pending', message: 'Pas encore confirmé. Réessaie dans quelques heures.' });
    }

    await pool.query(
      "UPDATE seals SET ots_proof = $1, ots_upgraded_at = NOW() WHERE id = $2",
      [upgraded.toString('base64'), id]
    );
    return NextResponse.json({ status: 'upgraded', size: upgraded.length });
  } catch (e) {
    return apiError(e, 'Erreur lors de la mise à jour de la preuve');
  }
}
EOF

#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -e
GREEN='\0;32m'; NC='\033[0m'
say() { echo -e "${GREEN}▶ $1${NC}"; }

# ==========================================================
# 1. API : Génération et listing des clés
# ==========================================================
say "1/3 → API /api/auth/api-keys (Génération et Lecture)"

mkdir -p "src/app/api/auth/api-keys"
cat > "src/app/api/auth/api-keys/route.ts" << 'EOF'
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';
import crypto from 'crypto';

// GET : Lister les clés de l'utilisateur (masquées)
export async function GET(req: Request) {
  const { searchParams } = new URL(req.url);
  const pubkey = searchParams.get('pubkey');
  if (!pubkey) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Clé manquante' }, { status: 400 });

  try {
    const user = await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE encode(pubkey, 'base64') = $1", [pubkey]);
    if (user.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Inconnu' }, { status: 401 });

    const result = await pool.query(
      "SELECT id, name, created_at, last_used_at FROM api_keys WHERE user_id = $1 ORDER BY created_at DESC",
      [user.rows[0].id]
    );
    return NextResponse.json({ keys: result.rows });
  } catch (e) {
    console.error(e);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

// POST : Générer une nouvelle clé
export async function POST(req: Request) {
  const { pubkey, name } = await req.json();
  if (!pubkey || !name) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Champs manquants' }, { status: 400 });

  try {
    const user = await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE encode(pubkey, 'base64') = $1", [pubkey]);
    if (user.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Inconnu' }, { status: 401 });

    // Générer une clé aléatoire (sceau_live_ + 32 caractères hexadécimaux)
    const rawKey = `sceau_live_${crypto.randomBytes(16).toString('hex')}`;
    
    // On ne stocke que le hash SHA-256 de la clé en base de données
    const keyHash = crypto.createHash('sha256').update(rawKey).digest('hex');

    await pool.query(
      "INSERT INTO api_keys (user_id, key_hash, name) VALUES ($1, $2, $3)",
      [user.rows[0].id, keyHash, name]
    );

    // On retourne la clé EN CLAIR une seule fois. Elle ne sera plus jamais visible.
    return NextResponse.json({ success: true, key: rawKey });
  } catch (e) {
    console.error(e);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 });
  }
}
EOF

# ==========================================================
# 2. API : Route pour les plugins (WordPress / GitHub)
# ==========================================================
say "2/3 → API /api/v1/plugin/seal (Le point d'entrée des plugins)"

mkdir -p "src/app/api/v1/plugin/seal"
cat > "src/app/api/v1/plugin/seal/route.ts" << 'EOF'
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';
import crypto from 'crypto';
import { sha256 } from '@noble/hashes/sha256';
import { submitToOts } from '@/lib/ots';

export const runtime = 'nodejs';
export const maxDuration = 30;

const CORS = {
  'Access-Control-Allow-Origin': '*',
  'Access-Control-Allow-Methods': 'POST, OPTIONS',
  'Access-Control-Allow-Headers': 'Content-Type, Authorization',
};

export async function OPTIONS() {
  return new NextResponse(null, { status: 204, headers: CORS });
}

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  try {
    const { apiKey, title, content, category } = await req.json();

    if (!apiKey || !title || !content) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Champs manquants (apiKey, title, content)' }, { status: 400, headers: CORS });
    }

    // 1. Vérifier la clé API
    const keyHash = crypto.createHash('sha256').update(apiKey).digest('hex');
    const keyQuery = await pool.query(
      "SELECT user_id FROM api_keys WHERE key_hash = $1",
      [keyHash]
    );

    if (keyQuery.rows.length === 0) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Clé API invalide' }, { status: 401, headers: CORS });
    }

    const userId = keyQuery.rows[0].user_id;

    // Mettre à jour la date de dernière utilisation
    await pool.query("UPDATE api_keys SET last_used_at = NOW() WHERE key_hash = $1", [keyHash]);

    // 2. Calculer le hash du contenu (texte)
    const textBuffer = Buffer.from(content, 'utf-8');
    const hashBytes = sha256(new Uint8Array(textBuffer));
    const hashB64 = Buffer.from(hashBytes).toString('base64');

    // 3. Ancrage OpenTimestamps (best effort)
    let otsProof: string | null = null;
    try {
      const ots = await submitToOts(new Uint8Array(hashBytes));
      if (ots.ok && ots.proof) {
        otsProof = ots.proof.toString('base64');
      }
    } catch (e) { console.warn('[Plugin OTS]', e); }

    // 4. Enregistrer le sceau
    const result = await pool.query(
      `INSERT INTO seals (
        hash, timestamp, author_id, title, description, is_public, visibility,
        is_hosted, file_path, file_size, is_flash, is_mystery, unlock_likes, is_ephemeral,
        ots_proof, ots_submitted_at, category
      ) VALUES (
        decode($1, 'base64'), NOW(), $2, $3, '', true, 'public',
        false, null, 0, false, false, 0, false,
        $4, $5, $6
      ) RETURNING id`,
      [hashB64, userId, title, otsProof, otsProof ? new Date() : null, category || 'Art']
    );

    return NextResponse.json({
      success: true,
      seal_id: result.rows[0].id,
      hash: hashB64,
      ots_secured: otsProof !== null
    }, { headers: CORS });

  } catch (e) {
    console.error('[plugin/seal]', e);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur interne' }, { status: 500, headers: CORS });
  }
}
EOF

#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -e
GREEN='\0;32m'; NC='\033[0m'
say() { echo -e "${GREEN}▶ $1${NC}"; }

# 1. API Feed Global des Créations
# ==========================================================
say "1/2 → API /api/creations/global (Feed aléatoire)"

mkdir -p "src/app/api/creations/global"
cat > "src/app/api/creations/global/route.ts" << 'EOF'
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';

export const runtime = 'nodejs';

export async function GET() {
  try {
    // On récupère toutes les créations principales (pas les commentaires)
    // On fait un ORDER BY RANDOM() pour le côté "découverte" et aléatoire demandé
    const result = await pool.query(`
      SELECT a.id, a.type, a.content, a.media_url, a.created_at, 
             u.handle as author,
             s.id as seal_id, s.title as seal_title
      FROM attachments a 
      JOIN users u ON a.user_id = u.id
      JOIN seals s ON a.seal_id = s.id
      WHERE a.parent_id IS NULL 
      ORDER BY RANDOM() 
      LIMIT 50
    `);
    return NextResponse.json({ creations: result.rows });
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('[creations/global]', e);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 });
  }
}
EOF

                    className="w-full text-left bg-[var(--bg-main)] border border-[var(--border-color)] p-3 rounded-xl hover:border-[#E76F51] transition flex items-center justify-between group"

# ==========================================================
# 2. API Attachments (Créations rattachées) - Refonte
# ==========================================================
say "2/4 → API Attachments (support vidéo/photo/texte + commentaires)"

mkdir -p "src/app/api/creations"
cat > "src/app/api/creations/route.ts" << 'EOF'
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';
import { r2Client } from '@/lib/r2';
import { PutObjectCommand } from '@aws-sdk/client-s3';
import { safeKeyPart } from '@/lib/safe-key';
import { requireS3 } from '@/lib/env';

export const runtime = 'nodejs';

// GET : Récupérer les créations d'un sceau (ou d'une création si parent_id est fourni)
export async function GET(req: Request) {
  const { searchParams } = new URL(req.url);
  const sealId = searchParams.get('seal_id');
  const parentId = searchParams.get('parent_id');

  if (!sealId) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'seal_id manquant' }, { status: 400 });

  try {
    let sql = `
      SELECT a.id, a.type, a.content, a.media_url, a.created_at, a.parent_id, u.handle as author
      FROM attachments a 
      JOIN users u ON a.user_id = u.id
      WHERE a.seal_id = $1
    `;
    const params: any[] = [sealId];

    if (parentId) {
      sql += " AND a.parent_id = $2";
      params.push(parentId);
    } else {
      sql += " AND a.parent_id IS NULL"; // On ne récupère que le niveau principal
    }

    sql += " ORDER BY a.created_at DESC LIMIT 50";
    
    const result = await pool.query(sql, params);
    return NextResponse.json({ creations: result.rows });
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('[creations GET]', e);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

// POST : Créer une nouvelle création (vidéo, image, ou texte)
export async function POST(req: Request) {
  try {
    const data = await req.formData();
    const seal_id = data.get('seal_id') as string;
    const pubkey = data.get('pubkey') as string;
    const content = data.get('content') as string || '';
    const type = data.get('type') as string || 'text';
    const parent_id = data.get('parent_id') as string || null;
    const file = data.get('file') as File | null;

    if (!seal_id || !pubkey) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Champs manquants' }, { status: 400 });

    const user = await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE encode(pubkey, 'base64') = $1", [pubkey]);
    if (user.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Inconnu' }, { status: 401 });

    let media_url: string | null = null;

    if (file && file.size > 0) {
      const buffer = Buffer.from(await file.arrayBuffer());
      const { endpoint, bucket } = requireS3();
      const key = `creations/${safeKeyPart(seal_id.slice(0, 8))}/${Date.now()}-${safeKeyPart(file.name)}`;
      
      await r2Client.send(new PutObjectCommand({
        Bucket: bucket,
        Key: key,
        Body: buffer,
        ContentType: file.type || 'application/octet-stream',
      }));
      media_url = `${endpoint}/${bucket}/${key}`;
    }

    const res = await pool.query(
      `INSERT INTO attachments (seal_id, user_id, content, type, media_url, parent_id)
       VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5, $6) RETURNING id`,
      [seal_id, user.rows[0].id, content, type, media_url, parent_id]
    );

    return NextResponse.json({ success: true, id: res.rows[0].id });
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('[creations POST]', e);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur lors de la création' }, { status: 500 });
  }
}
EOF

echo -e "${GREEN}✅ API Créations prête (vidéos, images, commentaires imbriqués).${NC}"

#!/usr/bin/env bash

      if (!allowedExtensions.includes(ext)) {

          error: `Format de fichier interdit (.${ext}). Formats acceptés : images, vidéos, audios, pdf, txt, zip, documents Office.` 
        }, { status: 400 });

    }

    if (!isFlash && file && file.size > 0) {"""

      // === SÉCURITÉ : Vérification de l'extension ===

=========================================
FICHIER : ./patch-merkle-api.sh
=========================================

#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -e
GREEN='\0;32m'; NC='\033[0m'
say() { echo -e "${GREEN}▶ $1${NC}"; }

# ==========================================================
# 1. Le Moteur Merkle (src/lib/merkle.ts)
# ==========================================================
say "1/2 → Création du moteur Merkle (src/lib/merkle.ts)"

cat > src/lib/merkle.ts << 'EOF'
import { sha256 } from '@noble/hashes/sha256';

/**
 * Prend une liste de hashs (en Base64) et calcule le Hash Racine (Merkle Root).
 * Les hashs sont triés pour garantir que l'ordre des fichiers n'affecte pas le résultat.
 */
export function buildMerkleRoot(hashesB64: string[]): string {
  if (hashesB64.length === 0) return '';
  if (hashesB64.length === 1) return hashesB64[0];

  // On s'assure que tous les hashs sont décodés en Buffer pour la concaténation
  let currentLevel = hashesB64.map(h => Buffer.from(h, 'base64'));

  while (currentLevel.length > 1) {
    const nextLevel: Buffer[] = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < currentLevel.length; i += 2) {
      if (i + 1 < currentLevel.length) {
        // Hachage de la concaténation de deux hashs
        const combined = Buffer.concat([currentLevel[i], currentLevel[i + 1]]);
        const hash = sha256(new Uint8Array(combined));
        nextLevel.push(Buffer.from(hash));
      } else {
        // S'il reste un élément impair, on le passe au niveau supérieur tel quel
        nextLevel.push(currentLevel[i]);
      }
    }
    currentLevel = nextLevel;
  }

  return currentLevel[0].toString('base64');
}
EOF

# ==========================================================
# 2. L'API Projets (src/app/api/projects/route.ts)
# ==========================================================
say "2/2 → Création de l'API Projets"

mkdir -p "src/app/api/projects"
cat > "src/app/api/projects/route.ts" << 'EOF'
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';
import { r2Client } from '@/lib/r2';
import { PutObjectCommand } from '@aws-sdk/client-s3';
import { submitToOts } from '@/lib/ots';
import { buildMerkleRoot } from '@/lib/merkle';
import { safeKeyPart } from '@/lib/safe-key';
import { apiError } from '@/lib/api-error';
import { requireS3 } from '@/lib/env';
import { sha256 } from '@noble/hashes/sha256';

export const runtime = 'nodejs';
export const maxDuration = 30;

export async function POST(req: Request) {
  try {
    const data = await req.formData();
    const title = ((data.get('title') as string) || '').trim();
    const description = ((data.get('description') as string) || '').trim();
    const pubkey = data.get('pubkey') as string;
    const isHosted = data.get('isHosted') !== 'false';
    const files = (data.getAll('files') as File[]).filter((f) => f && f.size > 0);

    if (!title) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Titre requis' }, { status: 400 });
    if (files.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Aucun fichier sélectionné' }, { status: 400 });
    if (files.length > 100) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Maximum 100 fichiers par projet' }, { status: 400 });

    const user = await pool.query("SELECT id FROM users WHERE encode(pubkey, 'base64') = $1", [pubkey]);
    if (user.rows.length === 0) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Utilisateur inconnu' }, { status: 401 });
    const authorId = user.rows[0].id;

    // 1. Calculer les hashs de tous les fichiers
    const fileRecords: { name: string; hashB64: string; size: number; file: File }[] = [];
    let totalSize = 0;

    const allowedExtensions = ['jpg', 'jpeg', 'png', 'gif', 'webp', 'mp4', 'webm', 'mp3', 'wav', 'ogg', 'pdf', 'txt', 'zip', 'doc', 'docx', 'xls', 'xlsx', 'ppt', 'pptx', 'html', 'css', 'js', 'ts', 'json'];

    for (const file of files) {
      totalSize += file.size;
      const ext = file.name.toLowerCase().split('.').pop() || '';
      if (!allowedExtensions.includes(ext)) {
        return NextResponse.json({ error: `Format interdit : ${file.name} (.${ext})` }, { status: 400 });
      }

      const buffer = Buffer.from(await file.arrayBuffer());
      const hash = sha256(new Uint8Array(buffer));
      const hashB64 = Buffer.from(hash).toString('base64');

      fileRecords.push({ name: file.name, hashB64, size: file.size, file });
    }

    if (totalSize > 100 * 1024 * 1024) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Le projet dépasse la taille maximale (100 Mo)' }, { status: 413 });
    }

    // 2. Construire l'Arbre de Merkle
    const hashes = fileRecords.map(r => r.hashB64);
    const merkleRootB64 = buildMerkleRoot(hashes);

    // 3. Ancrer la racine Merkle sur Bitcoin (OpenTimestamps)
    let otsProof: string | null = null;
    try {
      const rootHashBytes = new Uint8Array(Buffer.from(merkleRootB64, 'base64'));
      const ots = await submitToOts(rootHashBytes);
      if (ots.ok && ots.proof) {
        otsProof = ots.proof.toString('base64');
      }
    } catch (e) {
      console.warn('[OTS] exception :', e);
    }

    // 4. Uploader les fichiers sur S3 (si demandé)
    const uploadedRecords: { name: string; hashB64: string; size: number; path: string | null }[] = [];
    
    if (isHosted) {
      const { endpoint, bucket } = requireS3();
      for (const rec of fileRecords) {
        const buffer = Buffer.from(await rec.file.arrayBuffer());
        const safeName = safeKeyPart(rec.name, 60);
        const key = `projects/${authorId.slice(0, 8)}/${Date.now()}-${safeName}`;
        
        await r2Client.send(new PutObjectCommand({
          Bucket: bucket,
          Key: key,
          Body: buffer,
          ContentType: rec.file.type || 'application/octet-stream',
          ContentLength: buffer.length,
        }));
        
        uploadedRecords.push({ name: rec.name, hashB64: rec.hashB64, size: rec.size, path: `${endpoint}/${bucket}/${key}` });
      }
    } else {
      fileRecords.forEach(rec => uploadedRecords.push({ name: rec.name, hashB64: rec.hashB64, size: rec.size, path: null }));
    }

    // 5. Enregistrer en base de données (Transaction)
    const client = await pool.connect();
    let projectId: string;

    try {
      await client.query('BEGIN');

      const projectRes = await client.query(
        `INSERT INTO projects (author_id, title, description, merkle_root, ots_proof)
         VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5) RETURNING id`,
        [authorId, title, description, merkleRootB64, otsProof]
      );
      projectId = projectRes.rows[0].id;

      for (const rec of uploadedRecords) {
        await client.query(
          `INSERT INTO project_files (project_id, file_name, file_hash, file_size, file_path)
           VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5)`,
          [projectId, rec.name, rec.hashB64, rec.size, rec.path]
        );
      }

      await client.query('COMMIT');
    } catch (e) {
      await client.query('ROLLBACK');
      throw e;
    } finally {
      client.release();
    }

    return NextResponse.json({
      success: true,
      project_id: projectId,
      merkle_root: merkleRootB64,
      files_count: uploadedRecords.length,
      ots_secured: otsProof !== null
    });

  } catch (e) {
    return apiError(e, 'Erreur lors de la création du projet');
  }
}
EOF

echo ""
echo -e "${GREEN}✅ Moteur Merkle et API Projets prêts.${NC}"

#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -e
GREEN='\0;32m'; NC='\033[0m'
say() { echo -e "${GREEN}▶ $1${NC}"; }

# ==========================================================
# 1. Création du Plugin WordPress
# ==========================================================
say "1/3 → Création du fichier sceau-plugin.php"

mkdir -p public/downloads
cat > public/downloads/sceau-plugin.php << 'EOF'
<?php
/**
 * Plugin Name: SCEAU - Preuve d'Antériorité
 * Description: Scelle automatiquement vos articles sur la blockchain Bitcoin à chaque publication.
 * Version: 1.0
 * Author: SCEAU
 */

// Sécurité : Empêche l'accès direct au fichier
if (!defined('ABSPATH')) {
    exit;
}

// Ajouter la page de réglages dans le menu
add_action('admin_menu', 'sceau_add_admin_menu');
function sceau_add_admin_menu() {
    add_options_page(
        'SCEAU Réglages',
        'SCEAU',
        'manage_options',
        'sceau',
        'sceau_settings_page'
    );
}

// Enregistrer la clé API
add_action('admin_init', 'sceau_register_settings');
function sceau_register_settings() {
    register_setting('sceau_settings_group', 'sceau_api_key');
}

// Affichage de la page de réglages
function sceau_settings_page() {
    ?>
    <div class="wrap">
        <h2>Configuration de SCEAU</h2>
        <p>Entrez votre clé API générée sur <a href="https://sceau-app.vercel.app/settings" target="_blank">votre profil SCEAU</a>.</p>
        <form method="post" action="options.php">
            <?php settings_fields('sceau_settings_group'); ?>
            <?php do_settings_sections('sceau_settings_group'); ?>
            <table class="form-table">
                <tr valign="top">
                <th scope="row">Clé API SCEAU</th>
                <td>
                    <input type="password" name="sceau_api_key" value="<?php echo esc_attr(get_option('sceau_api_key')); ?>" style="width: 100%; max-width: 400px;" placeholder="sceau_live_..." />
                </td>
                </tr>
            </table>
            <?php submit_button(); ?>
        </form>
    </div>
    <?php
}

// Fonction qui envoie le scellement à SCEAU à la publication
add_action('transition_post_status', 'sceau_seal_on_publish', 10, 3);
function sceau_seal_on_publish($new_status, $old_status, $post) {
    // On ne scelle que si l'article passe à "publié"
    if ($new_status === 'publish' && $old_status !== 'publish' && $post->post_type === 'post') {
        
        $api_key = get_option('sceau_api_key');
        if (!$api_key) return;

        $title = $post->post_title;
        $content = strip_tags($post->post_content);
        $category = 'Écrit'; // On pourrait récupérer la catégorie WP

        $response = wp_remote_post('https://sceau-app.vercel.app/api/v1/plugin/seal', array(
            'method'    => 'POST',
            'timeout'   => 15,
            'headers'   => array('Content-Type' => 'application/json'),
            'body'      => wp_json_encode(array(
                'apiKey'   => $api_key,
                'title'    => $title,
                'content'  => $content,
                'category' => $category
            ))
        ));

        if (is_wp_error($response)) {
            error_log('SCEAU Erreur : ' . $response->get_error_message());
        } else {
            $body = wp_remote_retrieve_body($response);
            $data = json_decode($body, true);
            if (isset($data['success']) && $data['success']) {
                // On ajoute un marqueur dans les métadonnées de l'article pour dire qu'il est scellé
                update_post_meta($post->ID, 'sceau_sealed', 'true');
                update_post_meta($post->ID, 'sceau_id', $data['seal_id']);
            }
        }
    }
}

// Ajoute le badge "Scellé par SCEAU" à la fin de l'article
add_filter('the_content', 'sceau_append_badge');
function sceau_append_badge($content) {
    if (is_single() && get_post_meta(get_the_ID(), 'sceau_sealed', true) === 'true') {
        $seal_id = get_post_meta(get_the_ID(), 'sceau_id', true);
        $badge = '<div style="margin-top: 30px; padding: 15px; background: #F5F9EE; border: 1px solid #A8C77E; border-radius: 8px; text-align: center; font-family: sans-serif;">';
        $badge .= '<span style="color: #5D7A3A; font-weight: bold;">✅ Scellé par SCEAU</span><br>';
        $badge .= '<span style="font-size: 12px; color: #7A9B5A;">Ce contenu a été horodaté et ancré sur Bitcoin.</span><br>';
        $badge .= '<a href="https://sceau-app.vercel.app/o/' . esc_attr($seal_id) . '" target="_blank" style="font-size: 12px; color: #E76F51; font-weight: bold; text-decoration: none;">Voir la preuve</a>';
        $badge .= '</div>';
        return $content . $badge;
    }
    return $content;
}
EOF

# Créer un ZIP pour faciliter l'installation
cd public/downloads
zip sceau-wordpress.zip sceau-plugin.php
cd ../..

  push:
    branches: [ "main", "master" ]

jobs:
  sceau_seal:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
      - name: Envoi du commit à SCEAU
        run: |
          COMMIT_MSG=$(git log -1 --pretty=%B)
          COMMIT_SHA=$(git rev-parse --short HEAD)
          
          curl -X POST https://sceau-app.vercel.app/api/v1/plugin/seal \
            -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
            -d '{
              "apiKey": "'"${{ secrets.SCEAU_API_KEY }}"'",
              "title": "Code commit '"$COMMIT_SHA"'",
              "content": "'"$COMMIT_MSG"'",
              "category": "Code"
            }'
EOF

=========================================
FICHIER : ./patch-timeline.sh
=========================================

#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -e
GREEN='\0;32m'; NC='\033[0m'
say() { echo -e "${GREEN}▶ $1${NC}"; }

# ==========================================================
# 1. API Timeline (Remplace Pulse ou s'ajoute)
# ==========================================================
say "1/2 → API /api/verify/timeline"

mkdir -p "src/app/api/verify/timeline"
cat > "src/app/api/verify/timeline/route.ts" << 'EOF'
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { pool } from '@/db/pool';

export const runtime = 'nodejs';

export async function GET(req: Request) {
  const { searchParams } = new URL(req.url);
  const start = searchParams.get('start');
  const end = searchParams.get('end');
  const isLive = searchParams.get('live') === 'true';

  try {
    let sql = `
      SELECT s.id, s.title, s.category, s.timestamp, u.handle as author
      FROM seals s JOIN users u ON s.author_id = u.id
      WHERE s.is_public = TRUE
    `;
    const params: any[] = [];

    if (isLive) {
      // Mode direct : on prend les 20 derniers
      sql += ` ORDER BY s.timestamp DESC LIMIT 20`;
    } else {
      // Mode période : filtré par dates
      if (start) {
        sql += ` AND s.timestamp >= $${params.length + 1}`;
        params.push(new Date(start));
      }
      if (end) {
        sql += ` AND s.timestamp <= $${params.length + 1}`;
        params.push(new Date(end));
      }
      sql += ` ORDER BY s.timestamp DESC LIMIT 50`;
    }

    const result = await pool.query(sql, params);
    return NextResponse.json({ timeline: result.rows });
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('[timeline]', e);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Erreur' }, { status: 500 });
  }
}
EOF

# ==========================================================
# 2. UI Frise dans Verifier
# ==========================================================
say "2/2 → Mise à jour UI /verify (Frise Chronologique)"

python3 - << 'PYEOF'
import sys
p = 'src/app/verify/page.tsx'
try:
    s = open(p, encoding='utf-8').read()
except FileNotFoundError:

<?php
/**
 * Plugin Name: SCEAU - Preuve d'Antériorité
 * Description: Scelle automatiquement vos articles sur la blockchain Bitcoin à chaque publication.
 * Version: 1.0
 * Author: SCEAU
 */

// Sécurité : Empêche l'accès direct au fichier
if (!defined('ABSPATH')) {
    exit;
}

// Ajouter la page de réglages dans le menu
add_action('admin_menu', 'sceau_add_admin_menu');
function sceau_add_admin_menu() {
    add_options_page(
        'SCEAU Réglages',
        'SCEAU',
        'manage_options',
        'sceau',
        'sceau_settings_page'
    );
}

// Enregistrer la clé API
add_action('admin_init', 'sceau_register_settings');
function sceau_register_settings() {
    register_setting('sceau_settings_group', 'sceau_api_key');
}

// Affichage de la page de réglages
function sceau_settings_page() {
    ?>
    <div class="wrap">
        <h2>Configuration de SCEAU</h2>
        <p>Entrez votre clé API générée sur <a href="https://sceau-app.vercel.app/settings" target="_blank">votre profil SCEAU</a>.</p>
        <form method="post" action="options.php">
            <?php settings_fields('sceau_settings_group'); ?>
            <?php do_settings_sections('sceau_settings_group'); ?>
            <table class="form-table">
                <tr valign="top">
                <th scope="row">Clé API SCEAU</th>
                <td>
                    <input type="password" name="sceau_api_key" value="<?php echo esc_attr(get_option('sceau_api_key')); ?>" style="width: 100%; max-width: 400px;" placeholder="sceau_live_..." />
                </td>
                </tr>
            </table>
            <?php submit_button(); ?>
        </form>
    </div>
    <?php
}

// Fonction qui envoie le scellement à SCEAU à la publication
add_action('transition_post_status', 'sceau_seal_on_publish', 10, 3);
function sceau_seal_on_publish($new_status, $old_status, $post) {
    // On ne scelle que si l'article passe à "publié"
    if ($new_status === 'publish' && $old_status !== 'publish' && $post->post_type === 'post') {
        
        $api_key = get_option('sceau_api_key');
        if (!$api_key) return;

        $title = $post->post_title;
        $content = strip_tags($post->post_content);
        $category = 'Écrit'; // On pourrait récupérer la catégorie WP

        $response = wp_remote_post('https://sceau-app.vercel.app/api/v1/plugin/seal', array(
            'method'    => 'POST',
            'timeout'   => 15,
            'headers'   => array('Content-Type' => 'application/json'),
            'body'      => wp_json_encode(array(
                'apiKey'   => $api_key,
                'title'    => $title,
                'content'  => $content,
                'category' => $category
            ))
        ));

        if (is_wp_error($response)) {
            error_log('SCEAU Erreur : ' . $response->get_error_message());
        } else {
            $body = wp_remote_retrieve_body($response);
            $data = json_decode($body, true);
            if (isset($data['success']) && $data['success']) {
                // On ajoute un marqueur dans les métadonnées de l'article pour dire qu'il est scellé
                update_post_meta($post->ID, 'sceau_sealed', 'true');
                update_post_meta($post->ID, 'sceau_id', $data['seal_id']);
            }
        }
    }
}

// Ajoute le badge "Scellé par SCEAU" à la fin de l'article
add_filter('the_content', 'sceau_append_badge');
function sceau_append_badge($content) {
    if (is_single() && get_post_meta(get_the_ID(), 'sceau_sealed', true) === 'true') {
        $seal_id = get_post_meta(get_the_ID(), 'sceau_id', true);
        $badge = '<div style="margin-top: 30px; padding: 15px; background: #F5F9EE; border: 1px solid #A8C77E; border-radius: 8px; text-align: center; font-family: sans-serif;">';
        $badge .= '<span style="color: #5D7A3A; font-weight: bold;">✅ Scellé par SCEAU</span><br>';
        $badge .= '<span style="font-size: 12px; color: #7A9B5A;">Ce contenu a été horodaté et ancré sur Bitcoin.</span><br>';
        $badge .= '<a href="https://sceau-app.vercel.app/o/' . esc_attr($seal_id) . '" target="_blank" style="font-size: 12px; color: #E76F51; font-weight: bold; text-decoration: none;">Voir la preuve</a>';
        $badge .= '</div>';
        return $content . $badge;
    }
    return $content;
}

# 🏛️ SCEAU Protocol (v1.0)

Le protocole de preuve d'antériorité de SCEAU.
Permet d'ancrer l'empreinte cryptographique d'un fichier sur la blockchain Bitcoin.

## 🚀 Utilisation via l'API publique (Terminal)

Vous pouvez utiliser l'API REST gratuite pour sceller un document instantanément avec `curl` :

```bash
curl -X POST https://sceau-app.vercel.app/api/v1/public/seal \
  -F "file=@mon-document.pdf"

  "main": "src/index.ts",
  "scripts": {
    "build": "tsc"
  },
  "dependencies": {
    "@noble/hashes": "^1.4.0"
  }
