'use client';
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import Link from 'next/link';

export default function Feed() {
  const [seals, setSeals] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [stories, setStories] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [selectedSeal, setSelectedSeal] = useState<any>(null);
  const [activeCategory, setActiveCategory] = useState('all');
  const [activeStoryIndex, setActiveStoryIndex] = useState<number | null>(null);

  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');
  const [searchResults, setSearchResults] = useState<any[]>([]);

  const [creations, setCreations] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [newComment, setNewComment] = useState('');
  const [isSubmitting, setIsSubmitting] = useState(false);
  
  const [showCreationsFeed, setShowCreationsFeed] = useState(false);
  const [globalCreations, setGlobalCreations] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [loadingGlobal, setLoadingGlobal] = useState(false);

  const [showCreateModal, setShowCreateModal] = useState(false);
  const [createContent, setCreateContent] = useState('');
  const [createFile, setCreateFile] = useState<File | null>(null);
  const [isCreating, setIsCreating] = useState(false);

  const myHandle = typeof window !== 'undefined' ? localStorage.getItem('sceau_handle') : null;
  const myPubkey = typeof window !== 'undefined' ? localStorage.getItem('sceau_pubkey') : null;

  const loadFeed = () => {
    setLoading(true);
    fetch(`/api/feed?me=${myHandle || ''}&category=${activeCategory}`)
      .then(res => res.json())
      .then(data => { setSeals(data.seals || []); setLoading(false); });
  };

  const loadStories = () => {
    fetch('/api/feed/stories').then(res => res.json()).then(data => setStories(data.stories || []));
  };

  const loadCreations = (sealId: string) => {
    fetch(`/api/creations?seal_id=${sealId}`).then(r => r.json()).then(d => setCreations(d.creations || []));
  };

  useEffect(() => { loadFeed(); loadStories(); }, [myHandle, activeCategory]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (selectedSeal) loadCreations(selectedSeal.id);
  }, [selectedSeal]);

  const loadGlobalFeed = () => {
    setLoadingGlobal(true);
    fetch('/api/creations/global')
      .then(r => r.json())
      .then(d => { setGlobalCreations(d.creations || []); setLoadingGlobal(false); })
      .catch(() => setLoadingGlobal(false));
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    if (showCreationsFeed) loadGlobalFeed();
  }, [showCreationsFeed]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (activeStoryIndex !== null) {
      const timer = setTimeout(() => {
        if (activeStoryIndex < stories.length - 1) setActiveStoryIndex(activeStoryIndex + 1);
        else setActiveStoryIndex(null);
      }, 5000);
      return () => clearTimeout(timer);
    }
  }, [activeStoryIndex, stories.length]);

  async function likeCreation(id: string) {
    if (!myPubkey) return alert('Connecte-toi pour liker !');
    await fetch('/api/creations/like', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ creation_id: id, pubkey: myPubkey })
    });
    loadCreations(selectedSeal.id);
    if (showCreationsFeed) loadGlobalFeed();
  }

  async function submitCreation() {
    if ((!createContent.trim() && !createFile) || !myPubkey || !selectedSeal) return;
    setIsCreating(true);

    const formData = new FormData();
    formData.append('seal_id', selectedSeal.id);
    formData.append('pubkey', myPubkey);
    formData.append('content', createContent);
    formData.append('type', createFile ? (createFile.type.startsWith('video') ? 'video' : 'image') : 'text');
    if (createFile) formData.append('file', createFile);

    await fetch('/api/creations', { method: 'POST', body: formData });
    
    setCreateContent('');
    setCreateFile(null);
    setShowCreateModal(false);
    setIsCreating(false);
    loadCreations(selectedSeal.id);
  }

  async function submitComment() {
    if (!newComment.trim() || !myPubkey || !selectedSeal) return;
    setIsSubmitting(true);

    const formData = new FormData();
    formData.append('seal_id', selectedSeal.id);
    formData.append('pubkey', myPubkey);
    formData.append('content', newComment);
    formData.append('type', 'text');

    await fetch('/api/creations', { method: 'POST', body: formData });
    setNewComment('');
    loadCreations(selectedSeal.id);
    setIsSubmitting(false);
  }

  const rollRandom = async () => {
    const res = await fetch('/api/feed/random');
    const data = await res.json();
    if (data.seal) {
      setSelectedSeal(data.seal);
      if (myPubkey) {
        fetch(`/api/seal/${data.seal.id}/log-view`, { 
          method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, 
          body: JSON.stringify({ pubkey: myPubkey, context: 'random' }) 
        }).catch(() => {});
      }
    } else if (data.reason === 'empty') {
      alert("Le réseau est encore vide. Scelle la première œuvre !");
    } else {
      alert("Erreur réseau.");
    }
  };

  // === FEED GLOBAL (CRÉATIONS) ===
  if (showCreationsFeed) {
    return (
      <div className="p-6 max-w-lg mx-auto font-sans min-h-screen bg-[var(--bg-main)] pb-20">
        <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-6">
          <button onClick={() => setShowCreationsFeed(false)} className="text-sm font-bold flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2 bg-[var(--bg-surface)] rounded-xl border border-[var(--border-color)]" style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>
            ← Retour
          </button>
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold" style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>Créations</h1>
        </div>

        {loadingGlobal ? (
           <p className="text-center font-mono py-10">Chargement...</p>
        ) : globalCreations.length === 0 ? (
           <div className="text-center py-10 bg-[var(--bg-surface)] rounded-2xl border border-[var(--border-color)]">
             <p className="italic text-[var(--text-muted)]">Aucune création sur le réseau.</p>
           </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="space-y-6">
            {globalCreations.map((c: any) => (
              <div key={c.id} className="bg-[var(--bg-surface)] rounded-3xl border border-[var(--border-color)] shadow-sm overflow-hidden flex flex-col">
                <div className="p-4 flex justify-between items-center border-b border-[var(--border-color)]">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                    <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-gradient-to-br from-[#E76F51] to-[#F4A261] text-white flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-xs">
                      {c.author[0].toUpperCase()}
                    </div>
                    <div>
                      <div className="font-bold text-sm" style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>@{c.author}</div>
                      <div className="text-[0.6rem] text-[var(--text-muted)]">a publié une création</div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>

                {c.media_url && (
                  <div className="w-full bg-black flex justify-center max-h-[400px] overflow-hidden">
                    {c.type === 'video' ? (
                      <video src={c.media_url} controls className="max-h-[400px] w-full object-contain" />
                    ) : (
                      <img src={c.media_url} alt="création" className="w-full object-cover max-h-[400px]" />
                    )}
                  </div>
                )}

                <div className="p-5">
                  {c.content && <p className="text-sm mb-4" style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>{c.content}</p>}
                  
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-4">
                    <button onClick={() => likeCreation(c.id)} className="flex items-center gap-1 text-sm font-bold text-[#E76F51] hover:scale-105 transition">
                      ❤️ {c.likes_count > 0 ? c.likes_count : 'Aimer'}
                    </button>
                  </div>

                  <button 
                    onClick={() => { setShowCreationsFeed(false); setSelectedSeal({ id: c.seal_id, title: c.seal_title, author: c.author, description: '' }); }}
                    className="w-full text-left bg-[var(--bg-main)] border border-[var(--border-color)] p-3 rounded-xl hover:border-[#E76F51] transition flex items-center justify-between group"
                  >
                    <div>
                      <div className="text-[0.6rem] font-mono font-bold text-[var(--text-muted)] uppercase">Sceau fondateur</div>
                      <div className="font-bold text-sm" style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>{c.seal_title}</div>
                    </div>
                    <span className="text-[#E76F51] group-hover:translate-x-1 transition">→</span>
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    );
  }

  // === FEED NORMAL (CADRANS) ===
  return (
    <main className="min-h-screen p-6 font-sans max-w-3xl mx-auto relative">
      <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
        <h1 className="text-4xl font-bold" style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>SCEAU<span className="text-[#E76F51] italic">.</span></h1>
        <Link href="/seal" className="bg-[#E76F51] text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-lg font-bold text-sm shadow-md">+ Sceller</Link>
      </div>

      <div className="mb-6 relative z-40">
        <input 
          type="text" 
          placeholder="Rechercher une œuvre ou un créateur..." 
          className="w-full border-2 border-[var(--border-color)] p-3 rounded-xl outline-none bg-[var(--bg-surface)]"
          style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}
          value={searchQuery}
          onChange={async (e) => {
            setSearchQuery(e.target.value);
            if (e.target.value.length > 2) {
              const res = await fetch(`/api/search?q=${encodeURIComponent(e.target.value)}`);
              const d = await res.json();
              setSearchResults(d.results || []);
            } else {
              setSearchResults([]);
            }
          }}
        />
        {searchResults.length > 0 && (
          <div className="absolute top-full left-0 right-0 mt-2 bg-[var(--bg-surface)] border border-[var(--border-color)] rounded-xl shadow-xl overflow-hidden max-h-60 overflow-y-auto">
             {searchResults.map(r => (
                <div key={r.id} onClick={() => { setSelectedSeal(r); setSearchResults([]); setSearchQuery(''); }} className="p-3 border-b border-[var(--border-color)] hover:bg-[var(--bg-main)] cursor-pointer">
                   <div className="font-bold text-sm" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>{r.title}</div>
                   <div className="text-xs text-[var(--text-muted)]">Par @{r.author}</div>
                </div>
             ))}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {stories.length > 0 && (
        <div className="flex gap-4 overflow-x-auto pb-4 mb-2 snap-x hide-scrollbar">
          {stories.map((story, index) => (
            <div key={story.id} onClick={() => setActiveStoryIndex(index)} className="snap-start shrink-0 flex flex-col items-center cursor-pointer group">
              <div className="w-16 h-16 rounded-full p-[2px] bg-gradient-to-tr from-[#E76F51] to-[#F4A261]">
                <div className="w-full h-full bg-[var(--bg-surface)] rounded-full border-2 border-[var(--bg-surface)] flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-xl overflow-hidden relative">
                  {story.file_path && story.is_hosted ? <img src={story.file_path} alt="story" className="w-full h-full object-cover" /> : <span className="opacity-70">{story.author[0].toUpperCase()}</span>}
                  <span className="absolute bottom-0 right-0 bg-[#E76F51] text-white text-[0.5rem] px-1 rounded-tl-md">⏳</span>
                </div>
              </div>
              <span className="text-[0.6rem] font-bold mt-1 truncate w-16 text-center" style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>@{story.author}</span>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}

      <div className="flex bg-[var(--bg-surface)] border border-[var(--border-color)] p-1 rounded-xl mb-4">
        <button className="flex-1 py-2 text-sm font-bold rounded-lg bg-[var(--bg-main)] shadow" style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>Cadrans (Œuvres)</button>
        <button onClick={() => setShowCreationsFeed(true)} className="flex-1 py-2 text-sm font-bold rounded-lg opacity-50">Créations (Feed)</button>
      </div>

      <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-4 gap-2">
        <div className="flex gap-2 overflow-x-auto pb-2 snap-x flex-1 hide-scrollbar">
          {['all', 'Art', 'Musique', 'Code', 'Écrit'].map(cat => (
            <button key={cat} onClick={() => setActiveCategory(cat)} className={`snap-start shrink-0 px-4 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold transition border ${activeCategory === cat ? 'bg-[#E76F51] text-white border-[#E76F51] shadow-md' : 'bg-[var(--bg-surface)] text-[var(--text-muted)] border-[var(--border-color)]'}`}>
              {cat === 'all' ? '🌍 Tout' : cat}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
        <button onClick={rollRandom} className="shrink-0 bg-[#2C1810] text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold shadow-md flex items-center gap-1">🎲 Surprise</button>
      </div>

      {loading ? (
         <p className="text-center font-mono py-10">Connexion...</p>
      ) : (
        <div className="grid gap-6 mb-10">
          {seals.filter(s => !s.is_ephemeral).map((seal) => (
            <div key={seal.id} onClick={() => setSelectedSeal(seal)} className="bg-[var(--bg-surface)] p-6 rounded-2xl border border-[var(--border-color)] shadow-sm cursor-pointer hover:shadow-md transition">
              <div className="flex justify-between items-start mb-2">
                <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold" style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>{seal.title}</h2>
              </div>
              <p className="text-sm text-[var(--text-muted)] mb-2">Par @{seal.author} • {seal.category}</p>
              <p className="text-sm text-[var(--text-muted)] italic line-clamp-2">{seal.description}</p>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}

      {/* === LE CADRAN CARRE === */}
      {selectedSeal && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/60 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 backdrop-blur-sm" onClick={() => setSelectedSeal(null)}>
          <div className="bg-[var(--bg-main)] w-full max-w-lg rounded-2xl relative max-h-[90vh] flex flex-col overflow-hidden shadow-2xl border-2 border-[var(--border-color)]" onClick={e => e.stopPropagation()}>
            
            {/* En-tête du Cadran */}
            <div className="p-5 border-b border-[var(--border-color)] flex justify-between items-start bg-[var(--bg-surface)] z-10">
              <div>
                <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold mb-1" style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>{selectedSeal.title}</h2>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                  <p className="text-sm text-[var(--text-muted)]">Par @{selectedSeal.author}</p>
                </div>
              </div>
              <button onClick={() => setSelectedSeal(null)} className="text-2xl opacity-50 hover:opacity-100">✕</button>
            </div>

            {/* Contenu */}
            <div className="p-5 overflow-y-auto flex-1 bg-[var(--bg-main)] space-y-4">
              
              {/* Bloc Description (Carré) */}
              <div className="bg-[var(--bg-surface)] p-4 rounded-xl border border-[var(--border-color)]">
                <h3 className="text-[0.65rem] font-mono font-bold uppercase mb-2 text-[var(--text-muted)] tracking-wider">Description du créateur</h3>
                <p className="text-sm italic" style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>{selectedSeal.description || "Aucune description fournie."}</p>
              </div>

              {/* Bouton d'action (Créations) */}
              <button 
                onClick={() => setShowCreateModal(true)}
                className="w-full flex items-center justify-between p-4 rounded-xl border-2 border-dashed border-[#E76F51] bg-[#FDF4F0] hover:bg-[#FCEAD6] transition group"
              >
                <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                  <span className="text-2xl">🎬</span>
                  <div className="text-left">
                    <div className="font-bold text-[#C44A2E]">
                      {creations.length > 0 ? `${creations.length} création(s) rattachée(s)` : "Aucune création"}
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-xs text-[#C44A2E] opacity-80">
                      {creations.length > 0 ? "Cliquez pour explorer" : "Lancez la vôtre !"}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
                <span className="font-bold text-xl text-[#C44A2E] group-hover:translate-x-1 transition">→</span>
              </button>

              {/* Liste des créations (avec Likes) */}
              <div className="space-y-3 pb-4">
                {creations.map((c, i) => (
                  <div key={i} className="bg-[var(--bg-surface)] p-4 rounded-xl border border-[var(--border-color)]">
                    <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-2">
                      <span className="font-bold text-xs" style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>@{c.author}</span>
                      <span className="text-[0.6rem] bg-[#E76F51] text-white px-2 py-0.5 rounded">{c.type === 'text' ? 'Texte' : c.type}</span>
                    </div>
                    {c.media_url && c.type !== 'text' && (
                      <div className="mb-3 rounded-lg overflow-hidden border border-[var(--border-color)]">
                        {c.type === 'video' ? <video src={c.media_url} controls className="w-full max-h-48 object-cover" /> : <img src={c.media_url} className="w-full max-h-48 object-cover" />}
                      </div>
                    )}
                    <p className="text-sm mb-3" style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>{c.content}</p>
                    <button onClick={() => likeCreation(c.id)} className="flex items-center gap-1 text-xs font-bold text-[#E76F51]">
                      ❤️ {c.likes_count > 0 ? c.likes_count : 'Aimer'}
                    </button>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Barre de commentaire (Fix Bouton) */}
            {myHandle && (
              <div className="p-4 border-t border-[var(--border-color)] bg-[var(--bg-surface)]">
                <div className="flex gap-2 items-center">
                  <input 
                    type="text" 
                    value={newComment} 
                    onChange={e => setNewComment(e.target.value)} 
                    placeholder="Commenter..." 
                    className="flex-1 min-w-0 bg-[var(--bg-main)] border border-[var(--border-color)] rounded-xl px-4 py-2 text-sm outline-none focus:border-[#E76F51]" 
                    style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }} 
                    onKeyDown={e => e.key === 'Enter' && submitComment()}
                  />
                  <button 
                    onClick={submitComment} 
                    disabled={isSubmitting}
                    className="shrink-0 bg-[#E76F51] text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-xl font-bold text-sm disabled:opacity-50"
                  >
                    Publier
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* === MODALE : CRÉER UNE CRÉATION === */}
      {showCreateModal && selectedSeal && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/60 z-[60] flex items-center justify-center p-4 backdrop-blur-sm" onClick={() => setShowCreateModal(false)}>
          <div className="bg-[var(--bg-surface)] w-full max-w-md rounded-3xl p-6 shadow-2xl" onClick={e => e.stopPropagation()}>
            <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
              <h2 className="text-xl font-bold" style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>Nouvelle Création</h2>
              <button onClick={() => setShowCreateModal(false)} className="text-xl opacity-50 hover:opacity-100">✕</button>
            </div>
            
            <textarea 
              className="w-full border-2 border-[var(--border-color)] rounded-xl p-3 outline-none bg-transparent mb-4" 
              rows={3} 
              placeholder="Description de ton média..."
              value={createContent}
              onChange={e => setCreateContent(e.target.value)}
              style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}
            />

            <div className="mb-6">
              <input type="file" accept="image/*,video/*" onChange={e => setCreateFile(e.target.files?.[0] || null)} className="w-full text-sm" />
            </div>

            <button onClick={submitCreation} disabled={isCreating} className="w-full bg-[#E76F51] text-white py-3 rounded-xl font-bold shadow-md">
              {isCreating ? 'Publication...' : 'Publier'}
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

    </main>
  );
}

=========================================
FICHIER : ./src/app/forgot-password/page.tsx
=========================================

'use client';
import { useState } from 'react';

export default function ForgotPassword() {
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [link, setLink] = useState('');
  const [error, setError] = useState('');

  async function handleReset() {
    setLink(''); setError('');
    const res = await fetch('/api/auth/forgot-password', { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify({ email }) });
    const data = await res.json();
    if (res.ok) setLink(data.link);
    else setError(data.error);
  }

  return (
    <div className="max-w-md mx-auto bg-[#FFFCF7] p-8 rounded-3xl border border-[#EFE0CA] shadow-sm mt-10">
      <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold mb-4 font-serif">Mot de passe oublié</h1>
      <p className="text-sm text-[#7A5A3A] mb-6">Entre ton email pour recevoir un lien de réinitialisation.</p>
      
      <input type="email" placeholder="Ton email" className="w-full border-2 border-[#EFE0CA] p-3 rounded-xl mb-4" value={email} onChange={e => setEmail(e.target.value)} />
      <button onClick={handleReset} className="w-full bg-[#E76F51] text-white p-3 rounded-xl font-bold mb-4">Envoyer le lien</button>
      
      {link && (
        <div className="bg-[#F5F9EE] border border-[#7A9B5A] p-4 rounded-xl text-sm break-all">
          <p className="font-bold text-[#5D7A3A] mb-2">✅ (Mode Test) Voici ton lien :</p>
          <a href={link} className="text-blue-600 underline">{link}</a>
        </div>
      )}
      {error && <p className="text-red-500 font-bold text-center">{error}</p>}
    </div>
  );
}

=========================================
FICHIER : ./src/app/friends/page.tsx
=========================================

'use client';
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import Link from 'next/link';

export default function Friends() {
  // État pour basculer entre "Amis" et "Défis"
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<'network' | 'challenges'>('network');

  // États pour les Amis
  const [friendsData, setFriendsData] = useState<any>({ friends: [], received: [] });
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');
  const [searchResults, setSearchResults] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [friendsLoading, setFriendsLoading] = useState(true);
  const [friendsError, setFriendsError] = useState('');
  
  // États pour les Défis
  const [challenges, setChallenges] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [challengesLoading, setChallengesLoading] = useState(true);
  const [showChallengeModal, setShowChallengeModal] = useState(false);
  const [challengeTitle, setChallengeTitle] = useState('');
  const [challengeDesc, setChallengeDesc] = useState('');
  const [challengeAnon, setChallengeAnon] = useState(false);

  const [myHandle, setMyHandle] = useState<string | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    const h = localStorage.getItem('sceau_handle');
    setMyHandle(h ? h.replace('@', '') : null);
  }, []);

  // --- LOGIQUE AMIS ---
  const loadFriends = () => {
    if (!myHandle) return;
    setFriendsLoading(true);
    fetch(`/api/friends?me=${myHandle}`)
      .then(async (r) => {
        const d = await r.json();
        if (!r.ok) throw new Error(d.error || 'Erreur de chargement');
        return d;
      })
      .then(d => { 
        setFriendsData({ friends: d.friends || [], received: d.received || [] }); 
        setFriendsLoading(false);
      })
      .catch((err) => { 
        setFriendsError(err.message); 
        setFriendsLoading(false); 
      });
  };

  useEffect(() => { 
    if (myHandle && activeTab === 'network') loadFriends(); 
  }, [myHandle, activeTab]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!searchQuery.trim() || !myHandle) { setSearchResults([]); return; }
    const delay = setTimeout(() => {
      fetch(`/api/friends?me=${myHandle}&search=${searchQuery}`)
        .then(r => r.json())
        .then(d => setSearchResults(d.searchResults || []))
        .catch(() => {});
    }, 500);
    return () => clearTimeout(delay);
  }, [searchQuery, myHandle]);

  async function respond(target: string, action: string) {
    await fetch('/api/friends/respond', { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify({ me: myHandle, target, action }) });
    loadFriends();
  }

  async function addFriend(target: string) {
    await fetch('/api/friends', { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify({ me: myHandle, target }) });
    alert(`Demande envoyée à @${target}`);
    setSearchQuery('');
  }

  // --- LOGIQUE DÉFIS ---
  const loadChallenges = () => {
    setChallengesLoading(true);
    fetch('/api/challenges/list')
      .then(r => r.json())
      .then(d => {
        setChallenges(d.challenges || []);
        setChallengesLoading(false);
      })
      .catch(() => setChallengesLoading(false));
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    if (activeTab === 'challenges') loadChallenges();
  }, [activeTab]);

  async function createChallenge() {
    const pubkey = localStorage.getItem('sceau_pubkey');
    if (!pubkey) return alert("Connecte-toi !");
    if (!challengeTitle) return alert("Titre requis");

    const res = await fetch('/api/challenges/create', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ title: challengeTitle, description: challengeDesc, pubkey, is_anonymous: challengeAnon })
    });
    if (res.ok) {
      setShowChallengeModal(false); 
      setChallengeTitle(''); 
      setChallengeDesc(''); 
      loadChallenges();
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="p-6 max-w-2xl mx-auto font-serif min-h-screen">
      
      {/* EN-TÊTE ET BOUTONS DE NAVIGATION */}
      <div className="mb-6">
        <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold mb-4" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>Communauté.</h1>
        
        <div className="flex bg-[var(--bg-surface)] border border-[var(--border-color)] p-1 rounded-xl">
          <button 
            onClick={() => setActiveTab('network')} 
            className={`flex-1 py-2 text-sm font-bold rounded-lg transition ${activeTab === 'network' ? 'bg-[#F0F6FC] text-[#2D6A9E] shadow' : 'opacity-50'}`}
          >
            👥 Le Réseau
          </button>
          <button 
            onClick={() => setActiveTab('challenges')} 
            className={`flex-1 py-2 text-sm font-bold rounded-lg transition ${activeTab === 'challenges' ? 'bg-[#FCEAD6] text-[#C44A2E] shadow' : 'opacity-50'}`}
          >
            🏆 Les Défis
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* ========================================== */}
      {/* CONTENU : RÉSEAU (AMIS) */}
      {/* ========================================== */}
      {activeTab === 'network' && (
        <div className="animate-fade-in">
          {friendsError ? (
            <div className="text-center p-10 font-mono">
              <p className="text-red-500 font-bold mb-4">❌ {friendsError}</p>
              <button onClick={() => { localStorage.clear(); window.location.href = '/login'; }} className="bg-[#2C1810] text-white px-6 py-2 rounded-xl font-bold">Se reconnecter</button>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <>
              <div className="mb-8 relative">
                <input 
                  type="text" 
                  placeholder="🔍 Chercher un créateur par pseudo..." 
                  className="w-full border-2 border-[var(--border-color)] p-3 rounded-xl outline-none bg-[var(--bg-surface)]"
                  style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}
                  value={searchQuery}
                  onChange={e => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
                />
                {searchResults.length > 0 && (
                  <div className="mt-2 bg-[var(--bg-surface)] border border-[var(--border-color)] rounded-xl overflow-hidden shadow-lg absolute w-full z-10">
                    {searchResults.map(u => (
                      <div key={u.handle} className="flex justify-between items-center p-3 border-b border-[var(--border-color)] hover:bg-[var(--bg-main)]">
                        <div>
                          <Link href={`/u/${u.handle}`} className="font-bold hover:underline" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>@{u.handle}</Link>
                          <span className="text-xs text-[var(--text-muted)] ml-2">{u.role}</span>
                        </div>
                        <button onClick={() => addFriend(u.handle)} className="bg-[#E76F51] text-white px-3 py-1 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold">+ Ajouter</button>
                      </div>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>

              {friendsData.received.length > 0 && (
                <div className="mb-8">
                  <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-[#E76F51] mb-3">Demandes en attente ({friendsData.received.length})</h2>
                  <div className="space-y-2">
                    {friendsData.received.map((r: any) => (
                      <div key={r.handle} className="bg-[#FDF4F0] p-3 rounded-xl border border-[#FCE0D6] flex justify-between items-center">
                        <Link href={`/u/${r.handle}`} className="font-bold hover:underline" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>@{r.handle}</Link>
                        <div className="flex gap-2">
                          <button onClick={() => respond(r.handle, 'accept')} className="bg-green-500 text-white px-3 py-1 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold">Accepter</button>
                          <button onClick={() => respond(r.handle, 'reject')} className="bg-red-500 text-white px-3 py-1 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold">Refuser</button>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}

              <h2 className="text-lg font-bold mb-3">Mes Amis ({friendsData.friends.length})</h2>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                {friendsData.friends.map((f: any) => (
                  <div key={f.handle} className="bg-[var(--bg-surface)] p-4 rounded-xl border border-[var(--border-color)] shadow-sm">
                    <div className="flex justify-between items-start mb-2">
                      <Link href={`/u/${f.handle}`} className="font-bold hover:underline" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>@{f.handle}</Link>
                      <span className="text-xs bg-[#F5F9EE] text-[#5D7A3A] px-2 py-1 rounded">Ami</span>
                    </div>
                    {f.last_seal ? (
                      <Link href={`/o/${f.last_seal_id}`} className="block text-xs text-[var(--text-muted)] bg-[var(--bg-main)] p-2 rounded border border-[var(--border-color)] hover:border-[#E76F51] transition">
                        Dernière œuvre : <span className="font-bold" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>"{f.last_seal}"</span>
                      </Link>
                    ) : (
                      <p className="text-xs text-[var(--text-muted)] italic">Aucune œuvre publique récente.</p>
                    )}
                  </div>
                ))}
                {friendsData.friends.length === 0 && <p className="text-sm text-[var(--text-muted)] italic col-span-2 text-center py-4">Ta communauté est vide. Cherche des créateurs ci-dessus !</p>}
              </div>
            </>
          )}
        </div>
      )}

      {/* ========================================== */}
      {/* CONTENU : DÉFIS */}
      {/* ========================================== */}
      {activeTab === 'challenges' && (
        <div className="animate-fade-in">
          <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-6">
            <p className="text-sm text-[var(--text-muted)] italic">Lancez des duels de créativité, votez pour le meilleur.</p>
            <button onClick={() => setShowChallengeModal(true)} className="bg-[#E76F51] text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-lg font-bold text-sm shadow-md whitespace-nowrap">+ Créer un défi</button>
          </div>

          {challengesLoading ? <p className="text-center font-mono">Chargement...</p> : (
            <div className="grid gap-4">
              {challenges.map(c => (
                <Link href={`/challenges/${c.id}`} key={c.id} className="bg-[var(--bg-surface)] p-6 rounded-2xl border border-[var(--border-color)] shadow-sm hover:shadow-md transition block">
                  <div className="flex justify-between items-start mb-2">
                    <h2 className="text-xl font-bold" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>{c.title}</h2>
                    {c.is_anonymous && <span className="text-xs bg-gray-200 text-gray-700 px-2 py-1 rounded">Vote à l'aveugle</span>}
                  </div>
                  <p className="text-sm text-[var(--text-muted)] mb-4">Lancé par @{c.creator}</p>
                  <div className="flex justify-between items-center text-xs font-mono text-[#A89070]">
                    <span>👥 {c.entries_count} participations</span>
                    <span>⏱ Fin le {new Date(c.deadline).toLocaleDateString('fr-FR')}</span>
                  </div>
                </Link>
              ))}
              {challenges.length === 0 && <p className="text-center italic text-[var(--text-muted)]">Aucun défi en cours. Sois le premier à en lancer un !</p>}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      )}

      {/* MODALE DE CRÉATION DE DÉFI */}
      {showChallengeModal && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/60 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 backdrop-blur-sm">
          <div className="bg-[var(--bg-surface)] w-full max-w-md rounded-3xl p-6">
            <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold mb-4" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>Créer un Défi</h2>
            <input type="text" placeholder="Titre (ex: Meilleur design de l'été)" className="w-full border-2 border-[var(--border-color)] p-3 rounded-xl mb-4 bg-transparent" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}} value={challengeTitle} onChange={e => setChallengeTitle(e.target.value)} />
            <textarea placeholder="Description du défi..." className="w-full border-2 border-[var(--border-color)] p-3 rounded-xl mb-4 bg-transparent" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}} value={challengeDesc} onChange={e => setChallengeDesc(e.target.value)} />
            <label className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-6 cursor-pointer">
              <input type="checkbox" checked={challengeAnon} onChange={e => setChallengeAnon(e.target.checked)} className="w-4 h-4 accent-[#E76F51]" />
              <span className="text-sm font-bold" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>Cacher les noms (Vote à l'aveugle)</span>
            </label>
            <div className="flex gap-2">
              <button onClick={() => setShowChallengeModal(false)} className="flex-1 py-2 font-bold opacity-50">Annuler</button>
              <button onClick={createChallenge} className="flex-1 bg-[#E76F51] text-white py-2 rounded-xl font-bold">Lancer</button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

    </div>
  );
}

'use client';
import './globals.css'
import Link from 'next/link'
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import { usePathname } from 'next/navigation'
import Manifeste from '@/components/Manifeste'

export default function RootLayout({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  const pathname = usePathname();
  const [handle, setHandle] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [isDark, setIsDark] = useState(false);
  const [notifications, setNotifications] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [unread, setUnread] = useState(0);
  const [showNotifs, setShowNotifs] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    setHandle(localStorage.getItem('sceau_handle'));
    const theme = localStorage.getItem('sceau_theme');
    if (theme === 'dark') { setIsDark(true); document.documentElement.classList.add('dark'); }
  }, [pathname]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!handle) return;
    const fetchNotifs = () => {
      fetch(`/api/notifications?me=${handle}`).then(r => r.json()).then(d => {
        setNotifications(d.notifications || []);
        setUnread(d.unread || 0);
      });
    };
    fetchNotifs();
    const interval = setInterval(fetchNotifs, 10000);
    return () => clearInterval(interval);
  }, [handle]);

  const toggleTheme = () => {
    const next = !isDark; setIsDark(next);
    if (next) { document.documentElement.classList.add('dark'); localStorage.setItem('sceau_theme', 'dark'); }
    else { document.documentElement.classList.remove('dark'); localStorage.setItem('sceau_theme', 'light'); }
  };

  const markAsRead = () => {
    if (unread > 0 && handle) {
      fetch('/api/notifications/read', { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify({ me: handle }) });
      setUnread(0);
    }
  };

  const isActive = (path: string) => pathname === path;
  const isToolsActive = pathname === '/seal' || pathname === '/verify';
  const showManifeste = ['/profil', '/seal', '/verify', '/settings'].includes(pathname);

  return (
    <html lang="fr">
        <link rel="manifest" href="/manifest.json" />
        <meta name="theme-color" content="#E76F51" />
      <head>
        <link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.googleapis.com" />
        <link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.gstatic.com" crossOrigin="anonymous" />
        <link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Instrument+Serif:ital@0;1&family=Outfit:wght@300;400;500;600;700&family=JetBrains+Mono:wght@400;500&display=swap" rel="stylesheet" />
      </head>
      <body className="min-h-screen pb-24 font-sans">
        <nav className="bg-[var(--bg-surface)] border-b border-[var(--border-color)] px-6 py-4 flex justify-between items-center sticky top-0 z-50">
          <Link href="/feed" className="text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight font-serif" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>SCEAU<span className="text-[#E76F51]">.</span></Link>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
            <button onClick={toggleTheme} className="text-xl cursor-pointer">{isDark ? '☀️' : '🌙'}</button>
            
            {handle && (
              <div className="relative">
                <button onClick={() => { setShowNotifs(!showNotifs); if (!showNotifs) markAsRead(); }} className="text-xl relative">
                  🔔
                  {unread > 0 && <span className="absolute -top-1 -right-1 bg-red-500 text-white text-[0.6rem] font-bold w-4 h-4 flex items-center justify-center rounded-full">{unread}</span>}
                </button>
                
                {showNotifs && (
                  <div className="absolute right-0 mt-4 w-72 bg-[var(--bg-surface)] border border-[var(--border-color)] rounded-2xl shadow-xl overflow-hidden z-50">
                    <div className="p-3 border-b border-[var(--border-color)] font-bold text-sm">Notifications</div>
                    <div className="max-h-64 overflow-y-auto">
                      {notifications.length === 0 ? <p className="p-4 text-center text-xs opacity-50">Aucune notification.</p> : 
                        notifications.map((n: any) => (
                          <Link key={n.id} href={n.link} onClick={() => setShowNotifs(false)} className={`block p-3 border-b border-[var(--border-color)] hover:bg-[var(--bg-main)] transition ${!n.is_read ? 'bg-[#FDF4F0]' : ''}`}>
                            <p className="text-xs" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}><span className="font-bold text-[#E76F51]">@{n.actor}</span> {n.message}</p>
                            <span className="text-[0.6rem] font-mono text-[var(--text-muted)]">{new Date(n.created_at).toLocaleTimeString('fr-FR')}</span>
                          </Link>
                        ))
                      }
                    </div>
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            )}

            {!handle ? (
              <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                <Link href="/pricing" className="text-sm font-bold text-[var(--text-muted)] hover:text-[var(--text-main)] transition hidden sm:block">Tarifs</Link>
                <Link href="/signup" className="text-sm font-bold bg-[#E76F51] text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-lg">Créer un compte</Link>
              </div>
            ) : (
              <Link href="/profil" className="text-sm font-bold" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>@{handle}</Link>
            )}
          </div>
        </nav>
        
        <main className="max-w-2xl mx-auto">{children}</main>
        {showManifeste && <Manifeste />}

        <div className="fixed bottom-0 left-0 right-0 bg-[var(--bg-surface)] border-t border-[var(--border-color)] flex justify-around py-3 z-50">
          <Link href="/feed" className={`flex flex-col items-center transition ${isActive('/feed') ? 'text-[#E76F51]' : 'text-[var(--text-muted)] hover:text-[#E76F51]'}`}>
            <span className="text-xl">🌐</span><span className="text-[0.6rem] font-bold font-mono mt-1">Feed</span>
          </Link>
          <Link href="/friends" className={`flex flex-col items-center transition ${isActive('/friends') ? 'text-[#E76F51]' : 'text-[var(--text-muted)] hover:text-[#E76F51]'}`}>
            <span className="text-xl">👥</span><span className="text-[0.6rem] font-bold font-mono mt-1">Communauté</span>
          </Link>
          <Link href="/seal" className={`flex flex-col items-center transition ${isToolsActive ? 'text-[#E76F51]' : 'text-[var(--text-muted)] hover:text-[#E76F51]'}`}>
            <span className="text-xl">🛠️</span><span className="text-[0.6rem] font-bold font-mono mt-1">Outils</span>
          </Link>
          <Link href="/messages" className={`flex flex-col items-center transition ${isActive('/messages') ? 'text-[#E76F51]' : 'text-[var(--text-muted)] hover:text-[#E76F51]'}`}>
            <span className="text-xl">💬</span><span className="text-[0.6rem] font-bold font-mono mt-1">Messages</span>
          </Link>
          <Link href="/profil" className={`flex flex-col items-center transition ${isActive('/profil') ? 'text-[#E76F51]' : 'text-[var(--text-muted)] hover:text-[#E76F51]'}`}>
            <span className="text-xl">👤</span><span className="text-[0.6rem] font-bold font-mono mt-1">Profil</span>
          </Link>
        </div>
      </body>
    </html>
  )
}

FICHIER : ./src/app/login/page.tsx
=========================================

'use client';
import { useState } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import Link from 'next/link';

export default function Login() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  const [showPassword, setShowPassword] = useState(false);
  const [status, setStatus] = useState('');

  async function handleLogin() {
    if (!email || !password) return setStatus("Email et mot de passe requis");
    setStatus("Connexion...");
    
    const res = await fetch('/api/auth/login', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ email, password }) });
    const data = await res.json();
    
    if (res.ok) {
      localStorage.setItem('sceau_pubkey', data.pubkey);
      localStorage.setItem('sceau_handle', data.handle);
      setStatus("✅ Connecté !");
      setTimeout(() => router.push('/feed'), 500);
    } else {
      setStatus("❌ " + data.error);
    }
  }

  // La fonction magique pour "Entrée"
  const handleKeyPress = (e: React.KeyboardEvent) => { if (e.key === 'Enter') handleLogin(); };

  return (
    <div className="max-w-md mx-auto bg-[var(--bg-surface)] p-8 rounded-3xl border border-[var(--border-color)] shadow-sm mt-10">
      <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold mb-6 font-serif" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>Se connecter</h1>
      
      <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-[var(--text-muted)] mb-1 font-mono">Email</label>
      <input type="email" onKeyDown={handleKeyPress} className="w-full border-2 border-[var(--border-color)] p-3 rounded-xl mb-4 bg-transparent" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}} value={email} onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)} />
      
      <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-[var(--text-muted)] mb-1 font-mono">Mot de passe</label>
      <div className="relative mb-2">
        <input type={showPassword ? "text" : "password"} onKeyDown={handleKeyPress} className="w-full border-2 border-[var(--border-color)] p-3 rounded-xl pr-10 bg-transparent" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}} value={password} onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)} />
        <button type="button" onClick={() => setShowPassword(!showPassword)} className="absolute right-3 top-3 opacity-50">{showPassword ? "🙈" : "👁️"}</button>
      </div>
      
      <div className="text-right mb-6">
        <Link href="/forgot-password" className="text-sm text-[#E76F51] hover:underline font-bold">Mot de passe oublié ?</Link>
      </div>
      
      <button onClick={handleLogin} className="w-full bg-[var(--text-main)] text-[var(--bg-surface)] p-4 rounded-xl font-bold">Connexion</button>
      {status && <p className="mt-4 text-center font-bold text-[#E76F51]">{status}</p>}
      
      <p className="text-center mt-4 text-sm text-[var(--text-muted)]">Pas de compte ? <Link href="/signup" className="underline font-bold">Créer un compte</Link></p>
    </div>
  );
}

=========================================
FICHIER : ./src/app/messages/page.tsx
=========================================

'use client';
import { useEffect, useState, useRef, Suspense } from 'react';
import { useRouter, useSearchParams } from 'next/navigation';
import { sha256 } from '@noble/hashes/sha256';
import { generatePactCertificate } from '@/lib/pdf';
import QRCode from 'qrcode';

function MessagesInner() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const searchParams = useSearchParams();
  const targetUser = searchParams.get('user');

  const [myHandle, setMyHandle] = useState('');
  const [myPubkey, setMyPubkey] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [contacts, setContacts] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [friendHandles, setFriendHandles] = useState<Set<string>>(new Set());
  const [groups, setGroups] = useState<any[]>([]);

  const [activeChat, setActiveChat] = useState<any>(null);
  const [messages, setMessages] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [newMessage, setNewMessage] = useState('');

  const [showGroupModal, setShowGroupModal] = useState(false);
  const [groupName, setGroupName] = useState('');
  const [activePact, setActivePact] = useState<any>(null);
  const [isSealingChat, setIsSealingChat] = useState(false);
  const [showSharedVault, setShowSharedVault] = useState(false);

  // Audio
  const [isRecording, setIsRecording] = useState(false);
  const [recordingTime, setRecordingTime] = useState(0);
  const mediaRecorderRef = useRef<MediaRecorder | null>(null);
  const audioChunksRef = useRef<Blob[]>([]);
  const recordingIntervalRef = useRef<any>(null);

  const messagesEndRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const prevMessagesLength = useRef(0);

  useEffect(() => {
    const handle = localStorage.getItem('sceau_handle') || '';
    const pubkey = localStorage.getItem('sceau_pubkey');
    setMyHandle(handle);
    setMyPubkey(pubkey);

    if (handle) {
      fetch(`/api/messages?me=${handle}`).then(r => r.json()).then(d => setContacts(d.contacts || []));
      fetch(`/api/groups/list?me=${handle}`).then(r => r.json()).then(d => setGroups(d.groups || []));
      fetch(`/api/friends?me=${handle}`).then(r => r.json()).then(d => {
        const handles = new Set<string>((d.friends || []).map((f: any) => f.handle));
        setFriendHandles(handles);
      }).catch(() => {});
    }

    if (targetUser) setActiveChat({ type: 'dm', id: targetUser, name: targetUser });
  }, [targetUser]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!activeChat || !myHandle) return;
    const load = () => {
      const endpoint = activeChat.type === 'dm'
        ? `/api/messages/${activeChat.id}?me=${myHandle}`
        : `/api/groups/messages?id=${activeChat.id}`;

      fetch(endpoint).then(r => r.json()).then(data => {
        const serverMessages = data.messages || [];
        setMessages(prev => {
          const tempMessages = prev.filter(m => m.isTemp);
          const merged = [...serverMessages];
          tempMessages.forEach(temp => {
            if (!serverMessages.some((sm: any) => sm.content === temp.content && sm.sender === temp.sender)) merged.push(temp);
          });
          return merged;
        });
      });
    };
    load();
    const interval = setInterval(load, 3000);
    return () => clearInterval(interval);
  }, [activeChat, myHandle]);

  useEffect(() => { prevMessagesLength.current = 0; }, [activeChat]);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (messages.length > prevMessagesLength.current) {
      messagesEndRef.current?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' });
      prevMessagesLength.current = messages.length;
    }
  }, [messages]);

  async function sendMessage() {
    if (!newMessage.trim() || !activeChat) return;
    const msg = newMessage;
    setNewMessage('');
    const tempId = `temp-${Date.now()}`;
    setMessages(prev => [...prev, { content: msg, sender: myHandle, created_at: new Date().toISOString(), isTemp: true, id: tempId }]);

    try {
      if (activeChat.type === 'dm') {
        await fetch('/api/messages', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ sender_handle: myHandle, receiver_handle: activeChat.id, content: msg }) });
      } else {
        await fetch('/api/groups/messages', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ group_id: activeChat.id, sender_handle: myHandle, content: msg }) });
      }
      setTimeout(() => setMessages(prev => prev.filter(m => m.id !== tempId)), 500);
    } catch (e) { console.error(e); }
  }

  async function startRecording() {
    try {
      const stream = await navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({ audio: true });
      const mediaRecorder = new MediaRecorder(stream);
      mediaRecorderRef.current = mediaRecorder;
      audioChunksRef.current = [];
      setRecordingTime(0);
      recordingIntervalRef.current = setInterval(() => setRecordingTime(t => t + 1), 1000);

      mediaRecorder.ondataavailable = (event) => {
        if (event.data.size > 0) audioChunksRef.current.push(event.data);
      };

      mediaRecorder.onstop = async () => {
        clearInterval(recordingIntervalRef.current);
        const audioBlob = new Blob(audioChunksRef.current, { type: 'audio/webm' });
        if (audioBlob.size > 2 * 1024 * 1024) return alert("Audio trop lourd (max 2 Mo).");
        if (!myPubkey) return;

        const formData = new FormData();
        formData.append('pubkey', myPubkey);
        formData.append('audio', new File([audioBlob], `voice-${Date.now()}.webm`, { type: 'audio/webm' }));

        const res = await fetch('/api/messages/upload-audio', { method: 'POST', body: formData });
        const data = await res.json();
        if (!res.ok || !data.url) return alert('Erreur upload audio');

        const msg = `[AUDIO]${data.url}`;
        if (activeChat.type === 'dm') {
          await fetch('/api/messages', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ sender_handle: myHandle, receiver_handle: activeChat.id, content: msg, is_audio: true }) });
        } else {
          await fetch('/api/groups/messages', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ group_id: activeChat.id, sender_handle: myHandle, content: msg, is_audio: true }) });
        }
      };

      mediaRecorder.start();
      setIsRecording(true);
    } catch (err) { alert("Impossible d'accéder au micro."); }
  }

  function stopRecording() {
    if (mediaRecorderRef.current && isRecording) {
      mediaRecorderRef.current.stop();
      mediaRecorderRef.current.stream.getTracks().forEach(track => track.stop());
      setIsRecording(false);
      clearInterval(recordingIntervalRef.current);
    }
  }

  async function sealDiscussion() {
    if (!messages.length || !myPubkey) return;
    const confirm = window.confirm(`Sceller les ${Math.min(messages.length, 50)} derniers messages dans ton Coffre Privé ?`);
    if (!confirm) return;

    setIsSealingChat(true);
    const lastMessages = messages.slice(-50);
    const textToSeal = lastMessages.map(m => `[@${m.sender} - ${new Date(m.created_at).toLocaleString()}]\n${m.content.startsWith('[AUDIO]') ? 'Message vocal' : m.content}`).join('\n\n');

    const hash = sha256(new TextEncoder().encode(textToSeal));
    let hashStr = ''; for (let i = 0; i < hash.length; i++) hashStr += String.fromCharCode(hash[i]);

    const formData = new FormData();
    formData.append('hash', btoa(hashStr));
    formData.append('title', `Discussion avec ${activeChat.name} — ${new Date().toLocaleDateString('fr-FR')}`);
    formData.append('description', textToSeal);
    formData.append('pubkey', myPubkey);
    formData.append('visibility', 'private');
    formData.append('isHosted', 'false');
    formData.append('isFlash', 'false');
    formData.append('isMystery', 'false');
    formData.append('isEphemeral', 'false');

    const res = await fetch('/api/seal', { method: 'POST', body: formData });
    if (res.ok) alert("✅ Discussion scellée ! Retrouve-la dans ton Coffre Privé.");
    else alert("Erreur lors du scellement.");
    setIsSealingChat(false);
  }

  async function createGroup() {
    if (!groupName || !myPubkey) return;
    const res = await fetch('/api/groups/create', { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify({ name: groupName, creator_pubkey: myPubkey }) });
    if (res.ok) { alert("Groupe créé !"); setShowGroupModal(false); window.location.reload(); }
  }

  const startPact = () => router.push(`/seal?pact=true&chat_id=${activeChat.id}&chat_type=${activeChat.type}`);

  const getHandleColor = (handle: string) => {
    if (handle === myHandle) return 'var(--text-main)';
    return friendHandles.has(handle) ? '#4A90C2' : '#C75A22'; // Amis = Bleu, Inconnus = Orange
  };

  const sharedPacts = Array.from(
    new Map(
      messages
        .filter(m => m.content.startsWith('[PACTE]'))
        .map(m => {
          const [id, title] = m.content.replace('[PACTE]', '').split('|');
          return [id, { id, title }];
        })
    ).values()
  );

  const activeContacts = contacts.filter(c => c.has_chat);
  const suggestions = contacts.filter(c => !c.has_chat);

  return (
    <div className="p-4 md:p-6 h-[calc(100vh-140px)] flex gap-4 font-serif max-w-5xl mx-auto relative">

      {/* LISTE */}
      <div className={`bg-[var(--bg-surface)] border border-[var(--border-color)] rounded-2xl p-4 overflow-y-auto ${activeChat ? 'hidden md:block md:w-1/3' : 'w-full md:w-1/3'}`}>
        <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
          <h1 className="text-xl font-bold" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>Messages</h1>
          <button onClick={() => setShowGroupModal(true)} className="bg-[#E76F51] text-white text-xs font-bold px-2 py-1 rounded-lg">+ Groupe</button>
        </div>

        {groups.length > 0 && (
          <div className="mb-4">
            <h2 className="text-xs font-mono font-bold text-[#A89070] mb-2">Groupes</h2>
            {groups.map(g => (
              <div key={g.id} onClick={() => setActiveChat({ type: 'group', id: g.id, name: g.name })} className="p-2 rounded-xl cursor-pointer hover:bg-[var(--bg-main)] transition flex items-center gap-2 mb-1">
                <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg bg-[#2C1810] text-white flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-sm">G</div>
                <div className="font-bold text-sm truncate text-[#2C1810]">{g.name}</div>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        )}

        <h2 className="text-xs font-mono font-bold text-[#A89070] mb-2">Discussions</h2>
        <div className="text-[0.55rem] text-[var(--text-muted)] italic mb-2">🔵 Amis · 🟠 Inconnus</div>
        {activeContacts.map(c => {
          const isFriend = friendHandles.has(c.handle);
          return (
            <div key={c.id} onClick={() => setActiveChat({ type: 'dm', id: c.handle, name: c.handle })} className="p-2 rounded-xl cursor-pointer hover:bg-[var(--bg-main)] transition flex items-center gap-2 mb-1">
              <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-sm text-white" style={{ background: isFriend ? '#4A90C2' : '#C75A22' }}>
                {c.handle[0].toUpperCase()}
              </div>
              <div className="font-bold text-sm" style={{color: isFriend ? '#4A90C2' : '#C75A22'}}>@{c.handle}</div>
            </div>
          );
        })}
        {activeContacts.length === 0 && <p className="text-xs text-[var(--text-muted)] italic mb-4">Aucune discussion active.</p>}

        <div className="mt-6 border-t border-[var(--border-color)] pt-4">
          <h2 className="text-xs font-mono font-bold text-[#A89070] mb-2">Suggestions</h2>
          {suggestions.length > 0 ? (
            suggestions.map(c => (
              <div key={c.id} onClick={() => setActiveChat({ type: 'dm', id: c.handle, name: c.handle })} className="p-2 rounded-xl cursor-pointer hover:bg-[var(--bg-main)] transition flex items-center gap-2 mb-1 opacity-70 hover:opacity-100">
                <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-sm text-[#A89070] bg-[var(--bg-main)] border border-[var(--border-color)]">
                  {c.handle[0].toUpperCase()}
                </div>
                <div className="font-bold text-sm text-[var(--text-muted)]">@{c.handle}</div>
              </div>
            ))
          ) : (
            <p className="text-xs text-[var(--text-muted)] italic">Aucune suggestion pour le moment.</p>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* CHAT */}
      {activeChat ? (
        <div className={`bg-[var(--bg-surface)] border border-[var(--border-color)] rounded-2xl flex flex-col overflow-hidden w-full md:w-2/3 relative`}>
          <div className="p-4 border-b border-[var(--border-color)] flex justify-between items-center flex-wrap gap-2">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
              <button onClick={() => setActiveChat(null)} className="md:hidden text-xl font-bold" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>←</button>
              <h2 className="text-lg font-bold" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>
                {activeChat.type === 'group' ? `👥 ${activeChat.name}` : `@${activeChat.name}`}
              </h2>
            </div>
            <div className="flex gap-2">
              <button onClick={() => setShowSharedVault(true)} className="bg-[#FDF8F0] text-[#A88620] border border-[#E9C46A] text-xs font-bold px-3 py-1 rounded-lg">📂 Coffre</button>
              <button onClick={sealDiscussion} disabled={isSealingChat} className="bg-[#2C1810] text-white text-xs font-bold px-3 py-1 rounded-lg disabled:opacity-50">{isSealingChat ? '⏳...' : '🔒 Sceller'}</button>
              <button onClick={startPact} className="bg-[#4A90C2] text-white text-xs font-bold px-3 py-1 rounded-lg">+ Pacte</button>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="flex-1 p-4 overflow-y-auto space-y-3">
            {messages.map((m, i) => {
              if (m.content.startsWith('[PACTE]')) {
                const [id, title] = m.content.replace('[PACTE]', '').split('|');
                return (
                  <div key={i} className="bg-[var(--bg-main)] border-2 border-[#4A90C2] p-4 rounded-xl text-center my-4">
                    <div className="font-bold text-lg mb-2" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>🤝 {title}</div>
                    <button onClick={() => setActivePact(id)} className="bg-[#4A90C2] text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold">Voir / Signer</button>
                  </div>
                );
              }

              const isAudio = m.content.startsWith('[AUDIO]');
              const audioSrc = isAudio ? m.content.replace('[AUDIO]', '') : null;

              return (
                <div key={i} className={`max-w-[80%] p-3 rounded-xl text-sm ${m.sender === myHandle ? 'bg-[#E76F51] text-white ml-auto rounded-br-none' : 'bg-[var(--bg-main)] border border-[var(--border-color)] rounded-bl-none'} ${m.isTemp ? 'opacity-60' : ''}`}>
                  {activeChat.type === 'group' && m.sender !== myHandle && (
                    <div className="text-[0.6rem] font-bold mb-1" style={{ color: getHandleColor(m.sender) }}>@{m.sender}</div>
                  )}
                  {isAudio ? <audio controls src={audioSrc!} className="h-10 w-56" /> : m.content}
                </div>
              );
            })}
            <div ref={messagesEndRef} />
          </div>

          <div className="p-4 border-t border-[var(--border-color)] flex gap-2 items-center">
            <button onClick={isRecording ? stopRecording : startRecording} className={`p-2 rounded-xl font-bold text-xl ${isRecording ? 'bg-red-500 text-white animate-pulse' : 'bg-[var(--bg-main)] text-[var(--text-main)]'}`}>
              {isRecording ? `${recordingTime}s ⏹` : '🎙️'}
            </button>
            <input type="text" value={newMessage} onChange={e => setNewMessage(e.target.value)} placeholder="Écrire..." className="flex-1 bg-[var(--bg-main)] border border-[var(--border-color)] rounded-xl px-4 py-2 text-sm outline-none" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}} onKeyDown={e => e.key === 'Enter' && sendMessage()} />
            <button onClick={sendMessage} className="bg-[#E76F51] text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-xl font-bold">→</button>
          </div>

          {showSharedVault && (
            <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-[var(--bg-surface)] z-20 flex flex-col rounded-2xl">
              <div className="p-4 border-b border-[var(--border-color)] flex justify-between items-center">
                <h3 className="font-bold text-lg text-[#A88620]">📂 Coffre Partagé</h3>
                <button onClick={() => setShowSharedVault(false)} className="text-xl opacity-50">✕</button>
              </div>
              <div className="flex-1 p-4 overflow-y-auto">
                <p className="text-xs text-[var(--text-muted)] italic mb-4">Retrouvez ici tous les pactes et accords échangés dans cette discussion.</p>
                {sharedPacts.length === 0 ? (
                  <p className="text-center text-sm text-[var(--text-muted)] py-10">Aucun pacte pour le moment.</p>
                ) : (
                  <div className="space-y-3">
                    {sharedPacts.map(p => (
                      <div key={p.id} onClick={() => { setShowSharedVault(false); setActivePact(p.id); }} className="bg-[#FDF8F0] border border-[#E9C46A] p-4 rounded-xl cursor-pointer hover:shadow-md transition">
                        <div className="font-bold" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>🤝 {p.title}</div>
                        <div className="text-xs text-[#A88620] mt-1">Cliquez pour voir les détails et signer</div>
                      </div>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="hidden md:flex flex-1 bg-[var(--bg-surface)] border border-[var(--border-color)] rounded-2xl items-center justify-center text-[var(--text-muted)]">
          Sélectionnez une discussion.
        </div>
      )}

      {showGroupModal && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/60 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 backdrop-blur-sm">
          <div className="bg-[var(--bg-surface)] w-full max-w-sm rounded-3xl p-6">
            <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold mb-4" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>Créer un Groupe</h2>
            <input type="text" placeholder="Nom du groupe" className="w-full border-2 border-[var(--border-color)] p-3 rounded-xl mb-4 bg-transparent" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}} value={groupName} onChange={e => setGroupName(e.target.value)} />
            <div className="flex gap-2">
              <button onClick={() => setShowGroupModal(false)} className="flex-1 py-2 font-bold opacity-50">Annuler</button>
              <button onClick={createGroup} className="flex-1 bg-[#4A90C2] text-white py-2 rounded-xl font-bold">Créer</button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {activePact && <PactModal pactId={activePact} onClose={() => setActivePact(null)} />}
    </div>
  );
}

function PactModal({ pactId, onClose }: { pactId: string; onClose: () => void }) {
  const [pact, setPact] = useState<any>(null);
  const [isDownloading, setIsDownloading] = useState(false);
  
  const loadPact = () => fetch(`/api/pacts/${pactId}`).then(r => r.json()).then(d => setPact(d));
  useEffect(() => { loadPact(); }, [pactId]);

  async function signPact() {
    const pubkey = localStorage.getItem('sceau_pubkey');
    const res = await fetch('/api/pacts/sign', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ pact_id: pactId, pubkey }) });
    const data = await res.json();
    if (data.success) loadPact();
  }

  async function downloadCertificate() {
    if (!pact) return;
    setIsDownloading(true);
    const verifyUrl = `${window.location.origin}/o/${pact.id}`;
    const qrDataUrl = await QRCode.toDataURL(verifyUrl, { width: 300, margin: 1 });
    generatePactCertificate(
      pact.title, pact.description, pact.creator, pact.signataires || [], 
      pact.hash_str || 'Hash non disponible', qrDataUrl, pact.id
    );
    setIsDownloading(false);
  }

  if (!pact) return null;
  const myHandle = localStorage.getItem('sceau_handle');
  const hasSigned = pact.signataires?.includes(myHandle);
  const isSealed = pact.status === 'scelle';

  // Parse sécurisé des médias
  let mediaUrls: string[] = [];
  try {
    if (pact.media_urls) mediaUrls = JSON.parse(pact.media_urls);
  } catch (e) {
    if (pact.file_path) mediaUrls = [pact.file_path];
  }

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/80 z-[60] flex items-center justify-center p-4 backdrop-blur-md">
      <div className={`bg-[#FDF8F0] w-full max-w-md rounded-3xl p-8 border-4 shadow-2xl relative max-h-[90vh] overflow-y-auto ${isSealed ? 'border-[#E9C46A]' : 'border-[#4A90C2]'}`}>
        
        {isSealed && (
          <div className="absolute -top-6 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 bg-[#E9C46A] text-[#2C1810] px-6 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-bold tracking-widest shadow-md border-2 border-white">
            Certificat Scellé
          </div>
        )}

        <div className="text-center mb-6">
          <div className="text-5xl mb-3">{isSealed ? '🏆' : '🤝'}</div>
          <h2 className="text-3xl font-serif font-bold" style={{color: '#2C1810'}}>{pact.title}</h2>
          <p className="text-xs font-mono text-[#A88620] italic mt-2">Proposé par @{pact.creator}</p>
        </div>

        <p className="italic mb-6 p-4 bg-white/50 border border-[#EFE0CA] rounded-xl text-sm text-center" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>
          "{pact.description}"
        </p>

        {/* LA GALERIE DE MÉDIAS */}
        {mediaUrls.length > 0 && (
          <div className="mb-6">
            <p className="text-xs font-bold text-[#A89070] font-mono mb-2">Médias joints ({mediaUrls.length})</p>
            <div className="flex gap-2 overflow-x-auto snap-x hide-scrollbar pb-2">
              {mediaUrls.map((url: string, i: number) => {
                const isImage = url.match(/\.(jpeg|jpg|gif|png|webp)/i);
                const isVideo = url.match(/\.(mp4|webm|ogg|mov)/i);
                return (
                  <div key={i} className="snap-start shrink-0 w-32 h-32 bg-[#FDF8F0] border border-[#EFE0CA] rounded-xl overflow-hidden flex items-center justify-center relative group">
                    {isImage ? (
                      <img src={url} alt="media" className="w-full h-full object-cover" />
                    ) : isVideo ? (
                      <video src={url} className="w-full h-full object-cover" />
                    ) : (
                      <a href={url} target="_blank" className="text-center p-2 text-xs font-bold text-[#2C1810] hover:text-[#E76F51]">📄<br/>Fichier</a>
                    )}
                    <a href={url} target="_blank" rel="noopener" className="absolute inset-0 bg-black/40 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 flex items-center justify-center text-white text-xs font-bold transition">Ouvrir</a>
                  </div>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        <div className="mb-6">
          <h3 className="text-xs font-mono font-bold text-[#A88620] mb-3 text-center">
            Signatures officielles ({pact.signataires?.length || 0} / {pact.required_signatures})
          </h3>
          <div className="flex flex-wrap justify-center gap-2">
            {(pact.signataires || []).map((s: string) => (
              <span key={s} className="bg-[#F5F9EE] text-[#5D7A3A] border border-[#A8C77E] px-3 py-1 rounded-lg text-sm font-bold shadow-sm">✅ @{s}</span>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="flex flex-col gap-2 mt-8">
          {isSealed ? (
            <button onClick={downloadCertificate} disabled={isDownloading} className="w-full bg-gradient-to-r from-[#E9C46A] to-[#F4A261] text-[#2C1810] py-4 rounded-xl font-bold text-lg shadow-lg hover:shadow-xl transition flex justify-center items-center gap-2">
              {isDownloading ? 'Génération...' : '📜 Télécharger le Certificat PDF'}
            </button>
          ) : (
            <button onClick={signPact} disabled={hasSigned} className="w-full bg-[#4A90C2] text-white py-4 rounded-xl font-bold text-lg shadow-lg hover:bg-[#2D6A9E] transition disabled:opacity-50">
              {hasSigned ? '✅ Vous avez signé' : '✍️ Contresigner le Pacte'}
            </button>
          )}
          <button onClick={onClose} className="w-full py-2 font-bold opacity-50 hover:opacity-100 transition" style={{color: '#2C1810'}}>Fermer</button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export default function Messages() {
  return (
    <Suspense fallback={<div className="p-10 text-center font-mono">Chargement...</div>}>
      <MessagesInner />
    </Suspense>
  );
}

'use client';
import Link from 'next/link';

export default function NotFound() {
  return (
    <div className="min-h-[80vh] flex flex-col items-center justify-center p-6 text-center font-serif">
      <div className="text-6xl mb-6">🔍</div>
      <h1 className="text-4xl font-bold mb-4" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>Œuvre introuvable.</h1>
      <p className="text-[var(--text-muted)] italic mb-8 max-w-sm mx-auto">
        Cette page n'existe pas, ou l'œuvre a été déplacée dans le coffre privé de son créateur.
      </p>
      <Link href="/feed" className="bg-[#E76F51] text-white px-8 py-3 rounded-xl font-bold shadow-md hover:bg-[#F4A261] transition">
        Retour au réseau
