=========================================
FICHIER : ./src/app/o/[id]/page.tsx
=========================================

'use client';
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { useParams } from 'next/navigation';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { generateCertificate } from '@/lib/pdf';
import QRCode from 'qrcode';

export default function PublicSealPage() {
  const params = useParams();
  const [seal, setSeal] = useState<any>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [refreshMsg, setRefreshMsg] = useState('');
  const [versions, setVersions] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [currentVersion, setCurrentVersion] = useState(1);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!params.id) return;

    fetch(`/api/seal/${params.id}/versions`)
      .then(r => r.json())
      .then(d => {
        setVersions(d.versions || []);
        setCurrentVersion(d.current_version || 1);
      })
      .catch(() => {});

    const pubkey = localStorage.getItem('sceau_pubkey');
    const qs = pubkey ? `?viewer=${encodeURIComponent(pubkey)}` : '';
    fetch(`/api/o/${params.id}${qs}`)
      .then(r => r.json())
      .then(d => {
        if (d.seal) setSeal(d.seal);
        setLoading(false);
      })
      .catch(() => setLoading(false));
  }, [params.id]);

  async function refreshOts() {
    setRefreshMsg('Interrogation des calendars Bitcoin...');
    const res = await fetch(`/api/seal/${params.id}/ots/refresh`, { method: 'POST' });
    const d = await res.json();
    if (d.status === 'upgraded') setRefreshMsg('✅ Preuve upgradée avec confirmation Bitcoin');
    else setRefreshMsg(d.message || '⏳ Pas encore confirmé.');
  }

  async function downloadCertificate() {
    if (!seal) return;
    const verifyUrl = `${window.location.origin}/o/${seal.id}`;
    const qrDataUrl = await QRCode.toDataURL(verifyUrl, { width: 300, margin: 1 });
    generateCertificate(
      seal.title,
      seal.author,
      seal.timestamp,
      seal.hash,
      qrDataUrl,
      seal.hash_hex,
      seal.id
    );
  }

  function downloadUrl() {
    if (typeof window === 'undefined') return '#';
    const pubkey = localStorage.getItem('sceau_pubkey');
    const qs = pubkey ? `?viewer=${encodeURIComponent(pubkey)}` : '';
    return `/api/download/${params.id}${qs}`;
  }

  if (loading) return <div className="p-10 text-center font-mono">Chargement...</div>;
  if (!seal) return <div className="p-10 text-center font-mono text-red-500">Œuvre introuvable.</div>;

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-[var(--bg-main)] flex flex-col items-center justify-center p-6 font-serif">
      <div className="w-full max-w-xl bg-[var(--bg-surface)] p-8 rounded-3xl border border-[var(--border-color)] shadow-2xl relative overflow-hidden">
        <div className="absolute top-0 left-0 w-full h-2 bg-gradient-to-r from-[#E76F51] to-[#F4A261]"></div>

        <div className="text-center mb-6">
          <span className="inline-block bg-[#FDF8F0] border border-[#EFE0CA] text-[#A89070] text-xs font-mono font-bold px-3 py-1 rounded-full italic mb-4">
            Preuve d'antériorité
          </span>
          <h1 className="text-4xl font-bold mb-2" style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>{seal.title}</h1>
          <p className="text-[var(--text-muted)]">
            Créé par{' '}
            <Link href={`/u/${seal.author}`} className="font-bold text-[#E76F51] hover:underline">
              @{seal.author}
            </Link>
          </p>
        </div>

        {seal.description && (
          <p className="text-center italic mb-6 opacity-80" style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>
            "{seal.description}"
          </p>
        )}

        {versions.length > 1 && (
          <div className="mb-6 p-4 rounded-xl border-2 border-[#4A90C2] bg-[#F0F6FC]">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-3">
              <span className="text-xl">📚</span>
              <div>
                <p className="text-sm font-bold text-[#2D6A9E]">Dossier Miroir</p>
                <p className="text-xs text-[#2D6A9E]">{versions.length} versions — celle-ci est la V{currentVersion}</p>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-1">
              {versions.map((v: any) => (
                <a
                  key={v.id}
                  href={`/o/${v.id}`}
                  className={`flex justify-between items-center p-2 rounded-lg text-xs ${v.id === seal.id ? 'bg-white border border-[#4A90C2] font-bold' : 'hover:bg-white'}`}
                >
                  <span style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>V{v.version_number} · {v.title}</span>
                  <span className="text-[0.6rem] font-mono text-[#2D6A9E]">
                    {new Date(v.timestamp).toLocaleDateString('fr-FR')} {v.has_ots && '⛓️'}
                  </span>
                </a>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        <div className="bg-[var(--bg-main)] p-4 rounded-xl border border-[var(--border-color)] mb-6 text-center">
          <p className="text-xs font-mono font-bold text-[var(--text-muted)] mb-1">SHA-256</p>
          <p className="text-xs font-mono break-all" style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>
            {seal.hash_hex || seal.hash}
          </p>
          <p className="text-xs font-mono text-[var(--text-muted)] mt-2">
            Scellé le {new Date(seal.timestamp).toLocaleString('fr-FR')}
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className={`mb-6 p-4 rounded-xl border-2 ${seal.has_ots_proof ? 'border-[#7A9B5A] bg-[#F5F9EE]' : 'border-[#F4D0A8] bg-[#FCEAD6]'}`}>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-2">
            <span className="text-2xl">⛓️</span>
            <div>
              <p className={`text-sm font-bold ${seal.has_ots_proof ? 'text-[#5D7A3A]' : 'text-[#C44A2E]'}`}>
                Ancrage Bitcoin (OpenTimestamps)
              </p>
              <p className={`text-xs ${seal.has_ots_proof ? 'text-[#7A9B5A]' : 'text-[#C44A2E]'}`}>
                {seal.has_ots_proof ? 'Preuve .ots disponible' : 'Indisponible'}
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
          {seal.has_ots_proof && (
            <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2 mt-3">
              <a href={`/api/seal/${seal.id}/ots`} download className="bg-[#5D7A3A] text-white px-3 py-2 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold">
                ⬇️ Télécharger .ots
              </a>
              <button onClick={refreshOts} className="bg-white border-2 border-[#7A9B5A] text-[#5D7A3A] px-3 py-2 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold">
                🔄 Vérifier confirmation
              </button>
              <a href="https://opentimestamps.org/" target="_blank" rel="noopener" className="bg-white border-2 border-[#EFE0CA] text-[var(--text-main)] px-3 py-2 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold">
                🔍 Vérifier en ligne
              </a>
            </div>
          )}
          {refreshMsg && <p className="text-xs mt-2 italic text-[#5D7A3A]">{refreshMsg}</p>}
        </div>

        <button
          onClick={downloadCertificate}
          className="w-full text-center bg-gradient-to-r from-[#E76F51] to-[#F4A261] text-white py-3 rounded-xl font-bold shadow-md hover:shadow-lg transition mb-3"
        >
          📄 Télécharger le certificat d'antériorité (PDF)
        </button>

        {seal.is_hosted && seal.file_path && (
          <a
            href={downloadUrl()}
            className="block w-full text-center bg-[var(--text-main)] text-[var(--bg-surface)] py-3 rounded-xl font-bold shadow-md hover:opacity-80 transition mb-6"
          >
            📂 Découvrir l'œuvre originale
          </a>
        )}

        <div className="border-t border-[var(--border-color)] pt-6 mt-6 text-center">
          <Link
            href="/seal"
            className="inline-block bg-gradient-to-r from-[#E76F51] to-[#F4A261] text-white px-8 py-3 rounded-xl font-bold shadow-lg hover:shadow-xl transition"
          >
            Sceller mon œuvre gratuitement
          </Link>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export default function Home() {

=========================================
FICHIER : ./src/app/profil/AccessLogModal.tsx
=========================================

'use client';
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';

export default function AccessLogModal({ sealId, onClose }: { sealId: string; onClose: () => void }) {
  const [entries, setEntries] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [search, setSearch] = useState('');
  const [sort, setSort] = useState('recent');

  useEffect(() => {
    const pubkey = localStorage.getItem('sceau_pubkey');
    setLoading(true);
    fetch(`/api/seal/${sealId}/access-log?pubkey=${encodeURIComponent(pubkey || '')}&search=${search}&sort=${sort}`)
      .then(r => r.json())
      .then(d => { setEntries(d.entries || []); setLoading(false); });
  }, [sealId, search, sort]);

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/60 z-[60] flex items-center justify-center p-4 backdrop-blur-sm" onClick={onClose}>
      <div className="bg-[var(--bg-surface)] w-full max-w-md rounded-3xl p-6 max-h-[80vh] flex flex-col" onClick={e => e.stopPropagation()}>
        <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
          <h2 className="text-xl font-bold" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>📖 Journal des accès</h2>
          <button onClick={onClose} className="text-xl opacity-50">✕</button>
        </div>

        <div className="flex gap-2 mb-4">
          <input 
            type="text" 
            placeholder="Chercher un pseudo..." 
            className="flex-1 border-2 border-[var(--border-color)] rounded-xl px-3 py-2 text-sm outline-none focus:border-[#4A90C2] bg-[var(--bg-main)]"
            style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}
            value={search}
            onChange={e => setSearch(e.target.value)}
          />
          <select 
            className="border-2 border-[var(--border-color)] rounded-xl px-2 py-2 text-xs font-bold outline-none bg-[var(--bg-main)] cursor-pointer"
            style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}
            value={sort}
            onChange={e => setSort(e.target.value)}
          >
            <option value="recent">Vues récentes</option>
            <option value="newest_account">Comptes les + récents</option>
            <option value="oldest_account">Comptes les + anciens</option>
          </select>
        </div>
        <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto">
          {loading ? <p className="text-center italic text-[var(--text-muted)]">Chargement...</p> :
           entries.length === 0 ? <p className="text-center italic text-[var(--text-muted)] py-8">Aucun accès enregistré pour le moment.</p> :
           <div className="space-y-2">
             {entries.map((e, i) => (
               <div key={i} className="p-3 rounded-xl border border-[var(--border-color)] bg-[var(--bg-main)]">
                 <div className="flex justify-between items-baseline">
                   <span className="font-bold text-sm" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>@{e.viewer}</span>
                   <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                     {e.viewer_profile.account_age_days < 7 && <span className="text-[0.55rem] bg-red-100 text-red-600 font-bold px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded uppercase">Nouveau</span>}
                     <span className={`text-[0.6rem] font-mono px-2 py-0.5 rounded ${e.action === 'download' ? 'bg-[#FCEAD6] text-[#C44A2E]' : 'bg-[#DCE8F5] text-[#2D6A9E]'}`}>{e.action}</span>
                   </div>
                 </div>
                 <div className="text-xs text-[var(--text-muted)] mt-1">
                   {new Date(e.at).toLocaleString('fr-FR')} · compte de {e.viewer_profile.account_age_days} jours · {e.viewer_profile.seals_count} sceaux
                 </div>
               </div>
             ))}
           </div>
          }
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

=========================================
FICHIER : ./src/app/profil/page.tsx
=========================================

'use client';
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { generatePortfolioPDF } from '@/lib/pdf';
import AccessLogModal from './AccessLogModal';

export default function Profil() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [data, setData] = useState<any>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState('public');

  const [showPortfolioModal, setShowPortfolioModal] = useState(false);
  const [selectedSeals, setSelectedSeals] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [filterType, setFilterType] = useState('all');
  const [startDate, setStartDate] = useState('');
  const [endDate, setEndDate] = useState('');
  const [accessLogSealId, setAccessLogSealId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [showAccessLogList, setShowAccessLogList] = useState(false);
  const [showTechProofs, setShowTechProofs] = useState(false);
  const [refreshingId, setRefreshingId] = useState<string | null>(null);

  async function loadProfil() {
    const pubkey = localStorage.getItem('sceau_pubkey');
    if (!pubkey) { router.push('/login'); return; }
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`/api/profil?pubkey=${encodeURIComponent(pubkey)}`);
      const d = await res.json();
      if (res.ok && d.handle) setData({ ...d, seals: d.seals || [], badges: d.badges || [], favorites: d.favorites || [] });
      else setError("Profil introuvable.");
    } catch (e) { setError("Erreur serveur."); }
  }

  useEffect(() => { loadProfil(); }, [router]);

  const openPortfolioModal = () => {
    setShowPortfolioModal(true);
    setSelectedSeals(data.seals.map((s: any) => s.id));
    setFilterType('all');
    setStartDate('');
    setEndDate('');
  };

  const applyFilters = (type: string, start: string, end: string) => {
    let filtered = data.seals;
    if (type !== 'all') {
      if (type === 'flash') filtered = filtered.filter((s: any) => s.is_flash);
      else filtered = filtered.filter((s: any) => s.visibility === type && !s.is_flash);
    }
    if (start) filtered = filtered.filter((s: any) => new Date(s.timestamp) >= new Date(start));
    if (end) filtered = filtered.filter((s: any) => new Date(s.timestamp) <= new Date(end));
    setSelectedSeals(filtered.map((s: any) => s.id));
  };

  const handleTypeChange = (type: string) => { setFilterType(type); applyFilters(type, startDate, endDate); };
  const handleStartDate = (date: string) => { setStartDate(date); applyFilters(filterType, date, endDate); };
  const handleEndDate = (date: string) => { setEndDate(date); applyFilters(filterType, startDate, date); };

  const toggleSeal = (id: string) => {
    if (selectedSeals.includes(id)) setSelectedSeals(selectedSeals.filter(sid => sid !== id));
    else setSelectedSeals([...selectedSeals, id]);
  };

  const generatePDF = () => {
    const sealsToInclude = data.seals.filter((s: any) => selectedSeals.includes(s.id));
    if (sealsToInclude.length === 0) return alert("Sélectionne au moins une œuvre !");
    generatePortfolioPDF(data.handle, sealsToInclude, startDate, endDate, data.is_pro);
    setShowPortfolioModal(false);
  };

  async function changeVisibility(sealId: string, newVisibility: string) {
    const pubkey = localStorage.getItem('sceau_pubkey');
    await fetch('/api/seal/update-visibility', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ seal_id: sealId, pubkey, visibility: newVisibility })
    });
    loadProfil();
  }

  function openTechProofs() {
    if (activeTab === 'favorites') setActiveTab('public');
    setShowTechProofs(true);
  }

  async function refreshOts(sealId: string) {
    const pubkey = localStorage.getItem('sceau_pubkey');
    if (!pubkey) return alert('Connecte-toi.');
    setRefreshingId(sealId);
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`/api/seal/${sealId}/ots/refresh?pubkey=${encodeURIComponent(pubkey)}`, { method: 'POST' });
      const d = await res.json();
      if (d.status === 'upgraded') {
        alert('✅ Preuve mise à jour avec confirmation Bitcoin');
        await loadProfil();
      } else if (d.status === 'pending') {
        alert('⏳ Pas encore confirmé. Réessaie dans quelques heures.');
      } else if (d.status === 'no_proof') {
        alert('❌ Aucune preuve .ots enregistrée pour ce sceau.');
      } else {
        alert('❌ ' + (d.error || 'Erreur inconnue'));
      }
    } catch {
      alert('❌ Impossible de contacter le serveur.');
    } finally {
      setRefreshingId(null);
    }
  }

  if (error) return <div className="p-10 text-center font-mono text-red-500">{error}</div>;
  if (!data) return <div className="p-10 text-center font-mono">Chargement...</div>;

  const counts = {
    public: data.seals.filter((s:any) => s.visibility === 'public' && !s.is_flash).length,
    friends: data.seals.filter((s:any) => s.visibility === 'friends' && !s.is_flash).length,
    private: data.seals.filter((s:any) => s.visibility === 'private' && !s.is_flash).length,
    flash: data.seals.filter((s:any) => s.is_flash).length,
  };

  // ONGLET FAVORIS
  if (activeTab === 'favorites') {
    return (
      <div className="p-6 max-w-2xl mx-auto font-sans">
        <button onClick={() => setActiveTab('public')} className="text-sm font-bold text-[#A89070] mb-4 hover:underline">← Retour au coffre</button>
        <button onClick={openTechProofs} className="mb-4 bg-[#FDF8F0] text-[#A88620] border border-[#E9C46A] px-4 py-2 rounded-lg font-bold text-sm shadow-sm">🔬 Preuves techniques</button>
        <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold mb-4" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>📌 Mes Favoris</h2>
        <div className="grid gap-3">
          {data.favorites?.map((fav: any) => (
            <Link href={`/o/${fav.id}`} key={fav.id} className="bg-[var(--bg-surface)] p-4 rounded-xl border border-[var(--border-color)] block hover:border-[#E76F51]">
              <div className="font-bold text-lg" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>{fav.title}</div>
              <div className="text-xs text-[var(--text-muted)]">Par @{fav.author} • {new Date(fav.timestamp).toLocaleDateString('fr-FR')}</div>
            </Link>
          ))}
          {(!data.favorites || data.favorites.length === 0) && <p className="italic text-[var(--text-muted)] text-center py-10">Aucune œuvre sauvegardée.</p>}
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  const filteredSeals = data.seals.filter((seal: any) => {
    if (activeTab === 'flash') return seal.is_flash === true;
    if (activeTab === 'public') return seal.visibility === 'public' && !seal.is_flash;
    if (activeTab === 'friends') return seal.visibility === 'friends' && !seal.is_flash;
    if (activeTab === 'private') return seal.visibility === 'private' && !seal.is_flash;
    return true;
  });

  return (
    <div className="p-6 max-w-2xl mx-auto font-serif relative">
      
      <div className="text-center mb-6 relative">
        <Link href="/settings" className="absolute top-0 right-0 text-2xl opacity-50 hover:opacity-100 transition" title="Paramètres">⚙️</Link>
        
        <div className="w-24 h-24 rounded-full bg-gradient-to-br from-[#E76F51] to-[#F4A261] text-white flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-3xl mx-auto mb-4 shadow-lg relative">
          {data.handle[0].toUpperCase()}
          {data.sealed >= 10 && <div className="absolute bottom-0 right-0 bg-green-500 border-2 border-white rounded-full w-6 h-6 flex items-center justify-center text-xs">✓</div>}
        </div>
        
        <h1 className="text-4xl font-bold mb-1 flex items-center justify-center gap-2" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>
          @{data.handle}
          {data.is_pro && <span className="text-sm bg-[#2C1810] text-[#E9C46A] px-2 py-1 rounded-lg border border-[#E9C46A] tracking-widest">👑 PRO</span>}
          {data.streak > 0 && <span className="text-sm bg-[#FDF4F0] text-[#C44A2E] px-2 py-1 rounded-lg border border-[#FCE0D6]">🔥 {data.streak} jours</span>}
        </h1>
        <p className="text-sm font-mono text-[var(--text-muted)] italic">{data.role || 'Identité souveraine'}</p>
        {data.bio && <p className="mt-3 text-sm italic opacity-80 max-w-md mx-auto" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>"{data.bio}"</p>}
      </div>

      {data.badges.length > 0 && (
        <div className="mb-8 bg-[#FDF8F0] border border-[#EFE0CA] p-4 rounded-2xl">
          <h2 className="text-xs font-mono font-bold text-[#A89070] italic mb-3 text-center">Badges</h2>
          <div className="flex flex-wrap justify-center gap-2">
            {data.badges.map((badge: any, i: number) => (
              <div key={i} className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center bg-white border border-[#EFE0CA] w-16 h-16 rounded-2xl shadow-sm cursor-help" title={badge.desc}>
                <span className="text-2xl">{badge.icon}</span>
                <span className="text-[0.55rem] font-bold text-[#7A5A3A] mt-1">{badge.name}</span>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      <div className="flex bg-[var(--bg-surface)] border border-[var(--border-color)] p-6 rounded-2xl justify-around mb-8 shadow-sm">
        <div className="text-center">
          <div className="text-3xl font-mono font-bold text-[#E76F51]">{data.sealed}</div>
          <div className="text-[0.65rem] font-mono text-[var(--text-muted)] mt-1">Scellés</div>
        </div>
        <div className="text-center">
          <div className="text-3xl font-mono font-bold text-[#E76F51]">{data.hosted}</div>
          <div className="text-[0.65rem] font-mono text-[var(--text-muted)] mt-1">Hébergés</div>
        </div>
        <div className="text-center">
          <div className="text-3xl font-mono font-bold text-[#E76F51]">{data.pacts}</div>
          <div className="text-[0.65rem] font-mono text-[var(--text-muted)] mt-1">Pactes</div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-3 mb-8">
        <button onClick={openPortfolioModal} className="flex-1 min-w-[180px] bg-[var(--text-main)] text-[var(--bg-surface)] p-3 rounded-xl font-bold text-sm shadow-md hover:opacity-80 transition">📄 Exporter mon Portfolio</button>
        <button onClick={() => setShowAccessLogList(true)} className="flex-1 min-w-[180px] bg-[#F0F6FC] text-[#2D6A9E] border border-[#C9D8E8] p-3 rounded-xl font-bold text-sm shadow-sm hover:bg-[#E0EDF8] transition">📖 Journal des accès</button>
        <button onClick={openTechProofs} className="flex-1 min-w-[180px] bg-[#FDF8F0] text-[#A88620] border border-[#E9C46A] p-3 rounded-xl font-bold text-sm shadow-sm hover:bg-[#FCEFCB] transition">🔬 Preuves techniques</button>
      </div>

      <div className="mb-4">
        <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold mb-4" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>📚 Mon Coffre</h2>
        <div className="flex bg-[var(--bg-surface)] border border-[var(--border-color)] p-1 rounded-xl flex-wrap sm:flex-nowrap gap-1">
          <button onClick={() => setActiveTab('public')} className={`flex-1 py-2 text-xs font-bold rounded-lg transition ${activeTab === 'public' ? 'bg-[#F5F9EE] text-[#5D7A3A] shadow' : 'opacity-50'}`}>🌍 Public ({counts.public})</button>
          <button onClick={() => setActiveTab('friends')} className={`flex-1 py-2 text-xs font-bold rounded-lg transition ${activeTab === 'friends' ? 'bg-[#F0F6FC] text-[#2D6A9E] shadow' : 'opacity-50'}`}>👥 Amis ({counts.friends})</button>
          <button onClick={() => setActiveTab('private')} className={`flex-1 py-2 text-xs font-bold rounded-lg transition ${activeTab === 'private' ? 'bg-[#FDF4F0] text-[#C44A2E] shadow' : 'opacity-50'}`}>🔒 Privé ({counts.private})</button>
          <button onClick={() => setActiveTab('flash')} className={`flex-1 py-2 text-xs font-bold rounded-lg transition ${activeTab === 'flash' ? 'bg-[#2C1810] text-white shadow' : 'opacity-50'}`}>⚡ Flash ({counts.flash})</button>
          <button onClick={() => setActiveTab('favorites')} className={`flex-1 py-2 text-xs font-bold rounded-lg transition ${activeTab === 'favorites' ? 'bg-[#FCEAD6] text-[#C44A2E] shadow' : 'opacity-50'}`}>📌 Favoris ({data.favorites?.length || 0})</button>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="space-y-3 mb-10">
        {filteredSeals.length === 0 && <p className="text-[var(--text-muted)] italic text-sm p-4 bg-[var(--bg-surface)] rounded-xl text-center">Aucune œuvre dans ce compartiment.</p>}
        
        {filteredSeals.map((seal: any) => (
          <div key={seal.id} className="bg-[var(--bg-surface)] p-4 rounded-xl border border-[var(--border-color)] flex flex-col sm:flex-row justify-between sm:items-center shadow-sm gap-3">
            <div className="flex-1">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-1">
                <div className="font-bold text-lg" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>{seal.title}</div>
                {seal.is_flash && <span className="text-xs bg-[#FCEAD6] text-[#C44A2E] px-2 py-0.5 rounded">Flash</span>}
              </div>
              {seal.description && <div className="text-xs italic text-[var(--text-muted)] mb-2">"{seal.description}"</div>}
              <div className="flex gap-3 text-xs font-mono text-[var(--text-muted)]">
                <span>{new Date(seal.timestamp).toLocaleDateString('fr-FR')}</span>
                <span>❤️ {seal.likes_count || 0}</span>
                <span>💬 {seal.comments_count || 0}</span>
              </div>
            </div>
            
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <button onClick={() => setAccessLogSealId(seal.id)} className="text-xs font-bold text-[#4A90C2] hover:underline px-2" title="Voir le journal des accès">📖</button>
              <select value={seal.is_flash ? 'flash' : seal.visibility} onChange={(e) => changeVisibility(seal.id, e.target.value)} className="text-xs border border-[var(--border-color)] rounded-lg p-2 bg-[var(--bg-main)] font-bold outline-none cursor-pointer" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}} disabled={seal.is_flash}>
                <option value="public">🌍 Public</option>
                <option value="friends">👥 Amis</option>
                <option value="private">🔒 Privé</option>
                {seal.is_flash && <option value="flash">⚡ Flash</option>}
              </select>
              <button onClick={async () => {
                if (!confirm("Supprimer définitivement cette œuvre ?")) return;
                const p = localStorage.getItem('sceau_pubkey');
                await fetch(`/api/seal/delete?id=${seal.id}&pubkey=${encodeURIComponent(p!)}`, { method: 'DELETE' });
                loadProfil();
              }} className="text-red-400 hover:text-red-600 p-2 text-xl">🗑️</button>
            </div>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>

      {showPortfolioModal && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/60 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 backdrop-blur-sm">
          <div className="bg-[var(--bg-surface)] w-full max-w-md rounded-3xl p-6 flex flex-col max-h-[90vh]">
            <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold mb-2" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>Configurer le Portfolio</h2>
            <div className="flex bg-[var(--bg-main)] p-1 rounded-xl mb-3 text-[0.65rem] font-mono font-bold">
              <button onClick={() => handleTypeChange('all')} className={`flex-1 py-1.5 rounded-lg ${filterType === 'all' ? 'bg-white shadow' : 'opacity-50'}`}>Tout</button>
              <button onClick={() => handleTypeChange('public')} className={`flex-1 py-1.5 rounded-lg ${filterType === 'public' ? 'bg-white shadow text-[#5D7A3A]' : 'opacity-50'}`}>Public</button>
              <button onClick={() => handleTypeChange('private')} className={`flex-1 py-1.5 rounded-lg ${filterType === 'private' ? 'bg-white shadow text-[#C44A2E]' : 'opacity-50'}`}>Privé</button>
              <button onClick={() => handleTypeChange('flash')} className={`flex-1 py-1.5 rounded-lg ${filterType === 'flash' ? 'bg-white shadow text-[#C44A2E]' : 'opacity-50'}`}>Flash</button>
            </div>
            <div className="flex gap-2 mb-4">
              <div className="flex-1">
                <label className="text-[0.6rem] font-bold text-[var(--text-muted)] mb-1 block">Du</label>
                <input type="date" value={startDate} onChange={e => handleStartDate(e.target.value)} className="w-full border border-[var(--border-color)] p-2 rounded-lg text-xs bg-transparent" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}} />
              </div>
              <div className="flex-1">
                <label className="text-[0.6rem] font-bold text-[var(--text-muted)] mb-1 block">Au</label>
                <input type="date" value={endDate} onChange={e => handleEndDate(e.target.value)} className="w-full border border-[var(--border-color)] p-2 rounded-lg text-xs bg-transparent" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}} />
              </div>
            </div>
            <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto space-y-2 mb-4 pr-1">
              {data.seals.map((seal: any) => (
                <label key={seal.id} className="flex items-center gap-3 p-3 rounded-xl border border-[var(--border-color)] hover:bg-[var(--bg-main)] cursor-pointer transition">
                  <input type="checkbox" checked={selectedSeals.includes(seal.id)} onChange={() => toggleSeal(seal.id)} className="w-5 h-5 accent-[#E76F51]" />
                  <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                    <div className="font-bold text-sm truncate" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>{seal.title}</div>
                    <div className="text-[0.6rem] font-mono text-[var(--text-muted)]">{new Date(seal.timestamp).toLocaleDateString('fr-FR')}</div>
                  </div>
                </label>
              ))}
            </div>
            <div className="flex gap-2">
              <button onClick={() => setShowPortfolioModal(false)} className="flex-1 py-3 font-bold opacity-50">Annuler</button>
              <button onClick={generatePDF} className="flex-1 bg-[var(--text-main)] text-white py-3 rounded-xl font-bold shadow-md">Générer ({selectedSeals.length})</button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
      {accessLogSealId && <AccessLogModal sealId={accessLogSealId} onClose={() => setAccessLogSealId(null)} />}

      {/* MODALE — PREUVES TECHNIQUES (propriétaire uniquement) */}
      {showTechProofs && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/60 z-[60] flex items-center justify-center p-4 backdrop-blur-sm">
          <div className="bg-[var(--bg-surface)] w-full max-w-2xl rounded-3xl p-6 max-h-[90vh] flex flex-col">
            <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-3">
              <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>🔬 Preuves techniques</h2>
              <button onClick={() => setShowTechProofs(false)} className="text-2xl opacity-50">✕</button>
            </div>
            <p className="text-xs text-[var(--text-muted)] italic mb-4">
              Zone technique. Le public ne voit que <strong>Validé / Non validé</strong> sur la page de chaque œuvre.
              Ici tu peux vérifier, télécharger et forcer la confirmation des preuves OpenTimestamps.
            </p>
            <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto space-y-3 pr-1">
              {data.seals.length === 0 && (
                <p className="text-center italic text-[var(--text-muted)] py-10">Aucune œuvre dans ton coffre.</p>
              )}
              {data.seals.map((seal: any) => {
                const pubkey = typeof window !== 'undefined' ? localStorage.getItem('sceau_pubkey') : '';
                const isRefreshing = refreshingId === seal.id;
                return (
                  <div key={seal.id} className="bg-[var(--bg-main)] border border-[var(--border-color)] rounded-xl p-4">
                    <div className="flex justify-between items-start gap-3 mb-3">
                      <div className="min-w-0">
                        <div className="font-bold text-sm truncate" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>{seal.title}</div>
                        <div className="text-[0.65rem] font-mono text-[var(--text-muted)]">
                          Scellé le {new Date(seal.timestamp).toLocaleDateString('fr-FR')}
                        </div>
                      </div>
                      {seal.ots_upgraded_at ? (
                        <span className="shrink-0 bg-[#F5F9EE] text-[#5D7A3A] border border-[#A8C77E] px-2 py-1 rounded text-[0.65rem] font-bold whitespace-nowrap">
                          ✅ Confirmé {new Date(seal.ots_upgraded_at).toLocaleDateString('fr-FR')}
                        </span>
                      ) : seal.ots_proof ? (
                        <span className="shrink-0 bg-[#FCEAD6] text-[#C44A2E] border border-[#F4D0A8] px-2 py-1 rounded text-[0.65rem] font-bold whitespace-nowrap">
                          ⏳ En attente
                        </span>
                      ) : (
                        <span className="shrink-0 bg-[#F3F3F3] text-[#666] border border-[#DDD] px-2 py-1 rounded text-[0.65rem] font-bold whitespace-nowrap">
                          🔒 Pas d'ancrage
                        </span>
                      )}
                    </div>

                    {seal.hash_hex && (
                      <div className="mb-3">
                        <div className="text-[0.6rem] font-mono text-[var(--text-muted)] mb-1 uppercase">SHA-256 (hex)</div>
                        <div className="bg-white border border-[var(--border-color)] rounded p-2 font-mono text-[0.6rem] break-all leading-relaxed">
                          {seal.hash_hex}
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    )}

                    <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                      {seal.ots_proof ? (
                        <>
                          <a
                            href={`/api/seal/${seal.id}/ots?pubkey=${encodeURIComponent(pubkey || '')}`}
                            download
                            className="bg-[#5D7A3A] text-white px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold"
                          >
                            ⬇️ Télécharger .ots
                          </a>
                          <button
                            onClick={() => refreshOts(seal.id)}
                            disabled={isRefreshing || !!seal.ots_upgraded_at}
                            className="bg-white border-2 border-[#7A9B5A] text-[#5D7A3A] px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold disabled:opacity-40"
                          >
                            {isRefreshing ? '⏳...' : seal.ots_upgraded_at ? '✅ Confirmé' : '🔄 Vérifier confirmation'}
                          </button>
                          <a
                            href="https://opentimestamps.org/"
                            target="_blank"
                            rel="noopener noreferrer"
                            className="bg-white border border-[var(--border-color)] text-[var(--text-main)] px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold"
                          >
                            🔍 Vérifier en ligne
                          </a>
                        </>
                      ) : (
                        <span className="text-xs text-[var(--text-muted)] italic">
                          Aucune preuve .ots enregistrée (l'ancrage a échoué au scellage).
                        </span>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* MODALE DE SÉLECTION DU JOURNAL DES ACCÈS */}
      {showAccessLogList && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/60 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 backdrop-blur-sm">
          <div className="bg-[var(--bg-surface)] w-full max-w-md rounded-3xl p-6 max-h-[80vh] flex flex-col">
            <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
              <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>Journal des accès</h2>
              <button onClick={() => setShowAccessLogList(false)} className="text-2xl opacity-50">✕</button>
            </div>
            <p className="text-sm text-[var(--text-muted)] italic mb-4">Sélectionne une œuvre pour voir qui l'a consultée ou téléchargée.</p>
            <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto space-y-2">
              {data.seals.map((seal: any) => (
                <div 
                  key={seal.id} 
                  onClick={() => { setShowAccessLogList(false); setAccessLogSealId(seal.id); }} 
                  className="p-3 rounded-xl border border-[var(--border-color)] hover:bg-[var(--bg-main)] cursor-pointer transition flex justify-between items-center"
                >
                  <div>
                    <div className="font-bold text-sm" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>{seal.title}</div>
                    <div className="text-xs text-[var(--text-muted)]">{new Date(seal.timestamp).toLocaleDateString('fr-FR')}</div>
                  </div>
                  <span className="text-[#4A90C2] text-sm font-bold">Voir →</span>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

=========================================
FICHIER : ./src/app/reset-password/page.tsx
=========================================

'use client';
import { useState, Suspense } from 'react';
import { useSearchParams, useRouter } from 'next/navigation';

function ResetForm() {
  const searchParams = useSearchParams();
  const router = useRouter();
  const token = searchParams.get('token');
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  const [show, setShow] = useState(false);
  const [status, setStatus] = useState('');

  async function handleReset() {
    if (!password) return setStatus('Mot de passe requis');
    const res = await fetch('/api/auth/reset-password', { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify({ token, newPassword: password }) });
    const data = await res.json();
    if (res.ok) {
      setStatus('✅ Mot de passe modifié !');
      setTimeout(() => router.push('/login'), 1500);
    } else setStatus('❌ ' + data.error);
  }

  return (
    <div className="max-w-md mx-auto bg-[#FFFCF7] p-8 rounded-3xl border border-[#EFE0CA] shadow-sm mt-10">
      <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold mb-6 font-serif">Nouveau mot de passe</h1>
      <div className="relative mb-4">
        <input type={show ? "text" : "password"} placeholder="Nouveau mot de passe" className="w-full border-2 border-[#EFE0CA] p-3 rounded-xl pr-10" value={password} onChange={e => setPassword(e.target.value)} />
        <button onClick={() => setShow(!show)} className="absolute right-3 top-3">{show ? "🙈" : "👁️"}</button>
      </div>
      <button onClick={handleReset} className="w-full bg-[#2C1810] text-white p-3 rounded-xl font-bold">Valider</button>
      {status && <p className="mt-4 text-center font-bold">{status}</p>}
    </div>
  );
}

export default function ResetPassword() {
  return <Suspense fallback={<div>Chargement...</div>}><ResetForm /></Suspense>;
}

'use client';
import { useEffect } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';

export default function Reset() {
  const router = useRouter();
  
  useEffect(() => {
    // On vide toute la mémoire du téléphone
    localStorage.clear();
    // On attend 2 secondes et on renvoie vers l'accueil
    setTimeout(() => router.push('/'), 2000);
  }, [router]);

  return (
    <div className="min-h-[60vh] flex flex-col items-center justify-center p-6 text-center font-serif">
      <div className="text-5xl mb-4 animate-spin">⏳</div>
      <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold mb-2" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>Nettoyage en cours...</h1>
      <p className="text-[var(--text-muted)] italic">Fermeture de la session précédente. Redirection...</p>
    </div>
  );
}

=========================================
FICHIER : ./src/app/seal/page.tsx
=========================================

'use client';
import { useState, useRef, Suspense } from 'react';
import { sha256 } from '@noble/hashes/sha256';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { useSearchParams } from 'next/navigation';

function SealContent() {
  const searchParams = useSearchParams();
  const isPactMode = searchParams.get('pact') === 'true';
  const chatId = searchParams.get('chat_id');
  const chatType = searchParams.get('chat_type');
  const challengeIdParam = searchParams.get('challenge_id');

  const [isFlashMode, setIsFlashMode] = useState(false);
  const [mode, setMode] = useState<'file' | 'text' | 'media' | 'audio' | 'project'>('file');
  const [projectFiles, setProjectFiles] = useState<File[]>([]);

  const [title, setTitle] = useState('');
  const [desc, setDesc] = useState('');
  const [textContent, setTextContent] = useState('');
  const [file, setFile] = useState<File | null>(null);
  const [pactFiles, setPactFiles] = useState<File[]>([]);

  const [visibility, setVisibility] = useState('public');
  const [isHosted, setIsHosted] = useState(true);
  const [isMystery, setIsMystery] = useState(false);
  const [isEphemeral, setIsEphemeral] = useState(false);
  const [unlockLikes, setUnlockLikes] = useState(10);
  const [revealAt, setRevealAt] = useState('');
  const [requiredSignatures, setRequiredSignatures] = useState(2);

  const [status, setStatus] = useState('');
  const [sealedResult, setSealedResult] = useState<any>(null);

  const [isRecording, setIsRecording] = useState(false);
  const [audioUrl, setAudioUrl] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const mediaRecorderRef = useRef<MediaRecorder | null>(null);
  const audioChunksRef = useRef<Blob[]>([]);

  async function startRecording() {
    try {
      const stream = await navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({ audio: true });
      const mediaRecorder = new MediaRecorder(stream);
      mediaRecorderRef.current = mediaRecorder;
      audioChunksRef.current = [];
      mediaRecorder.ondataavailable = (event) => { if (event.data.size > 0) audioChunksRef.current.push(event.data); };
      mediaRecorder.onstop = () => {
        const audioBlob = new Blob(audioChunksRef.current, { type: 'audio/webm' });
        setFile(new File([audioBlob], `audio-${Date.now()}.webm`, { type: 'audio/webm' }));
        setAudioUrl(URL.createObjectURL(audioBlob));
      };
      mediaRecorder.start();
      setIsRecording(true);
    } catch { setStatus("❌ Impossible d'accéder au micro."); }
  }

  function stopRecording() {
    if (mediaRecorderRef.current && isRecording) {
      mediaRecorderRef.current.stop();
      mediaRecorderRef.current.stream.getTracks().forEach(track => track.stop());
      setIsRecording(false);
    }
  }

  async function handleSeal() {
    setStatus('');

    if (!title.trim()) return setStatus("❌ Un titre est requis");
    if (mode === 'project' && projectFiles.length === 0) return setStatus("❌ Sélectionne des fichiers pour ton projet");
    if (isPactMode && pactFiles.length === 0) return setStatus("❌ Ajoute au moins un fichier au pacte");
    if (!isPactMode && mode === 'text' && !textContent.trim()) return setStatus("❌ Écris ton texte");
    if (!isPactMode && mode === 'file' && !file) return setStatus("❌ Choisis un fichier à sceller");
    if (!isPactMode && mode === 'media' && !file) return setStatus("❌ Prends une photo ou une vidéo");
    if (!isPactMode && mode === 'audio' && !file) return setStatus("❌ Enregistre un audio");

    setStatus(isPactMode ? "Préparation du pacte..." : "Calcul de l'empreinte...");
    try {
      let hashContent: Uint8Array;

      if (mode === 'project') {
        // Pour un projet, le hash principal est calculé côté serveur (Merkle Root)
        hashContent = sha256(new TextEncoder().encode(title)); // Hash factice temporaire
      } else if (isPactMode) {
        const metaString = `${title}-${desc}-${pactFiles.map(f => f.name).join(',')}`;
        hashContent = sha256(new TextEncoder().encode(metaString));
      } else if (mode === 'text') {
        hashContent = sha256(new TextEncoder().encode(textContent));
      } else {
        if (!file) return setStatus("❌ Fichier manquant");
        hashContent = sha256(new Uint8Array(await file.arrayBuffer()));
      }

      let hashStr = '';
      for (let i = 0; i < hashContent.length; i++) hashStr += String.fromCharCode(hashContent[i]);
      await sendToServer(btoa(hashStr));
    } catch (e: any) {
      console.error(e);
      setStatus("❌ Erreur de gravure : " + (e?.message || 'inconnue'));
    }
  }

  async function sendToServer(hashB64: string) {
    const pubkey = localStorage.getItem('sceau_pubkey');
    if (!pubkey) return setStatus("❌ Connecte-toi d'abord !");

    const formData = new FormData();
    formData.append('hash', hashB64);
    formData.append('title', title);
    formData.append('description', mode === 'text' ? textContent : desc);
    formData.append('pubkey', pubkey);

    let endpoint = '/api/seal';

    if (mode === 'project') {
      endpoint = '/api/projects';
      formData.append('isHosted', isHosted.toString());
      projectFiles.forEach(f => formData.append('files', f));
    } else if (isPactMode) {
      endpoint = '/api/pacts/create';
      formData.append('group_id', chatType === 'group' ? (chatId as string) : '');
      formData.append('required_signatures', requiredSignatures.toString());
      pactFiles.forEach(f => formData.append('files', f));
    } else {
      formData.append('isFlash', isFlashMode.toString());
      formData.append('visibility', visibility);
      formData.append('isMystery', isMystery.toString());
      formData.append('isEphemeral', isEphemeral.toString());
      formData.append('unlockLikes', unlockLikes.toString());
      formData.append('reveal_at', revealAt);
      formData.append('isHosted', isHosted.toString());
      if (challengeIdParam) formData.append('challenge_id', challengeIdParam);
      if (mode !== 'text' && file) formData.append('file', file);
    }

    setStatus(isPactMode ? "Envoi du pacte..." : "Ancrage dans la blockchain (max 8s)…");

    try {
      const res = await fetch(endpoint, { method: 'POST', body: formData });
      const data = await res.json().catch(() => ({} as any));

      if (!res.ok) {
        return setStatus("❌ " + (data?.error || `Erreur serveur (${res.status})`));
      }

      if (isPactMode) {
        const msg = `[PACTE]${data.id}|${data.title}`;
        if (chatType === 'dm') {
          await fetch('/api/messages', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ sender_handle: localStorage.getItem('sceau_handle'), receiver_handle: chatId, content: msg }) });
        } else {
          await fetch('/api/groups/messages', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ group_id: chatId, sender_handle: localStorage.getItem('sceau_handle'), content: msg }) });
        }
        window.location.href = '/messages';
      } else {
        setStatus("✅ Scellé !");
        setSealedResult({ title, hash: hashB64, date: new Date().toLocaleDateString('fr-FR'), isFlash: isFlashMode, ots: !!data.ots_secured });
      }
    } catch (e: any) {
      console.error(e);
      setStatus("❌ Erreur réseau : " + (e?.message || 'impossible de contacter le serveur'));
    }
  }

  if (sealedResult) {
    return (
      <div className="p-6 font-sans text-center flex flex-col items-center max-w-md mx-auto">
        <div className="text-6xl mb-4">{sealedResult.isFlash ? '⚡' : '✅'}</div>
        <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold mb-2" style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>C'est gravé.</h1>
        <div className="bg-[var(--bg-surface)] p-6 rounded-3xl border border-[var(--border-color)] w-full text-left shadow-lg mt-6">
          <h3 className="font-bold mb-2" style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>{sealedResult.title}</h3>
          <p className="text-[0.6rem] font-mono break-all text-[var(--text-muted)] bg-[var(--bg-main)] p-2 rounded border border-[var(--border-color)]">{sealedResult.hash}</p>
          <p className="text-xs text-right mt-3 text-[var(--text-muted)]">Scellé le {sealedResult.date}</p>
          {sealedResult.ots && <p className="text-xs mt-2 text-[#5D7A3A] font-bold">✅ Preuve horodatée</p>}
        </div>
        <button onClick={() => window.location.reload()} className="mt-8 bg-[#2C1810] text-white p-3 rounded-xl font-bold w-full">Sceller une autre œuvre</button>
        <Link href="/feed" className="mt-3 border-2 border-[var(--border-color)] text-[var(--text-main)] p-3 rounded-xl font-bold w-full text-center">Voir le Feed</Link>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="p-6 font-sans max-w-md mx-auto">

      {/* === ONGLETS SCeller / Vérifier === */}
      {!isPactMode && (
        <div className="flex justify-center mb-6">
          <div className="flex bg-[var(--bg-main)] p-1 rounded-xl border border-[var(--border-color)]">
            <Link href="/seal" className="px-6 py-2 rounded-lg font-bold text-sm bg-[var(--bg-surface)] shadow text-[#E76F51]">✍️ Sceller</Link>
            <Link href="/verify" className="px-6 py-2 rounded-lg font-bold text-sm opacity-50 hover:opacity-100 transition" style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>🔍 Vérifier</Link>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      <div className="text-center mb-6">
        <h1 className="text-4xl font-bold mb-2" style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>
          {isPactMode ? "Nouveau Pacte." : (isFlashMode ? "Flash." : "L'Atelier.")}
        </h1>
      </div>

      {/* === Toggle Normal / Flash === */}
      {!isPactMode && (
        <div className="flex bg-[var(--bg-main)] p-1 rounded-xl mb-4 border border-[var(--border-color)]">
          <button type="button" onClick={() => { setIsFlashMode(false); setMode('file'); setIsHosted(true); setVisibility('public'); }} className={`flex-1 py-2 rounded-lg font-bold text-sm transition ${!isFlashMode ? 'bg-[var(--bg-surface)] shadow text-[#E76F51]' : 'opacity-50'}`}>📁 Normal</button>
          <button type="button" onClick={() => { setIsFlashMode(true); setIsHosted(false); setVisibility('private'); }} className={`flex-1 py-2 rounded-lg font-bold text-sm transition ${isFlashMode ? 'bg-[#2C1810] text-white shadow' : 'opacity-50'}`}>⚡ Flash</button>
        </div>
      )}

      <div className="space-y-4 bg-[var(--bg-surface)] p-6 rounded-2xl border border-[var(--border-color)] shadow-sm">

        {/* === Sélecteur de mode === */}
        {!isPactMode && (
          <div className="flex bg-[var(--bg-main)] p-1 rounded-xl text-xs font-mono">
            <button type="button" onClick={() => setMode('file')} className={`flex-1 py-2 rounded-lg font-bold transition ${mode === 'file' ? 'bg-[var(--bg-surface)] shadow' : 'opacity-50'}`} style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>📁 Fichier</button>
            <button type="button" onClick={() => setMode('text')} className={`flex-1 py-2 rounded-lg font-bold transition ${mode === 'text' ? 'bg-[var(--bg-surface)] shadow' : 'opacity-50'}`} style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>✍️ Texte</button>
            <button type="button" onClick={() => setMode('media')} className={`flex-1 py-2 rounded-lg font-bold transition ${mode === 'media' ? 'bg-[var(--bg-surface)] shadow' : 'opacity-50'}`} style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>📸 Photo</button>
            <button type="button" onClick={() => setMode('audio')} className={`flex-1 py-2 rounded-lg font-bold transition ${mode === 'audio' ? 'bg-[var(--bg-surface)] shadow' : 'opacity-50'}`} style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>🎙️ Audio</button>
            <button type="button" onClick={() => setMode('project')} className={`flex-1 py-2 rounded-lg font-bold transition ${mode === 'project' ? 'bg-[#FDF4F0] text-[#C44A2E] shadow' : 'opacity-50'}`} style={{ color: mode === 'project' ? '' : 'var(--text-main)' }}>📁 Projet</button>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* === Titre === */}
        <div>
          <label className="text-xs font-mono font-bold uppercase" style={{ color: 'var(--text-muted)' }}>Titre</label>
          <input
            type="text"
            className="w-full border-2 border-[var(--border-color)] focus:border-[#E76F51] rounded-xl p-3 outline-none bg-transparent mt-1"
            style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}
            value={title}
            onChange={e => setTitle(e.target.value)}
            placeholder={isPactMode ? "Nom du pacte" : (isFlashMode ? "Ton idée en un mot" : "Titre de l'œuvre")}
          />
        </div>

        {/* === Pacte : fichiers === */}
        {isPactMode && (
          <div className="bg-[#FDF8F0] p-4 rounded-xl border border-[#EFE0CA]">
            <label className="text-xs font-mono font-bold block mb-2" style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>📁 Médias (multi-fichiers)</label>
            <input type="file" multiple className="w-full text-sm" onChange={e => setPactFiles(Array.from(e.target.files || []))} />
            {pactFiles.length > 0 && <p className="text-xs mt-1 text-[var(--text-muted)]">{pactFiles.length} fichier(s) sélectionné(s)</p>}
          </div>
        )}

        {/* === Description (sauf text) === */}
        {!isPactMode && mode !== 'text' && (
          <div>
            <label className="text-xs font-mono font-bold uppercase" style={{ color: 'var(--text-muted)' }}>Description</label>
            <textarea className="w-full border-2 border-[var(--border-color)] focus:border-[#E76F51] rounded-xl p-3 outline-none bg-transparent mt-1" rows={2} value={desc} onChange={e => setDesc(e.target.value)} />
          </div>
        )}

        {/* === Texte === */}
        {mode === 'text' && !isPactMode && (
          <div>
            <label className="text-xs font-mono font-bold uppercase" style={{ color: 'var(--text-muted)' }}>Texte à sceller</label>
            <textarea className="w-full border-2 border-[var(--border-color)] focus:border-[#E76F51] rounded-xl p-4 outline-none bg-transparent min-h-[100px] font-mono text-sm mt-1" value={textContent} onChange={e => setTextContent(e.target.value)} placeholder="Écris ton texte ici..." />
          </div>
        )}

        {/* === Fichier === */}
        {!isPactMode && mode === 'file' && (
          <div>
            <label className="text-xs font-mono font-bold uppercase" style={{ color: 'var(--text-muted)' }}>Fichier</label>
            <input type="file" className="w-full text-sm mt-1" onChange={e => setFile(e.target.files?.[0] || null)} />
            {file && <p className="text-xs mt-1 text-[var(--text-muted)]">📎 {file.name} ({(file.size / 1024).toFixed(0)} Ko)</p>}
          </div>
        )}
        
        {!isPactMode && mode === 'project' && (
          <div className="bg-[#FDF8F0] p-4 rounded-xl border border-[#EFE0CA]">
            <label className="text-xs font-mono font-bold uppercase block mb-2" style={{ color: '#A88620' }}>Dossier (Multi-fichiers)</label>
            <p className="text-xs text-[#A88620] mb-2 italic">SCEAU va créer un Arbre de Merkle et ancrer une seule preuve pour tout le dossier.</p>
            <input type="file" multiple className="w-full text-sm" onChange={e => setProjectFiles(Array.from(e.target.files || []))} />
            {projectFiles.length > 0 && <p className="text-xs mt-2 text-[#5D7A3A] font-bold">📎 {projectFiles.length} fichier(s) sélectionné(s)</p>}
          </div>
        )}

        {/* === Photo / vidéo === */}
        {!isPactMode && mode === 'media' && (
          <div>
            <label className="text-xs font-mono font-bold uppercase" style={{ color: 'var(--text-muted)' }}>Photo / Vidéo</label>
            <input type="file" accept="image/*,video/*" capture="environment" className="w-full text-sm mt-1" onChange={e => setFile(e.target.files?.[0] || null)} />
            {file && <p className="text-xs mt-1 text-[var(--text-muted)]">📎 {file.name}</p>}
          </div>
        )}

        {/* === Audio === */}
        {!isPactMode && mode === 'audio' && (
          <div>
            <label className="text-xs font-mono font-bold uppercase" style={{ color: 'var(--text-muted)' }}>Audio</label>
            <div className="mt-1">
              {!isRecording && !audioUrl && <button type="button" onClick={startRecording} className="w-full bg-red-500 text-white py-2 rounded-lg font-bold text-xs">🔴 Enregistrer</button>}
              {isRecording && <button type="button" onClick={stopRecording} className="w-full bg-[#2C1810] text-white py-2 rounded-lg font-bold text-xs animate-pulse">⏹ Arrêter</button>}
              {audioUrl && !isRecording && <audio controls src={audioUrl} className="w-full mt-2 h-10" />}
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* === Options (non-flash, non-pacte) === */}
        {!isFlashMode && !isPactMode && mode !== 'project' && (
          <div className="bg-[var(--bg-main)] p-4 rounded-xl border border-[var(--border-color)] space-y-3 mt-4">
            <div>
              <label className="text-[0.65rem] font-mono font-bold uppercase block mb-2" style={{ color: 'var(--text-muted)' }}>Visibilité</label>
              <div className="flex bg-[var(--bg-surface)] rounded-xl border border-[var(--border-color)] p-1">
                <button type="button" onClick={() => setVisibility('public')} className={`flex-1 py-2 text-xs font-bold rounded transition ${visibility === 'public' ? 'bg-[#F5F9EE] text-[#5D7A3A]' : 'opacity-50'}`}>🌍 Public</button>
                <button type="button" onClick={() => setVisibility('friends')} className={`flex-1 py-2 text-xs font-bold rounded transition ${visibility === 'friends' ? 'bg-[#F0F6FC] text-[#2D6A9E]' : 'opacity-50'}`}>👥 Amis</button>
                <button type="button" onClick={() => setVisibility('private')} className={`flex-1 py-2 text-xs font-bold rounded transition ${visibility === 'private' ? 'bg-[#FDF4F0] text-[#C44A2E]' : 'opacity-50'}`}>🔒 Privé</button>
              </div>
            </div>

            <label className="flex items-center gap-3 cursor-pointer">
              <input type="checkbox" checked={isHosted} onChange={e => setIsHosted(e.target.checked)} className="w-4 h-4 accent-[#E76F51]" />
              <span className="text-sm font-bold" style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>☁️ Héberger le fichier</span>
            </label>

            <label className="flex items-center gap-3 cursor-pointer">
              <input type="checkbox" checked={isMystery} onChange={e => setIsMystery(e.target.checked)} className="w-4 h-4 accent-[#E76F51]" />
              <span className="text-sm font-bold" style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>🎭 Sceau Mystère</span>
            </label>

            {isMystery && (
              <div className="pl-7">
                <label className="text-[0.6rem] font-mono text-[var(--text-muted)] block">Likes requis pour révéler</label>
                <input type="number" min="1" value={unlockLikes} onChange={e => setUnlockLikes(parseInt(e.target.value) || 1)} className="w-full border border-[var(--border-color)] p-2 rounded text-sm bg-[var(--bg-surface)] mt-1" style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }} />
              </div>
            )}

            <label className="flex items-center gap-3 cursor-pointer">
              <input type="checkbox" checked={isEphemeral} onChange={e => setIsEphemeral(e.target.checked)} className="w-4 h-4 accent-[#E76F51]" />
              <span className="text-sm font-bold" style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }}>⏳ Éphémère (24h)</span>
            </label>

            <div className="pt-2 border-t border-[var(--border-color)]">
              <label className="text-xs font-mono font-bold uppercase block mb-1" style={{ color: 'var(--text-muted)' }}>⏱️ Capsule Temporelle (optionnel)</label>
              <input type="date" value={revealAt} onChange={e => setRevealAt(e.target.value)} className="w-full border-2 border-[var(--border-color)] p-2 rounded-lg bg-transparent text-sm" style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }} />
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* === Pacte : signatures === */}
        {isPactMode && (
          <div>
            <label className="text-xs font-mono font-bold uppercase" style={{ color: 'var(--text-muted)' }}>Signatures requises</label>
            <input type="number" min="2" value={requiredSignatures} onChange={e => setRequiredSignatures(parseInt(e.target.value) || 2)} className="w-full border-2 border-[var(--border-color)] p-2 rounded-lg text-center font-bold text-lg bg-transparent mt-1" style={{ color: 'var(--text-main)' }} />
          </div>
        )}

        {/* === Bouton SCELLER === */}
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={handleSeal}
          className={`w-full p-4 rounded-xl font-bold text-white shadow-lg mt-2 ${isPactMode ? 'bg-[#4A90C2]' : (isFlashMode ? 'bg-[#2C1810]' : 'bg-gradient-to-r from-[#E76F51] to-[#F4A261]')}`}
        >
          {isPactMode ? 'CRÉER LE PACTE' : (isFlashMode ? 'GRAVER EN 1 CLIC' : 'SCELLER MON ŒUVRE')}
        </button>

        {/* === ✅ Affichage du statut — CE QUI MANQUAIT === */}
        {status && (
          <p className={`text-center font-bold text-sm p-3 rounded-lg border ${status.includes('✅') ? 'bg-[#F5F9EE] text-[#5D7A3A] border-[#A8C77E]' : status.includes('❌') ? 'bg-[#FDF4F0] text-[#C44A2E] border-[#FCE0D6]' : 'bg-[#F0F6FC] text-[#2D6A9E] border-[#C9D8E8]'}`}>
            {status}
          </p>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export default function Seal() {
  return <Suspense fallback={<div className="p-10 text-center font-mono">Chargement...</div>}><SealContent /></Suspense>;
}

FICHIER : ./src/app/settings/page.tsx

'use client';
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import Link from 'next/link';

export default function Settings() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [msg, setMsg] = useState('');

  const [oldHandle, setOldHandle] = useState('');
  const [newHandle, setNewHandle] = useState('');
  const [bio, setBio] = useState('');
  const [role, setRole] = useState('createur');
  const [openToCollab, setOpenToCollab] = useState(false);
  const [firstName, setFirstName] = useState('');
  const [lastName, setLastName] = useState('');
  const [birthDate, setBirthDate] = useState('');
  const [pubkey, setPubkey] = useState('');
  const [apiKeys, setApiKeys] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [newKeyName, setNewKeyName] = useState('');
  const [generatedKey, setGeneratedKey] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [isGeneratingKey, setIsGeneratingKey] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    const h = localStorage.getItem('sceau_handle');
    const p = localStorage.getItem('sceau_pubkey');
    if (!h || !p) { router.push('/login'); return; }

    setOldHandle(h);
    setNewHandle(h);
    setPubkey(p);

    fetch(`/api/profil?pubkey=${encodeURIComponent(p)}`)
      .then(r => r.json())
      .then(d => {
        if (d.handle) {
          setBio(d.bio || '');
          setRole(d.role || 'createur');
          setOpenToCollab(d.open_to_collaboration === true);
          setFirstName(d.first_name || '');
          setLastName(d.last_name || '');
          setBirthDate(d.birth_date ? d.birth_date.split('T')[0] : '');
        }
        setLoading(false);
      })
      .catch(() => setLoading(false));
  }, [router]);

  async function saveProfile() {
    setSaving(true); setMsg('');
    const res = await fetch('/api/profil/update', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        pubkey,
        old_handle: oldHandle,
        new_handle: newHandle,
        bio,
        role,
        open_to_collaboration: openToCollab,
        first_name: firstName,
        last_name: lastName,
        birth_date: birthDate,
      }),
    });
    const data = await res.json();

    if (res.ok) {
      setMsg('✅ Profil mis à jour !');
      localStorage.setItem('sceau_handle', data.new_handle);
      setOldHandle(data.new_handle);
    } else {
      setMsg('❌ ' + data.error);
    }
    setSaving(false);
  }

  async function loadApiKeys() {
    if (!pubkey) return;
    const res = await fetch(`/api/auth/api-keys?pubkey=${encodeURIComponent(pubkey)}`);
    if (res.ok) {
      const d = await res.json();
      setApiKeys(d.keys || []);
    }
  }

  useEffect(() => {
    if (pubkey) loadApiKeys();
  }, [pubkey]);

  async function generateApiKey() {
    if (!newKeyName.trim()) return alert("Donne un nom à ta clé (ex: WordPress, GitHub)");
    setIsGeneratingKey(true);
    const res = await fetch('/api/auth/api-keys', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ pubkey, name: newKeyName })
    });
    const data = await res.json();
    if (res.ok) {
      setGeneratedKey(data.key);
      setNewKeyName('');
      loadApiKeys();
    } else {
      alert('Erreur: ' + data.error);
    }
    setIsGeneratingKey(false);
  }

  function logout() {
    localStorage.clear();
    router.push('/login');
  }

  if (loading) return <div className="p-10 text-center font-mono">Chargement...</div>;

  return (
    <div className="p-6 max-w-2xl mx-auto font-sans">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-4 mb-8">
        <Link href="/profil" className="text-2xl opacity-50 hover:opacity-100 transition">←</Link>
        <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>Paramètres.</h1>
      </div>

      <div className="space-y-6">
        <div className="bg-[var(--bg-surface)] p-6 rounded-2xl border border-[var(--border-color)] shadow-sm">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-bold mb-4" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>👤 Identité publique</h2>

          <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-[var(--text-muted)] mb-1 font-mono">Pseudo</label>
          <input type="text" value={newHandle} onChange={e => setNewHandle(e.target.value.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9_-]/g, ''))} className="w-full border-2 border-[var(--border-color)] p-3 rounded-xl mb-4 bg-transparent" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}} />

          <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-[var(--text-muted)] mb-1 font-mono">Bio / Description</label>
          <textarea value={bio} onChange={e => setBio(e.target.value)} rows={3} className="w-full border-2 border-[var(--border-color)] p-3 rounded-xl mb-4 bg-transparent" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}} placeholder="Parle de toi en quelques mots..." />

          <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-[var(--text-muted)] mb-1 font-mono">Ton rôle</label>
          <select value={role} onChange={e => setRole(e.target.value)} className="w-full border-2 border-[var(--border-color)] p-3 rounded-xl bg-transparent mb-4" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>
            <option value="createur">Créateur / Artiste</option>
            <option value="freelance">Freelance</option>
            <option value="entreprise">Entreprise / Agence</option>
            <option value="etudiant">Étudiant</option>
          </select>

          <label className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-6 cursor-pointer bg-[#F0F6FC] p-3 rounded-xl border border-[#C9D8E8]">
            <input type="checkbox" checked={openToCollab} onChange={e => setOpenToCollab(e.target.checked)} className="w-4 h-4 accent-[#4A90C2]" />
            <span className="text-sm font-bold text-[#2D6A9E]">🤝 Ouvert aux collaborations / partenariats</span>
          </label>

          <button onClick={saveProfile} disabled={saving} className="w-full bg-[#E76F51] text-white py-3 rounded-xl font-bold disabled:opacity-50">
            {saving ? 'Sauvegarde...' : 'Sauvegarder les modifications'}
          </button>
          {msg && <p className="text-center font-bold mt-3" style={{color: msg.includes('✅') ? '#7A9B5A' : '#C44A2E'}}>{msg}</p>}
        </div>

        <div className="bg-[#F0F6FC] p-6 rounded-2xl border border-[#C9D8E8]">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-bold mb-2 text-[#2D6A9E]">🛡️ Identité de confiance (Privée)</h2>
          <p className="text-xs text-[#2D6A9E] mb-4 italic">Ces informations ne sont jamais visibles sur votre profil. Elles servent uniquement à prouver que ce compte vous appartient en cas de litige.</p>
          
          <div className="flex gap-4 mb-4">
            <div className="flex-1">
              <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-[#2D6A9E] mb-1 font-mono">Prénom</label>
              <input type="text" value={firstName} onChange={e => setFirstName(e.target.value)} className="w-full border-2 border-[#C9D8E8] p-3 rounded-xl bg-white focus:border-[#4A90C2] outline-none" />
            </div>
            <div className="flex-1">
              <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-[#2D6A9E] mb-1 font-mono">Nom</label>
              <input type="text" value={lastName} onChange={e => setLastName(e.target.value)} className="w-full border-2 border-[#C9D8E8] p-3 rounded-xl bg-white focus:border-[#4A90C2] outline-none" />
            </div>
          </div>
          
          <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-[#2D6A9E] mb-1 font-mono">Date de naissance</label>
          <input type="date" value={birthDate} onChange={e => setBirthDate(e.target.value)} className="w-full border-2 border-[#C9D8E8] p-3 rounded-xl bg-white focus:border-[#4A90C2] outline-none" />
        </div>

        <div className="bg-[#FDF8F0] p-6 rounded-2xl border border-[#EFE0CA]">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-bold mb-2" style={{color: '#A88620'}}>🔑 Clé cryptographique</h2>
          <p className="text-sm text-[#7A5A3A] mb-3">C'est l'empreinte de ton identité souveraine sur SCEAU. Garde-la secrète.</p>
          <div className="bg-white p-3 rounded-xl border border-[#EFE0CA] overflow-x-auto">
            <code className="text-xs font-mono text-gray-600 whitespace-nowrap">{pubkey}</code>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="bg-[#F0F6FC] p-6 rounded-2xl border border-[#C9D8E8]">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-bold mb-2 text-[#2D6A9E]">🔌 Clés API (Développeurs & Plugins)</h2>
          <p className="text-xs text-[#2D6A9E] mb-4 italic">Générez une clé pour connecter SCEAU à votre site WordPress, GitHub ou une application externe.</p>

          {generatedKey && (
            <div className="bg-[#FFFCF7] border border-[#E9C46A] p-4 rounded-xl mb-4">
              <p className="text-xs font-bold text-[#A88620] mb-1">⚠️ Copiez cette clé maintenant, elle ne sera plus jamais affichée :</p>
              <div className="flex gap-2">
                <code className="flex-1 bg-[var(--bg-main)] p-2 rounded text-xs break-all border border-[var(--border-color)]">{generatedKey}</code>
                <button onClick={() => { navigator.clipboard.writeText(generatedKey); alert('Copié !'); }} className="bg-[#E9C46A] text-white px-3 rounded font-bold text-xs">Copier</button>
              </div>
              <button onClick={() => setGeneratedKey(null)} className="text-xs text-[#A88620] font-bold mt-2 underline">J'ai copié la clé, la fermer.</button>
            </div>
          )}

          <div className="flex gap-2 mb-4">
            <input 
              type="text" 
              placeholder="Nom (ex: Mon site WordPress)" 
              className="flex-1 border-2 border-[#C9D8E8] p-2 rounded-xl bg-white focus:border-[#4A90C2] outline-none text-sm" 
              value={newKeyName} 
              onChange={e => setNewKeyName(e.target.value)} 
            />
            <button 
              onClick={generateApiKey} 
              disabled={isGeneratingKey}
              className="bg-[#4A90C2] text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-xl font-bold text-sm shadow-md disabled:opacity-50"
            >
              {isGeneratingKey ? '...' : 'Générer'}
            </button>
          </div>

          {apiKeys.length > 0 && (
            <div className="space-y-2 mt-4 border-t border-[#C9D8E8] pt-4">
              <p className="text-xs font-bold text-[#2D6A9E] mb-2">Vos clés actives :</p>
              {apiKeys.map(k => (
                <div key={k.id} className="flex justify-between items-center bg-white p-2 rounded-lg border border-[#C9D8E8] text-xs">
                  <span className="font-bold">{k.name}</span>
                  <span className="text-[#8A7A72]">
                    {k.last_used_at ? `Utilisée le ${new Date(k.last_used_at).toLocaleDateString('fr-FR')}` : 'Jamais utilisée'}
                  </span>
                </div>
              ))}

          )}

          <div className="mt-6 pt-6 border-t border-[#C9D8E8]">

            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3">

=========================================
FICHIER : ./src/app/signup/page.tsx

'use client';
import { useState } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { ed25519 } from '@noble/curves/ed25519';
import Link from 'next/link';

export default function Signup() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [handle, setHandle] = useState('');
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  const [showPassword, setShowPassword] = useState(false);
  const [status, setStatus] = useState('');

  async function handleSignup() {
    if (!handle || !email || !password) return setStatus("Tous les champs sont requis");
    setStatus("Création en cours...");
    
    try {
      const secret = crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(32));
      const pubkey = ed25519.getPublicKey(secret);
      let pubkeyStr = ''; for (let i = 0; i < pubkey.length; i++) pubkeyStr += String.fromCharCode(pubkey[i]);
      let secretStr = ''; for (let i = 0; i < secret.length; i++) secretStr += String.fromCharCode(secret[i]);
      const pubkeyB64 = btoa(pubkeyStr);

      localStorage.setItem('sceau_secret', btoa(secretStr));

      const res = await fetch('/api/auth/signup', { 
        method: 'POST', 
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, 
        body: JSON.stringify({ handle, email, password, pubkey: pubkeyB64 }) 
      });
      
      const data = await res.json();
      
      if (res.ok) {
        localStorage.setItem('sceau_pubkey', pubkeyB64);
        localStorage.setItem('sceau_handle', handle);
        setStatus("✅ Compte créé !");
        setTimeout(() => router.push('/feed'), 500);
      } else {
        setStatus("❌ " + (data.error || "Une erreur est survenue"));
      }
    } catch (e) { setStatus("❌ Impossible de contacter le serveur. Vérifiez votre connexion."); }
  }

  const handleKeyPress = (e: React.KeyboardEvent) => { if (e.key === 'Enter') handleSignup(); };

  return (
    <div className="max-w-md mx-auto bg-[var(--bg-surface)] p-8 rounded-3xl border border-[var(--border-color)] shadow-sm mt-10">
      <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold mb-6 font-serif" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>Créer un compte</h1>
      
      <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-[var(--text-muted)] mb-1 font-mono">Pseudo</label>
      <input type="text" onKeyDown={handleKeyPress} className="w-full border-2 border-[var(--border-color)] p-3 rounded-xl mb-4 bg-transparent" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}} value={handle} onChange={(e) => setHandle(e.target.value)} />
      
      <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-[var(--text-muted)] mb-1 font-mono">Email</label>
      <input type="email" onKeyDown={handleKeyPress} className="w-full border-2 border-[var(--border-color)] p-3 rounded-xl mb-4 bg-transparent" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}} value={email} onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)} />
      
      <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-[var(--text-muted)] mb-1 font-mono">Mot de passe</label>
      <div className="relative mb-6">
        <input type={showPassword ? "text" : "password"} onKeyDown={handleKeyPress} className="w-full border-2 border-[var(--border-color)] p-3 rounded-xl pr-10 bg-transparent" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}} value={password} onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)} />
        <button type="button" onClick={() => setShowPassword(!showPassword)} className="absolute right-3 top-3 opacity-50">{showPassword ? "🙈" : "👁️"}</button>
      </div>
      
      <button onClick={handleSignup} className="w-full bg-gradient-to-r from-[#E76F51] to-[#F4A261] text-white p-4 rounded-xl font-bold">S'inscrire</button>
      {status && <p className="mt-4 text-center font-bold text-[#E76F51]">{status}</p>}
      
      <p className="text-center mt-4 text-sm text-[var(--text-muted)]">Déjà un compte ? <Link href="/login" className="underline font-bold">Se connecter</Link></p>
    </div>
  );
}

FICHIER : ./src/app/u/[handle]/page.tsx
