'use client';
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { useParams } from 'next/navigation';
import Link from 'next/link';

export default function PublicProfile() {
  const params = useParams();
  const [data, setData] = useState<any>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [myHandle, setMyHandle] = useState<string | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    const h = localStorage.getItem('sceau_handle');
    setMyHandle(h);
    if (params.handle) {
      fetch(`/api/user-profile/${params.handle}?viewer=${h || ''}`)
        .then(r => r.json())
        .then(d => { setData(d); setLoading(false); });
    }
  }, [params.handle]);

  async function addFriend() {
    await fetch('/api/friends', { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify({ me: myHandle, target: data.handle }) });
    alert(`Demande d'ami envoyée à @${data.handle} !`);
    // Recharger pour mettre à jour le bouton
    window.location.reload();
  }

  // Couleurs par catégorie pour rendre la grille vivante
  const getCategoryStyle = (category: string) => {
    switch (category) {
      case 'Art': return 'border-[#E9C46A] bg-[#FDF8F0]';
      case 'Musique': return 'border-[#4A90C2] bg-[#F0F6FC]';
      case 'Code': return 'border-[#2A9D8F] bg-[#F0F9F7]';
      case 'Écrit': return 'border-[#A78BC8] bg-[#F5F0FA]';
      default: return 'border-[var(--border-color)] bg-[var(--bg-surface)]';
    }
  };

  if (loading) return <div className="p-10 text-center font-mono">Chargement du profil...</div>;
  if (data?.error) return <div className="p-10 text-center font-mono text-red-500">{data.error}</div>;

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-[var(--bg-main)] pb-20 font-serif">
      
      {/* EN-TÊTE DU PROFIL PUBLIC */}
      <div className="bg-[var(--bg-surface)] border-b border-[var(--border-color)] pt-12 pb-8 px-6 text-center relative">
        <div className="w-28 h-28 rounded-full bg-gradient-to-br from-[#E76F51] to-[#F4A261] text-white flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-4xl mx-auto mb-4 shadow-xl">
          {data.handle[0].toUpperCase()}
        </div>
        <h1 className="text-4xl font-bold mb-1" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>@{data.handle}</h1>
        {data.open_to_collaboration && (
          <span className="inline-block mt-2 bg-[#F0F6FC] text-[#2D6A9E] border border-[#C9D8E8] px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-bold">
            🤝 Ouvert aux collaborations
          </span>
        )}
        <p className="text-sm font-mono text-[var(--text-muted)] mb-4">{data.role || 'Créateur sur SCEAU'}</p>
        
        {myHandle !== data.handle && (
          <div className="mb-4">
            {data.relationship === 'friend' && <span className="bg-green-100 text-green-800 px-4 py-2 rounded-full font-bold text-sm">✅ Vous êtes amis</span>}
            {data.relationship === 'pending' && <span className="bg-yellow-100 text-yellow-800 px-4 py-2 rounded-full font-bold text-sm">⏳ Demande en attente</span>}
            {data.relationship === 'none' && <button onClick={addFriend} className="bg-[#E76F51] text-white px-8 py-2 rounded-xl font-bold shadow-md hover:bg-[#F4A261] transition">+ Ajouter à mon cercle</button>}
          </div>
        )}

        <div className="flex justify-center gap-6 text-sm font-mono font-bold text-[var(--text-muted)]">
          <span>{data.seals.length} Œuvres</span>
          <span>{data.seals.filter((s:any)=>s.is_hosted).length} Fichiers</span>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* LA VITRINE (GRILLE) */}
      <div className="max-w-4xl mx-auto px-6 mt-8">
        <h2 className="text-xl font-bold mb-4 flex items-center gap-2" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>
          🖼️ Galerie Publique
        </h2>
        
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
          {data.seals.map((seal: any) => (
            <Link href={`/o/${seal.id}`} key={seal.id} className={`block p-5 rounded-2xl border-2 shadow-sm hover:shadow-md hover:-translate-y-1 transition duration-200 ${getCategoryStyle(seal.category)}`}>
              
              <div className="flex justify-between items-start mb-3">
                <span className="text-xs font-mono font-bold italic opacity-60" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>
                  {seal.category}
                </span>
                {seal.is_hosted ? <span title="Fichier hébergé">📁</span> : <span title="Preuve seule">🔒</span>}
              </div>

              <h3 className="font-bold text-lg mb-2 leading-tight" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>{seal.title}</h3>
              
              {seal.description && (
                <p className="text-xs italic opacity-70 line-clamp-2 mb-3" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>
                  "{seal.description}"
                </p>
              )}

              <div className="text-[0.65rem] font-mono opacity-50 border-t border-black/5 pt-2 mt-auto" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>
                {new Date(seal.timestamp).toLocaleDateString('fr-FR')}
              </div>
            </Link>
          ))}
          
          {data.seals.length === 0 && (
            <div className="col-span-full text-center py-10 bg-[var(--bg-surface)] rounded-2xl border border-[var(--border-color)]">
              <p className="text-[var(--text-muted)] italic">Ce créateur n'a pas encore publié d'œuvre publique.</p>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

=========================================
FICHIER : ./src/app/verify/page.tsx
=========================================

'use client';
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import Link from 'next/link'; // L'IMPORT MANQUANT EST ICI
import { sha256 } from '@noble/hashes/sha256';

export default function Verify() {
  const [tab, setTab] = useState('file');
  const [result, setResult] = useState<any>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [titleQuery, setTitleQuery] = useState('');
  
  const [timelineData, setTimelineData] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [timelineLoading, setTimelineLoading] = useState(true);
  const [timelineMode, setTimelineMode] = useState<'live' | 'period'>('live');
  const [startDate, setStartDate] = useState('');
  const [endDate, setEndDate] = useState('');

  useEffect(() => {
    if (tab !== 'timeline') return;
    const fetchTimeline = () => {
      setTimelineLoading(true);
      const url = timelineMode === 'live' 
        ? '/api/verify/timeline?live=true'
        : `/api/verify/timeline?start=${startDate}&end=${endDate}`;
        
      fetch(url).then(res => res.json()).then(data => {
        setTimelineData(data.timeline || []);
        setTimelineLoading(false);
      });
    };
    fetchTimeline();
    if (timelineMode === 'live') {
      const interval = setInterval(fetchTimeline, 5000);
      return () => clearInterval(interval);
    }
  }, [tab, timelineMode, startDate, endDate]);

  async function verifyFile(file: File) {
    setLoading(true);
    const buffer = await file.arrayBuffer();
    const hash = sha256(new Uint8Array(buffer));
    let hashStr = ''; for (let i = 0; i < hash.length; i++) hashStr += String.fromCharCode(hash[i]);
    const res = await fetch('/api/verify', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ hash: btoa(hashStr) }) });
    const data = await res.json(); setResult(data); setLoading(false);
  }

  async function verifyHash(input: string) {
    if(!input) return; setLoading(true);
    const res = await fetch('/api/verify', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ hash: input }) });
    const data = await res.json(); setResult(data); setLoading(false);
  }

  async function searchByTitle() {
    if(!titleQuery) return; setLoading(true);
    const res = await fetch('/api/verify', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ query: titleQuery }) });
    const data = await res.json(); setResult(data); setLoading(false);
  }

  const timeAgo = (dateStr: string) => {
    const diff = Math.floor((new Date().getTime() - new Date(dateStr).getTime()) / 60000);
    if (diff < 1) return "À l'instant";
    if (diff === 1) return "Il y a 1 minute";
    if (diff < 60) return `Il y a ${diff} minutes`;
    return new Date(dateStr).toLocaleDateString('fr-FR');
  };

  return (
    <div className="p-6 max-w-2xl mx-auto font-sans">
      <div className="flex justify-center mb-6">
        <div className="flex bg-[var(--bg-main)] p-1 rounded-xl border border-[var(--border-color)]">
          <Link href="/seal" className="px-6 py-2 rounded-lg font-bold text-sm opacity-50 hover:opacity-100 transition">✍️ Sceller</Link>
          <Link href="/verify" className="px-6 py-2 rounded-lg font-bold text-sm bg-[var(--bg-surface)] shadow text-[#E76F51]">🔍 Vérifier</Link>
        </div>
      </div>

      <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold mb-2 text-center" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>Vérifier & Découvrir.</h1>
      <p className="text-[var(--text-muted)] italic mb-6 text-center">Prouvez l'authenticité ou découvrez le monde.</p>
      
      <div className="flex bg-[var(--bg-surface)] border border-[var(--border-color)] p-1 rounded-xl mb-6">
        <button onClick={()=>{setTab('file');setResult(null)}} className={`flex-1 py-2 text-xs font-mono font-bold rounded-lg ${tab==='file'?'bg-[var(--bg-main)] shadow':'opacity-50'}`} style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>Fichier</button>
        <button onClick={()=>{setTab('hash');setResult(null)}} className={`flex-1 py-2 text-xs font-mono font-bold rounded-lg ${tab==='hash'?'bg-[var(--bg-main)] shadow':'opacity-50'}`} style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>Hash</button>
        <button onClick={()=>{setTab('title');setResult(null)}} className={`flex-1 py-2 text-xs font-mono font-bold rounded-lg ${tab==='title'?'bg-[var(--bg-main)] shadow':'opacity-50'}`} style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>Titre</button>
        <button onClick={()=>{setTab('timeline');setResult(null)}} className={`flex-1 py-2 text-xs font-mono font-bold rounded-lg ${tab==='timeline'?'bg-[#E76F51] text-white shadow':'opacity-50'}`} style={{color: tab==='timeline' ? '#FFF' : 'var(--text-main)'}}>📅 Frise</button>
      </div>

      {tab === 'file' && (
        <div className="border-2 border-dashed border-[#4A90C2] bg-[var(--bg-surface)] p-8 rounded-xl text-center">
          <input type="file" onChange={e=>e.target.files?.[0] && verifyFile(e.target.files[0])} className="w-full text-sm" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}} />
        </div>
      )}

      {tab === 'hash' && (
        <div className="space-y-4">
          <input type="text" placeholder="Collez le hash (ex: b2e7...)" className="w-full border-2 border-[var(--border-color)] p-3 rounded-xl outline-none font-mono bg-transparent" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}} onChange={e=>verifyHash(e.target.value)} />
        </div>
      )}

      {tab === 'title' && (
        <div className="space-y-4">
          <input type="text" placeholder="Titre de l'œuvre (ex: Mon premier roman)" className="w-full border-2 border-[var(--border-color)] p-3 rounded-xl outline-none bg-transparent" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}} value={titleQuery} onChange={e=>setTitleQuery(e.target.value)} onKeyDown={e => e.key === 'Enter' && searchByTitle()} />
          <button onClick={searchByTitle} className="w-full bg-[#4A90C2] text-white py-3 rounded-xl font-bold">Rechercher</button>
        </div>
      )}

      {tab === 'timeline' && (
        <div className="space-y-5">
          
          {/* Filtres de la frise */}
          <div className="bg-[var(--bg-surface)] border border-[var(--border-color)] rounded-xl p-3 flex flex-col gap-3">
            <div className="flex bg-[var(--bg-main)] p-1 rounded-lg">
              <button onClick={() => setTimelineMode('live')} className={`flex-1 py-1.5 text-xs font-bold rounded-md transition ${timelineMode === 'live' ? 'bg-white shadow text-[#E76F51]' : 'opacity-50'}`}>En direct</button>
              <button onClick={() => setTimelineMode('period')} className={`flex-1 py-1.5 text-xs font-bold rounded-md transition ${timelineMode === 'period' ? 'bg-white shadow text-[#E76F51]' : 'opacity-50'}`}>Par période</button>
            </div>
            
            {timelineMode === 'period' && (
              <div className="flex gap-2">
                <input type="date" value={startDate} onChange={e => setStartDate(e.target.value)} className="flex-1 border border-[var(--border-color)] rounded-lg p-2 text-xs bg-transparent" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}} />
                <input type="date" value={endDate} onChange={e => setEndDate(e.target.value)} className="flex-1 border border-[var(--border-color)] rounded-lg p-2 text-xs bg-transparent" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}} />
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          {timelineLoading ? (
            <p className="text-center font-mono text-[var(--text-muted)] py-10">Synchronisation...</p>
          ) : timelineData.length === 0 ? (
            <p className="text-center italic text-[var(--text-muted)] py-10">Aucun sceau sur cette période.</p>
          ) : (
            <div className="relative pl-6">
              {/* La ligne verticale */}
              <div className="absolute left-2.5 top-2 bottom-0 w-0.5 bg-[#E76F51] opacity-30"></div>

              <div className="space-y-6">
                {timelineData.map((item, index) => (
                  <div key={item.id} className="relative animate-fade-in">
                    {/* Le point */}
                    <div className="absolute -left-[1.35rem] top-1.5 w-3 h-3 rounded-full bg-[#E76F51] border-2 border-[var(--bg-main)] shadow-sm"></div>
                    
                    <div className="bg-[var(--bg-surface)] border border-[var(--border-color)] p-3 rounded-xl shadow-sm hover:border-[#E76F51] transition">
                      <div className="flex justify-between items-start mb-1">
                        <span className="font-bold text-xs" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>@{item.author}</span>
                        <span className="text-[0.6rem] font-mono text-[var(--text-muted)]">{timeAgo(item.timestamp)}</span>
                      </div>
                      <div className="font-bold text-sm" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>{item.title}</div>
                      <div className="text-[0.65rem] font-mono text-[var(--text-muted)] mt-1">{item.category}</div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      )}

      {tab !== 'pulse' && result && (
        <div className={`mt-8 p-6 rounded-2xl border-2 ${result.found ? 'bg-[var(--bg-surface)] border-[#7A9B5A]' : 'bg-[#FDF4F0] border-[#D64545]'}`}>
          {result.found ? (
            <div>
              <div className="text-center mb-6">
                <div className="text-3xl mb-2">✅</div>
                <h2 className="font-bold text-xl" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>{tab === 'title' ? 'ŒUVRES TROUVÉES' : 'FICHIER AUTHENTIQUE'}</h2>
                <p className="text-sm text-[var(--text-muted)]">{result.seals.length} preuve(s)</p>
              </div>
              <div className="space-y-4">
                {result.seals.map((seal: any, index: number) => (
                  <div key={index} className={`p-4 rounded-xl border ${index === 0 ? 'border-[#E76F51] bg-[var(--bg-main)]' : 'border-[var(--border-color)]'}`}>
                    <div className="flex justify-between items-start">
                      <div>
                        <div className="font-bold" style={{color: 'var(--text-main)'}}>{seal.title}</div>
                        <div className="text-xs text-[var(--text-muted)] mt-1">
                          Par <span className="font-bold text-[#E76F51]">@{seal.author}</span> le {new Date(seal.timestamp).toLocaleString('fr-FR')}
                        </div>
                        {seal.type === 'project' && (
                          <div className="mt-2 inline-block bg-[#F0F6FC] border border-[#C9D8E8] text-[#2D6A9E] px-2 py-1 rounded text-[0.65rem] font-bold">
                            🛡️ Fait partie du dossier : {seal.project_title}
                          </div>
                        )}
                        {seal.hash && <div className="text-[0.65rem] font-mono text-[var(--text-muted)] mt-2 break-all">Hash: {seal.hash}</div>}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                    {seal.is_hosted && seal.file_path && (
                      <a href={seal.file_path} target="_blank" className="mt-3 inline-block bg-[#4A90C2] text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold">⬇️ Télécharger l'œuvre</a>
                    )}
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <p className="text-center font-bold text-[#A02E2E]">❌ Aucune œuvre trouvée.</p>
          )}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

=========================================
FICHIER : ./src/db/pool.ts
=========================================

import { Pool } from 'pg';
import { requireDb } from '@/lib/env';

let _pool: Pool | null = null;

function create(): Pool {
  const url = requireDb();
  const pool = new Pool({
    connectionString: url,
    max: 5,
    idleTimeoutMillis: 30_000,
    connectionTimeoutMillis: 10_000,
    ssl: { rejectUnauthorized: true },
  });
  pool.on('error', (err) => {
    console.error('[pg] idle client error:', err.message);
  });
  return pool;
}

export function getPool(): Pool {
  if (!_pool) _pool = create();
  return _pool;
}

// Compat : `import { pool } from '@/db/pool'` continue de fonctionner.
export const pool = new Proxy({} as Pool, {
  get(_t, prop) {
    const real = getPool() as any;
    const v = real[prop];
    return typeof v === 'function' ? v.bind(real) : v;
  },
});

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS users (
  id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  handle TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
  email TEXT UNIQUE,
  password_hash TEXT,
  pubkey BYTEA NOT NULL,
  recovery_email TEXT,
  reset_token TEXT,
  reset_expiry TIMESTAMPTZ,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS seals (
  id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  hash BYTEA NOT NULL,
  timestamp TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,
  author_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id),
  title TEXT,
  description TEXT,
  category TEXT DEFAULT 'Art',
  is_public BOOLEAN DEFAULT true,
  is_hosted BOOLEAN DEFAULT false,
  file_path TEXT,
  file_size INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
  CONSTRAINT unique_seal UNIQUE (hash, author_id)
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS messages (
  id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  sender_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  receiver_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  content TEXT NOT NULL,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS likes (
  seal_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES seals(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  user_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  PRIMARY KEY (seal_id, user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS comments (
  id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  seal_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES seals(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  user_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  content TEXT NOT NULL,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS pacts (
  id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  title TEXT NOT NULL,
  description TEXT,
  creator_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id),
  file_path TEXT,
  status TEXT DEFAULT 'en_attente',
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS pact_signatures (
  pact_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES pacts(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  user_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
  PRIMARY KEY (pact_id, user_id)
);

-- Journal des accès
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS access_log (
  id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  seal_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES seals(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  viewer_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  action TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (action IN ('view','download')),
  context TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'public',
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()
);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_access_seal ON access_log(seal_id, created_at DESC);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_access_viewer ON access_log(viewer_id);

=========================================
FICHIER : ./src/lib/api-error.ts

import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';

function isDbError(e: any): boolean {
  const code = e?.code;
  if (['ECONNREFUSED', 'ENOTFOUND', 'ETIMEDOUT', '28P01', '3D000', '57P01'].includes(code)) return true;
  return /ECONNREFUSED|ENOTFOUND|timeout|connect.*pool|database/i.test(String(e?.message || ''));
}

export function apiError(e: unknown, fallback = 'Erreur serveur'): NextResponse {
  if (isDbError(e)) {
    console.error('[api-error] base injoignable :', (e as any)?.message);
    return NextResponse.json(
      { error: 'Base de données injoignable. Réessayez dans un instant.' },
      { status: 503 }
    );
  }
  console.error('[api-error]', e);
  return NextResponse.json({ error: fallback }, { status: 500 });
}

// Validation centralisée des variables d'environnement.
// Échoue TÔT avec un message clair au lieu de produire `undefined/undefined/clé`.

function clean(v: string | undefined): string | undefined {
  if (!v) return undefined;
  return v.trim().replace(/^['"]|['"]$/g, '');
}

function required(name: string): string {
  const v = clean(process.env[name]);
  if (!v) throw new Error(`Variable d'environnement manquante ou vide : ${name}`);
  return v;
}

function optional(name: string, def = ''): string {
  return clean(process.env[name]) ?? def;
}

export function requireS3(): { endpoint: string; bucket: string; region: string } {
  const endpoint = required('AWS_ENDPOINT_URL_S3').replace(/\/$/, '');
  const bucket = required('S3_BUCKET').replace(/^\/+|\/+$/g, '');
  const region = optional('AWS_REGION', 'us-west-004');
  return { endpoint, bucket, region };
}

export function requireDb(): string {
  return required('DATABASE_URL');
}

FICHIER : ./src/lib/merkle.ts
=========================================

import { sha256 } from '@noble/hashes/sha256';

/**
 * Prend une liste de hashs (en Base64) et calcule le Hash Racine (Merkle Root).
 * Utilise Uint8Array pour éviter les conflits de types avec Buffer.
 */
export function buildMerkleRoot(hashesB64: string[]): string {
  if (hashesB64.length === 0) return '';
  if (hashesB64.length === 1) return hashesB64[0];

  // Convertir les hashs Base64 en Uint8Array
  let currentLevel: Uint8Array[] = hashesB64.map(h => {
    const binaryString = atob(h);
    const bytes = new Uint8Array(binaryString.length);
    for (let i = 0; i < binaryString.length; i++) {
      bytes[i] = binaryString.charCodeAt(i);
    }
    return bytes;
  });

  while (currentLevel.length > 1) {
    const nextLevel: Uint8Array[] = [];
    
    for (let i = 0; i < currentLevel.length; i += 2) {
      if (i + 1 < currentLevel.length) {
        // Concaténer les deux hashs
        const combined = new Uint8Array(currentLevel[i].length + currentLevel[i + 1].length);
        combined.set(currentLevel[i], 0);
        combined.set(currentLevel[i + 1], currentLevel[i].length);
        
        // Calculer le hash du résultat
        const hash = sha256(combined);
        nextLevel.push(hash);
      } else {
        // S'il reste un élément impair, on le passe au niveau supérieur
        nextLevel.push(currentLevel[i]);
      }
    }
    currentLevel = nextLevel;
  }

  // Convertir le Uint8Array final en Base64
  const root = currentLevel[0];
  let binary = '';
  const len = root.byteLength;
  for (let i = 0; i < len; i++) {
    binary += String.fromCharCode(root[i]);
  }
  
  return btoa(binary);
}

FICHIER : ./src/lib/ots.ts
=========================================

// En-tête .ots complet (31 octets) :
// \x00 + "OpenTimestamps" (14) + \x00\x00 + "Proof" (5) + \x00 + 8 octets magiques
const HEADER_MAGIC = Buffer.from(
  '004f70656e54696d657374616d7073000050726f6f6600bf89e2e884e89294',
  'hex'
);

const CALENDARS = [
  'https://alice.btc.calendar.opentimestamps.org',
  'https://bob.btc.calendar.opentimestamps.org',
  'https://finney.calendar.eternitywall.com',
];

const CALENDAR_TIMEOUT_MS = 8_000;
const MAX_PROOF_BYTES = 64 * 1024;

export type OtsResult = {
  ok: boolean;
  proof?: Buffer;
  calendars: string[];
  error?: string;
};

function toDetachedOts(hash: Uint8Array, calendarBody: Buffer): Buffer {
  if (calendarBody.length >= HEADER_MAGIC.length &&
      calendarBody.subarray(0, HEADER_MAGIC.length).equals(HEADER_MAGIC)) {
    return calendarBody;
  }
  return Buffer.concat([
    HEADER_MAGIC,
    Buffer.from([0x01, 0x08]), // version 1 + op SHA-256
    Buffer.from(hash),
    calendarBody,
  ]);
}

async function submitOne(url: string, hash: Uint8Array): Promise<Buffer> {
  const res = await fetch(`${url}/digest`, {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: {
      'Content-Type': 'application/octet-stream',
      Accept: 'application/vnd.opentimestamps.v1',
      'User-Agent': 'sceau/1.0',
    },
    body: Buffer.from(hash),
    signal: AbortSignal.timeout(CALENDAR_TIMEOUT_MS),
    cache: 'no-store',
  });
  if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`HTTP ${res.status}`);
  const buf = Buffer.from(await res.arrayBuffer());
  if (buf.length === 0 || buf.length > MAX_PROOF_BYTES) throw new Error('taille invalide');
  if (buf[0] === 0x3c || buf[0] === 0x7b) throw new Error('réponse non binaire (HTML/JSON)');
  return buf;
}

export async function submitToOts(hash: Uint8Array): Promise<OtsResult> {
  if (hash.length !== 32) return { ok: false, calendars: [], error: 'hash_invalid_length' };

  const settled = await Promise.allSettled(CALENDARS.map((u) => submitOne(u, hash)));

  const successes: string[] = [];
  let body: Buffer | undefined;
  settled.forEach((r, i) => {
    if (r.status === 'fulfilled') {
      successes.push(CALENDARS[i]);
      if (!body) body = r.value;
    }
  });

  if (!body) return { ok: false, calendars: successes, error: 'all_calendars_failed' };
  return { ok: true, proof: toDetachedOts(hash, body), calendars: successes };
}

// Upgrade : interroge les calendars avec le hash de l'ENGAGEMENT contenu dans la preuve
// (pas le hash du fichier — sinon ça ne confirme jamais).
export async function upgradeOts(hash: Uint8Array, proofBase64: string): Promise<Buffer | null> {
  let engagement: Uint8Array = hash;
  try {
    const raw = Buffer.from(proofBase64, 'base64');
    if (raw.length > HEADER_MAGIC.length + 34 &&
        raw.subarray(0, HEADER_MAGIC.length).equals(HEADER_MAGIC)) {
      const version = raw[HEADER_MAGIC.length];
      const op = raw[HEADER_MAGIC.length + 1];
      if (version === 0x01 && op === 0x08) {
        engagement = raw.subarray(HEADER_MAGIC.length + 2, HEADER_MAGIC.length + 34);
      }
    }
  } catch { /* fallback hash */ }

  const hashHex = Buffer.from(engagement).toString('hex');
  const settled = await Promise.allSettled(
    CALENDARS.map(async (url) => {
      const res = await fetch(`${url}/timestamp/${hashHex}`, {
        signal: AbortSignal.timeout(CALENDAR_TIMEOUT_MS),
        cache: 'no-store',
      });
      if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`HTTP ${res.status}`);
      const buf = Buffer.from(await res.arrayBuffer());
      if (buf.length === 0 || buf.length > MAX_PROOF_BYTES) throw new Error('taille invalide');
      return buf;
    })
  );

  let best: Buffer | null = null;
  for (const r of settled) {
    if (r.status === 'fulfilled' && (!best || r.value.length > best.length)) best = r.value;
  }
  return best;
}

import { jsPDF } from 'jspdf';

const APP_URL = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_URL || 'https://sceau-app.vercel.app';

export function generateCertificate(
  title: string,
  author: string,
  date: string,
  hashBase64: string,
  qrCodeDataUrl: string,
  hashHex?: string,
  sealId?: string
) {
  // Convertir en hex si non fourni
  const hash = hashHex || (() => {
    try {
      return Buffer.from(hashBase64, 'base64').toString('hex');
    } catch {
      return hashBase64;
    }
  })();

  const hashChunks = hash.toUpperCase().match(/.{1,32}/g) || [hash];

  const doc = new jsPDF({ orientation: 'landscape', unit: 'mm', format: 'a4' });

  // Fond et cadre
  doc.setFillColor(253, 248, 240);
  doc.rect(0, 0, 297, 210, 'F');
  doc.setDrawColor(231, 111, 81);
  doc.setLineWidth(2);
  doc.rect(10, 10, 277, 190);

  // Titre
  doc.setFont('helvetica', 'bold');
  doc.setFontSize(32);
  doc.setTextColor(44, 24, 16);
  doc.text("CERTIFICAT D'ANTÉRIORITÉ", 148.5, 38, { align: 'center' });

  doc.setFontSize(14);
  doc.setTextColor(122, 90, 58);
  doc.text("Délivré par le protocole SCEAU", 148.5, 48, { align: 'center' });

  // Métadonnées
  doc.setFontSize(13);
  doc.setTextColor(44, 24, 16);
  doc.text(`Titre : ${title}`, 25, 68);
  doc.text(`Créateur : @${author}`, 25, 76);
  doc.text(`Date de scellement : ${new Date(date).toLocaleString('fr-FR')}`, 25, 84);

  // Hash hex complet
  doc.setFont('helvetica', 'bold');
  doc.setFontSize(10);
  doc.setTextColor(44, 24, 16);
  doc.text("EMPREINTE SHA-256 (hexadécimal) :", 25, 96);

  doc.setFont('courier', 'normal');
  doc.setFontSize(8);
  doc.setTextColor(90, 75, 68);
  hashChunks.forEach((chunk: string, i: number) => {
    doc.text(chunk, 25, 104 + i * 5);
  });

  // Mention OTS
  doc.setFont('helvetica', 'bold');
  doc.setFontSize(10);
  doc.setTextColor(93, 122, 58);
  doc.text("Ancrage temporel : OpenTimestamps (blockchain Bitcoin)", 25, 145);

  doc.setFont('helvetica', 'normal');
  doc.setFontSize(8);
  doc.setTextColor(122, 90, 58);
  doc.text("La preuve .ots est téléchargeable depuis la page publique de l'œuvre", 25, 152);
  doc.text("et vérifiable indépendamment sur https://opentimestamps.org", 25, 158);

  // QR code
  if (qrCodeDataUrl) {
    doc.addImage(qrCodeDataUrl, 'PNG', 215, 60, 60, 60);
    doc.setFontSize(8);
    doc.setTextColor(122, 90, 58);
    doc.text("Scannez pour vérifier en ligne", 245, 125, { align: 'center' });
  }

  // Lien de vérification
  if (sealId) {
    doc.setFontSize(8);
    doc.setTextColor(74, 144, 194);
    const url = `${APP_URL}/o/${sealId}`;
    doc.textWithLink(`Vérifier : ${url}`, 25, 175, { url });
  }

  // Mention légale
  doc.setFont('helvetica', 'italic');
  doc.setFontSize(7);
  doc.setTextColor(150, 150, 150);
  doc.text(
    "Document généré par SCEAU. Le hash SHA-256 est infalsifiable : toute modification, même d'un seul bit, change le hash.",
    25,
    188
  );

  doc.save(`SCEAU-${title.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9]/g, '-')}.pdf`);
}

export function generatePortfolioPDF(
  author: string,
  seals: any[],
  startDate?: string,
  endDate?: string,
  isPro: boolean = false
) {
  const doc = new jsPDF({ orientation: 'portrait', unit: 'mm', format: 'a4' });

  doc.setFillColor(245, 233, 216);
  doc.rect(0, 0, 210, 297, 'F');
  doc.setFont('helvetica', 'bold');
  doc.setFontSize(36);
  doc.setTextColor(231, 111, 81);
  doc.text('PORTFOLIO DE PREUVE', 105, 110, { align: 'center' });

  doc.setFontSize(22);
  doc.setTextColor(44, 24, 16);
  doc.text(`@${author}`, 105, 128, { align: 'center' });

  doc.setFontSize(11);
  doc.setTextColor(122, 90, 58);
  let dateText = `Généré le ${new Date().toLocaleDateString('fr-FR')}`;
  if (startDate && endDate) {
    dateText += ` | Période : du ${new Date(startDate).toLocaleDateString('fr-FR')} au ${new Date(endDate).toLocaleDateString('fr-FR')}`;
  }
  doc.text(dateText, 105, 142, { align: 'center' });
  doc.text(`${seals.length} œuvre(s) certifiée(s)`, 105, 150, { align: 'center' });

  doc.setFontSize(9);
  doc.text(`Vérification : ${APP_URL}/o/[id]`, 105, 162, { align: 'center' });

  doc.addPage();
  let y = 20;

  seals.forEach((seal) => {
    if (y > 230) {
      doc.addPage();
      y = 20;
    }

    doc.setDrawColor(239, 224, 202);
    doc.setLineWidth(0.5);
    doc.rect(15, y, 180, 65);

    doc.setFont('helvetica', 'bold');
    doc.setFontSize(13);
    doc.setTextColor(44, 24, 16);
    doc.text(seal.title || 'Sans titre', 20, y + 10);

    doc.setFont('helvetica', 'normal');
    doc.setFontSize(9);
    doc.setTextColor(122, 90, 58);
    const dateStr = new Date(seal.timestamp).toLocaleString('fr-FR');
    doc.text(`Scellé le : ${dateStr}`, 20, y + 18);

    if (seal.description) {
      doc.setFont('helvetica', 'italic');
      const splitDesc = doc.splitTextToSize(`"${seal.description}"`, 170);
      doc.text(splitDesc.slice(0, 2), 20, y + 26);
    }

    if (seal.has_ots_proof || seal.ots_proof) {
      doc.setFont('helvetica', 'bold');
      doc.setFontSize(8);
      doc.setTextColor(93, 122, 58);
      doc.text('✓ Ancré via OpenTimestamps (Bitcoin)', 20, y + 42);
    }

    doc.setTextColor(74, 144, 194);
    doc.setFontSize(8);
    doc.textWithLink('[ Vérifier en ligne ]', 20, y + 52, { url: `${APP_URL}/o/${seal.id}` });

    if (seal.is_hosted && seal.file_path) {
      doc.textWithLink('[ Ouvrir le fichier original ]', 110, y + 52, { url: seal.file_path });
    }

    y += 75;
  });

  if (!isPro) {
    const pageCount = doc.getNumberOfPages();
    for (let i = 1; i <= pageCount; i++) {
      doc.setPage(i);
      doc.setFontSize(50);
      doc.setTextColor(220, 200, 180);
      doc.text('SCEAU FREE', 40, 150, { angle: 45 });
    }
  }

  doc.save(`Portfolio-${author}.pdf`);
}

export function generatePactCertificate(
  title: string,
  description: string,
  creator: string,
  signataires: string[],
  hash: string,
  qrCodeDataUrl: string,
  pactId: string
) {
  const doc = new jsPDF({ orientation: 'portrait', unit: 'mm', format: 'a4' });

  // Fond crème
  doc.setFillColor(253, 248, 240);
  doc.rect(0, 0, 210, 297, 'F');

  // Cadre doré
  doc.setDrawColor(233, 196, 106);
  doc.setLineWidth(2);
  doc.rect(10, 10, 190, 277);
  doc.setLineWidth(0.5);
  doc.rect(14, 14, 182, 269);

  // Titre
  doc.setFont('helvetica', 'bold');
  doc.setFontSize(28);
  doc.setTextColor(44, 24, 16);
  doc.text("CERTIFICAT DE PACTE", 105, 40, { align: 'center' });

  doc.setFontSize(12);
  doc.setTextColor(168, 134, 32); // Or foncé
  doc.text("SCEAU · La poignée de main numérique", 105, 50, { align: 'center' });

  // Ligne de séparation
  doc.setDrawColor(233, 196, 106);
  doc.line(60, 55, 150, 55);

  // Titre du pacte
  doc.setFontSize(20);
  doc.setTextColor(44, 24, 16);
  doc.text(title, 105, 75, { align: 'center' });

  // Description
  doc.setFont('helvetica', 'italic');
  doc.setFontSize(11);
  doc.setTextColor(90, 75, 68);
  const splitDesc = doc.splitTextToSize(`"${description}"`, 150);
  doc.text(splitDesc, 105, 90, { align: 'center' });

  // Créateur
  doc.setFont('helvetica', 'bold');
  doc.setFontSize(10);
  doc.setTextColor(122, 90, 58);
  doc.text(`Proposé par : @${creator}`, 105, 115, { align: 'center' });

  // Signatures
  doc.setFontSize(14);
  doc.setTextColor(44, 24, 16);
  doc.text("Signataires", 105, 135, { align: 'center' });

  doc.setFontSize(12);
  doc.setTextColor(74, 144, 194);
  const sigsText = signataires.map(s => `@${s}`).join('  ·  ');
  doc.text(sigsText, 105, 145, { align: 'center' });

  // Hash
  doc.setFont('courier', 'normal');
  doc.setFontSize(8);
  doc.setTextColor(150, 150, 150);
  const hashChunks = hash.match(/.{1,40}/g) || [hash];
  hashChunks.forEach((chunk, i) => {
    doc.text(chunk, 105, 170 + (i * 5), { align: 'center' });
  });

  // QR Code
  if (qrCodeDataUrl) {
    doc.addImage(qrCodeDataUrl, 'PNG', 80, 195, 50, 50);
    doc.setFontSize(9);
    doc.setTextColor(122, 90, 58);
    doc.text("Scannez pour vérifier l'authenticité", 105, 255, { align: 'center' });
  }

  // Mention Blockchain
  doc.setFont('helvetica', 'bold');
  doc.setFontSize(9);
  doc.setTextColor(93, 122, 58);
  doc.text("Preuve ancrée via OpenTimestamps (Bitcoin)", 105, 265, { align: 'center' });

  doc.save(`Pacte-${title.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9]/g, '-')}.pdf`);
}

FICHIER : ./src/lib/r2.ts

import { S3Client } from '@aws-sdk/client-s3';
import { requireS3 } from '@/lib/env';

let _client: S3Client | null = null;

function create(): S3Client {
  const { endpoint, region } = requireS3();
  const accessKeyId = (process.env.AWS_ACCESS_KEY_ID || '').trim();
  const secretAccessKey = (process.env.AWS_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY || '').trim();
  if (!accessKeyId || !secretAccessKey) {
    throw new Error('Clés S3 manquantes : AWS_ACCESS_KEY_ID / AWS_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY');
  }
  return new S3Client({
    region,
    endpoint,
    credentials: { accessKeyId, secretAccessKey },
    forcePathStyle: true,
    requestChecksumCalculation: 'WHEN_REQUIRED',
    responseChecksumValidation: 'WHEN_REQUIRED',
  });
}

export function getR2Client(): S3Client {
  if (!_client) _client = create();
  return _client;
}

export const r2Client = new Proxy({} as S3Client, {
  get(_t, prop) {
    const real = getR2Client() as any;
    const v = real[prop];
    return typeof v === 'function' ? v.bind(real) : v;
  },
});

FICHIER : ./src/lib/safe-key.ts
=========================================

export function safeKeyPart(input: string, maxLen = 80): string {
  if (!input) return 'file';
  const ascii = input.normalize('NFD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, '');
  const cleaned = ascii.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9._-]+/g, '-');
  const safe = cleaned.replace(/^\.+/, '').replace(/\.{2,}/g, '.').replace(/-+/g, '-');
  return (safe || 'file').slice(0, maxLen);
}

export function buildS3Key(opts: {
  authorId: string; hashBase64: string; originalName: string; prefix?: string;
}): string {
  const prefix = opts.prefix || 'seal';
  const authorShort = opts.authorId.replace(/-/g, '').slice(0, 8);
  const hashShort = opts.hashBase64.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9]/g, '').slice(0, 16);
  const safeName = safeKeyPart(opts.originalName, 80);
  return `${prefix}/${authorShort}/${hashShort}-${safeName}`;
}

export function extractS3Key(filePath: string): string | null {
  const endpoint = (process.env.AWS_ENDPOINT_URL_S3 || '').replace(/\/$/, '');
  const bucket = (process.env.S3_BUCKET || '').replace(/^\/+|\/+$/g, '');
  const prefix = `${endpoint}/${bucket}/`;
  if (filePath.startsWith(prefix)) return filePath.slice(prefix.length);
  return filePath.split('/').pop() || null;
}
